```
   127→        "Nosy debug logging: entry/exit logs on every function, full object dumps to console",

   130→        "Pass-through wrapper functions with no added logic (just forward args to another function)",
   131→        "Generic names in domain code: handleData, processItem, doOperation where domain terms exist",
   132→        "Identical boilerplate error handling copied verbatim across multiple files"
   133→      ],
   134→      "skip": [
   135→        "Comments explaining WHY (business rules, non-obvious constraints, external dependencies)",
   136→        "Defensive checks at genuine API boundaries (user input, network, file I/O)",
   137→        "Generated code (protobuf, GraphQL codegen, ORM migrations)",
   138→        "Wrapper functions that add auth, logging, metrics, or caching"
   139→      ]
   140→    },
```

- `review_batch_max_files: 80` — batch reviews in groups of 80 files
- `holistic_max_age_days: 30` — holistic reviews expire after 30 days
- `generate_scorecard: true` — generates `scorecard.png` badge

- `needs_rescan: false` — no rescan required now

### **The Gate System: Understanding "auto/initial-review"**

The lifecycle/gate system uses **cluster assignments** as gates. The 20 **subjective review** gate is a critical block:

#### **How it Works:**

1. **Initial Assignment:**
   - All 20 subjective dimensions start with `cluster: "auto/initial-review"`
   - These gates **block the entire work queue** until cleared

2. **Queue Structure:**
   - `queue_order[]` contains 261 items
   - First 20 are subjective reviews (ALL must clear)
   - Remaining 241 structural/code issues follow

3. **Status in Current State:**
   ```json
   20 items with cluster: "auto/initial-review"
   [
     "subjective::abstraction_fitness",
     "subjective::ai_generated_debt",
     "subjective::api_surface_coherence",
     ... through ...
     "subjective::type_safety"
   ]
   ```

#### **Why Rescan Reinserts Them:**

When a rescan happens (`desloppify scan`):

1. **Scan finds new findings** → state-typescript.json is updated
2. **Findings are scored** → may change severity/open count
3. **plan.json review dimensions** are checked against current state
4. **If any subjective dimension has new findings**, the gate is **re-inserted** with `cluster: "auto/initial-review"`
5. **All 261 queue items are recalculated** → reordered based on new state

This is why you see the gates "blocking the queue" on rescan — they're gates over **subjective judgment calls**, not automatic checks.

---

### **Review Packet Lifecycle**

Each packet captures one **holistic review run**:

**Packet contents:**
- `command: "review"`
- `mode: "holistic"`
- `language: "typescript"`
- `dimensions: [20 subjective dimensions]`
- `dimension_prompts: {detailed guidance per dimension}`
- `investigation_batches: [batches to review]`
- `narrative: [narrative text from review run]`
- `next_command: [command to run after]`

**Batch execution:**
- Batches 1-20 (one per file pattern or concern area)
- Subagents process them in parallel or serial (configurable)
- Results → `subagents/runs/{timestamp}/results/batch-{N}.raw.txt`
- Issues extracted → merged into state-typescript.json

---

### **Subagent Run Tracking**

Each run captures:

```json
{
  "created_at": "2026-03-05T03:55:20+00:00",
  "run_stamp": "20260305_035511",
```

### **Summary: The Lifecycle/Gate System**

The desloppify system is a **multi-phase code quality gate** with these key mechanics:

1. **Phase 1: Scan** → detects issues, updates state-typescript.json
2. **Phase 2: Review** → holistic LLM review of issues in batches
3. **Phase 3: Triage** → expert judgment on which issues to fix vs. wontfix
4. **Phase 4: Plan** → generates actionable work queue in plan.json
5. **Phase 5: Execute** → user fixes items in queue order

**The auto/initial-review gates** are the **20 subjective review dimensions** that must be explicitly cleared before the structural/code-level fixes are addressed. On rescan, if any dimension gains new findings, the gate reappears to force re-review before proceeding.

This prevents "gate collapse" — where structural fixes accumulate and hide new design debt under old patterns.

2. Find the actual source code - especially the `scan` command, `next` command, and `review` command
3. Focus on understanding:
   - How does `scan` trigger the "auto/initial-review" gate reinsertion?
   - What's the difference between a "start loop" scan and an "end loop" scan?
   - Is there a concept of loop phases where scanning mid-loop should NOT trigger subjective review gates?
   - How does `next` decide what to show - does it check for gates?

Search broadly: try `node_modules/.desloppify`, `node_modules/desloppify`, also grep for "initial-review" and "auto/initial" across the codebase and node_modules.

```
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass

     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import QueueContext

    17→    enrich_plan_metadata,
    18→    filter_cluster_focus,

    41→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    42→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    43→
    44→# Sentinel: "read scan_path from state" (the safe default).
    45→# Callers that want to override can pass an explicit str or None.
    46→_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE = object()
    47→
    48→
    49→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    50→class QueueBuildOptions:
    51→    """Configuration for queue construction.
    52→
    53→    ``scan_path`` defaults to reading from ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers
    54→    don't need to thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` or ``None``
    55→    to override (``None`` disables scope filtering).
    56→    """
    57→
    58→    # Output control
    59→    count: int | None = 1
    60→    explain: bool = False
    61→
    62→    # Scope filtering
    63→    scan_path: str | None | object = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
    64→    scope: str | None = None
    65→    status: str = "open"
    66→    chronic: bool = False
    67→
    68→    # Subjective gating
    69→    include_subjective: bool = True
    70→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    71→
    72→    # Plan integration
    73→    plan: dict | None = None
    74→    include_skipped: bool = False
    75→    cluster: str | None = None
    76→
    77→    # Pre-computed context (overrides plan)
    78→    context: QueueContext | None = None
    79→
    80→
    81→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    82→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    83→
    84→    items: list[WorkQueueItem]
    85→    total: int
    86→    grouped: dict[str, list[WorkQueueItem]]
    87→    new_ids: set[str]
    88→
    89→
    90→def build_work_queue(
    91→    state: StateModel,
    92→    *,
    93→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    94→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    95→    """Build a ranked work queue from state issues.
    96→
    97→    Pipeline:
    98→    1. Gather    — issue items, subjective dimensions, workflow stages
    99→    2. Score     — estimate impact from dimension headroom
   100→    3. Presort   — stamp plan positions, separate skipped items
   101→    4. Lifecycle — filter endgame-only items when objective work remains
   102→    5. Sort      — rank by impact/confidence, apply plan order
   103→    6. Limit     — truncate to count, optionally add explain metadata
   104→    """
   105→    opts = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   106→    plan, scan_path, status, threshold = _resolve_inputs(opts, state)
   107→
   108→    # 1. Gather
   109→    items = build_issue_items(
   110→        state, scan_path=scan_path, status_filter=status,
   111→        scope=opts.scope, chronic=opts.chronic,
   112→    )
   113→    items += _gather_subjective_items(state, opts, threshold)
   114→    items += _gather_workflow_items(state, plan, status)
   115→
   116→    # 2. Score
   117→    enrich_with_impact(items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   118→
   119→    # 3. Plan-aware ordering (part 1: separate skipped items)
   120→    new_ids, skipped = _plan_presort(items, state, plan)
   121→
   122→    # 4. Lifecycle filter — endgame-only items filtered when objective work remains
   123→    items = _apply_lifecycle_filter(items)
   124→
   125→    # 5. Sort & plan post-processing
   126→    items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   127→    _plan_postsort(items, skipped, plan, opts)
   128→
   129→    # 6. Finalize
   130→    if not items:
   131→        items += _empty_queue_fallback(plan)
   132→    total = len(items)
   133→    if opts.count is not None and opts.count > 0:
   134→        items = items[:opts.count]
   135→    if opts.explain:
   136→        for item in items:
   137→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   138→
   139→    return {
   140→        "items": items,
   141→        "total": total,
   142→        "grouped": group_queue_items(items, "item"),
   143→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   144→    }
   145→
   146→
   147→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   148→# Pipeline helpers (private to this module)
   149→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   150→
   151→
   152→def _resolve_inputs(
   153→    opts: QueueBuildOptions, state: StateModel,
   154→) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None, str, float]:
   155→    """Resolve plan, scan_path, status, and subjective threshold from options."""
   156→    ctx = opts.context
   157→    plan = ctx.plan if ctx is not None else opts.plan
   158→
   159→    scan_path: str | None = (
   160→        state.get("scan_path")
   161→        if opts.scan_path is _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
   162→        else opts.scan_path  # type: ignore[assignment]
   163→    )
   164→
   165→    status = opts.status
   166→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   167→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   168→
   169→    try:
   170→        threshold = float(opts.subjective_threshold)
   171→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   172→        threshold = 100.0
   173→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, threshold))
   174→
   175→    return plan, scan_path, status, threshold
   176→
   177→
   178→def _gather_subjective_items(
   179→    state: StateModel,
   180→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
   181→    threshold: float,
   182→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   183→    """Build subjective dimension candidates.
   184→
   185→    Lifecycle filtering (endgame gating) happens in _apply_lifecycle_filter,
   186→    not here. This function only handles configuration and scope.
   187→    """
   188→    if not opts.include_subjective:
   189→        return []
   190→    if opts.status not in {"open", "all"}:
   191→        return []
   192→    if opts.chronic:
   193→        return []
   194→
   195→    candidates = build_subjective_items(
   196→        state, state.get("issues", {}), threshold=threshold,
   197→    )
   198→    return [item for item in candidates if scope_matches(item, opts.scope)]
   199→
   200→
   201→def _gather_workflow_items(
   202→    state: StateModel, plan: dict | None, status: str,
   203→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   204→    """Inject triage stages, checkpoints, and create-plan when plan is active."""
   205→    if not plan or status not in {"open", "all"}:
   206→        return []
   207→
   208→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = list(build_triage_stage_items(plan, state))
   209→    for builder in (
   210→        build_score_checkpoint_item,
   211→        build_import_scores_item,
   212→        build_communicate_score_item,
   213→    ):
   214→        item = builder(plan, state)
   215→        if item is not None:
   216→            items.append(item)
   217→    plan_item = build_create_plan_item(plan)
   218→    if plan_item is not None:
   219→        items.append(plan_item)
   220→    return items
   221→
   222→
   223→
   224→def _plan_presort(
   225→    items: list[WorkQueueItem], state: StateModel, plan: dict | None,
   226→) -> tuple[set[str], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   227→    """Enrich plan metadata and stamp sort keys before sorting.
   228→
   229→    Returns ``(new_ids, skipped)`` — skipped items are removed from
   230→    ``items`` in place and returned separately for post-sort re-append.
   231→    """
   232→    if not plan:
   233→        return set(), []
   234→
   235→    new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   236→    enrich_plan_metadata(items, plan)
   237→    stamp_plan_sort_keys(items, plan, new_ids)
   238→    remaining, skipped = separate_skipped(items, plan)
   239→    items[:] = remaining
   240→    return new_ids, skipped
   241→
   242→
   243→def _plan_postsort(
   244→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   245→    skipped: list[WorkQueueItem],
   246→    plan: dict | None,
   247→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
   248→) -> None:
   249→    """Re-append skipped items, stamp positions, filter to cluster focus."""
   250→    if not plan:
   251→        return
   252→
   253→    if opts.include_skipped:
   254→        items.extend(skipped)
   255→    stamp_positions(items, plan)
   256→    focused = filter_cluster_focus(items, plan, opts.cluster)
   257→    items[:] = focused
   258→
   259→
   260→def _has_objective_items(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
   261→    """True if any objective mechanical work items remain in the queue."""
   262→    return any(
   263→        i.get("kind") == "issue"
   264→        and i.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   265→        for i in items
   266→    )
   267→
   268→
   269→def _has_initial_reviews(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
   270→    """True if any unassessed subjective dimensions need initial review."""
   271→    return any(
   272→        i.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
   273→        and i.get("initial_review")
   274→        for i in items
   275→    )
   276→
   277→
   278→def _is_endgame_only(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
   279→    """True if this item should only appear when the objective queue is drained."""
   280→    return (
   281→        item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
   282→        and not item.get("initial_review")
   283→    )
   284→
   285→
   286→def _apply_lifecycle_filter(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   287→    """Enforce lifecycle visibility rules.
   288→
   289→    The queue enforces a phase order: scan → initial review → objective work.
   290→
   291→    1. **Initial reviews pending** → only show initial-review subjective items.
   292→       The user must complete first-time reviews before working objective items.
   293→    2. **Objective work remains** → show objective items, hide endgame-only
   294→       subjective reassessments (stale re-reviews).
   295→    3. **Objective drained** → everything visible (endgame).
   296→    """
   297→    if _has_initial_reviews(items):
   298→        # Phase 1: only initial reviews visible — triage and workflow
   299→        # items stay hidden until initial reviews are complete.
   300→        return [
   301→            i for i in items
   302→            if i.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" and i.get("initial_review")
   303→        ]
   304→    if not _has_objective_items(items):
   305→        return items  # endgame: everything visible
   306→    return [i for i in items if not _is_endgame_only(i)]
   307→
   308→
   309→def _empty_queue_fallback(plan: dict | None) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   310→    """Return a 'run scan' nudge when an active plan cycle has cleared."""
   311→    if not plan:
   312→        return []
   313→    plan_scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   314→    if plan_scores.get("strict") is None:
   315→        return []
   316→    return [{
   317→        "id": "workflow::run-scan",
   318→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   319→        "summary": "Queue cleared \u2014 run scan to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   320→        "primary_command": "desloppify scan",
   321→        "file": "",
   322→        "detector": "workflow",
   323→        "confidence": "high",
   324→    }]
   325→
   326→
   327→__all__ = [
   328→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   329→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   330→    "QueueContext",
   331→    "WorkQueueResult",
   332→    "build_work_queue",
   333→    "collapse_clusters",
   334→    "group_queue_items",
   335→]
   336→
```

```
1→"""Synthetic work-queue item builders and dimension scoring.
     2→
     3→Builds workflow stage items, score checkpoint items, create-plan items,
     4→subjective dimension items, and subjective score lookups.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from typing import Any
    10→
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    12→from desloppify.engine._scoring.subjective.core import DISPLAY_NAMES
    13→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
    14→    detail_dict,
    15→    slugify,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic_workflow import (
    18→    build_communicate_score_item,
    19→    build_create_plan_item,
    20→    build_import_scores_item,
    21→    build_score_checkpoint_item,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    24→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    25→    all_subjective_entries,
    26→)
    27→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    28→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    29→)
    30→
    31→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32→# Dimension key normalization
    33→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34→
    35→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
    36→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
    37→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    38→    target = cleaned.lower()
    39→
    40→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    41→        if str(label).lower() == target:
    42→            return str(dim_key)
    43→    return slugify(cleaned)
    44→
    45→
    46→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
    47→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with issue dimension keys."""
    48→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    49→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
    50→    return {
    51→        cleaned.lower(),
    52→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
    53→        slugify(cleaned),
    54→        canonical.lower(),
    55→        slugify(canonical),
    56→    }
    57→
    58→
    59→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    60→# Subjective strict scores
    61→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    62→
    63→def subjective_strict_scores(state: StateModel | dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, float]:
    64→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    65→    if not dim_scores:
    66→        return {}
    67→
    68→    entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    69→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
    70→    for entry in entries:
    71→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    72→        if not name:
    73→            continue
    74→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    75→        [REDACTED](name)
    76→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
    77→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
    78→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
    79→            if not key:
    80→                continue
    81→            aliases.add(key)
    82→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
    83→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
    84→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
    85→        for alias in aliases:
    86→            scores[alias] = strict_val
    87→    return scores
    88→
    89→
    90→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    91→# Synthetic item builders
    92→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    93→
    94→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    95→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
    96→
    97→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
    98→    """
    99→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage_playbook import (
   100→        TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
   101→        TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS,
   102→    )
   103→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   104→        TRIAGE_IDS,
   105→        TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
   106→    )
   107→
   108→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   109→    order_set = set(order)
   110→    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   111→    if not present:
   112→        return []
   113→
   114→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   115→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   116→
   117→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   118→    open_review_count = sum(
   119→        1 for f in issues.values()
   120→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   121→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   122→    )
   123→
   124→    label_map = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   125→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   126→
   127→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   128→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   129→        if sid not in present:
   130→            continue
   131→        if name in confirmed:
   132→            continue
   133→
   134→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   135→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   136→        blocked_by = sorted(
   137→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps
   138→            if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   139→        )
   140→
   141→        cmd = f"desloppify plan triage --stage {name}"
   142→        if name == "commit":
   143→            cmd = 'desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "..."'
   144→
   145→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   146→            "id": sid,
   147→            "tier": 1,
   148→            "confidence": "high",
   149→            "detector": "triage",
   150→            "file": ".",
   151→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   152→            "summary": f"Triage: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   153→            "detail": {
   154→                "total_review_issues": open_review_count,
   155→                "stage": name,
   156→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   157→            },
   158→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   159→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   160→        }
   161→        item["primary_command"] = cmd
   162→        items.append(item)
   163→    return items
   164→
   165→
   166→def build_subjective_items(
   167→    state: dict, issues: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   168→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   169→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   170→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   171→    if not dim_scores:
   172→        return []
   173→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   174→
   175→    subjective_entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   176→    if not subjective_entries:
   177→        return []
   178→    unassessed_dims = {
   179→        str(name).strip()
   180→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   181→            dim_scores
   182→        )
   183→    }
   184→
   185→    # Review issues are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   186→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   187→    for issue in issues.values():
   188→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
   189→            continue
   190→        if issue.get("detector") == "review":
   191→            dim_key = str(detail_dict(issue).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   192→            if dim_key:
   193→                review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   194→
   195→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   196→    def _prepare_command(
   197→        cli_keys: list[str],
   198→        *,
   199→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   200→    ) -> str:
   201→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   202→        if cli_keys:
   203→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   204→        if force_review_rerun:
   205→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   206→        return command
   207→
   208→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   209→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   210→        if not name:
   211→            continue
   212→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   213→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   214→            continue
   215→
   216→        [REDACTED](name)
   217→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   218→        cli_keys = [
   219→            str(key).strip().lower()
   220→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   221→            if str(key).strip()
   222→        ]
   223→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   224→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   225→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   226→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   227→            name in unassessed_dims
   228→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("failing", 0)) == 0)
   229→        )
   230→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   231→        # If review issues already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   232→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   233→        if open_review > 0:
   234→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   235→        else:
   236→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   237→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   238→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   239→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   240→            "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   241→            "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   242→            "file": ".",
   243→            "confidence": "medium",
   244→            "summary": summary,
   245→            "detail": {
   246→                "dimension_name": name,
   247→                "dimension": dim_key,
   248→                "failing": int(entry.get("failing", 0)),
   249→                "strict_score": strict_val,
   250→                "open_review_issues": open_review,
   251→                "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   252→            },
   253→            "status": "open",
   254→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   255→        }
   256→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command
   257→        item["initial_review"] = is_unassessed
   258→        items.append(item)
   259→    return items
   260→
   261→
   262→__all__ = [
   263→    "build_communicate_score_item",
   264→    "build_create_plan_item",
   265→    "build_import_scores_item",
   266→    "build_score_checkpoint_item",
   267→    "build_subjective_items",
   268→    "build_triage_stage_items",
   269→    "subjective_strict_scores",
   270→]
   271→
```

```
    53→def _print_scan_header(lang_label: str) -> None:
    54→    """Print the scan header line."""
    55→    print(colorize(f"\nDesloppify Scan{lang_label}\n", "bold"))
    56→
    57→
    58→def _print_scan_complete_banner() -> None:
    59→    """Print scan completion hint banner."""
    60→    lines = [
    61→        colorize("  Scan complete", "bold"),
    62→        colorize("  " + "─" * 50, "dim"),
    63→    ]
    64→    print("\n".join(lines))
    65→
    66→
    67→def _show_scan_visibility(noise, effective_include_slow: bool) -> None:
    68→    """Print fast-scan and noise budget visibility hints.
    69→
    70→    Side-effect only: conditionally prints scan-mode warnings to stdout.
    71→    All branches may be skipped (full scan, no noise budget hit, no hidden
    72→    issues), so the function can legitimately produce no output.
    73→    """
    74→    if not effective_include_slow:
    75→        print(colorize("  * Fast scan — slow phases (duplicates) skipped", "yellow"))
    76→    if noise.budget_warning:
    77→        print(colorize(f"  * {noise.budget_warning}", "yellow"))
    78→    if noise.hidden_total:
    79→        print(
    80→            colorize(
    81→                f"  * {noise.hidden_total} issues hidden (showing {noise.noise_budget}/detector). "
    82→                "Use `desloppify show <detector>` to see all.",
    83→                "dim",
    84→            )
    85→        )
    86→
    87→
    88→def _show_coverage_preflight(runtime) -> None:
    89→    """Print preflight warnings when scan coverage confidence is reduced."""
    90→    warnings = getattr(runtime, "coverage_warnings", []) or []
    91→    if not isinstance(warnings, list) or not warnings:
    92→        return
    93→
    94→    for entry in warnings:
    95→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    96→            continue
    97→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
    98→        impact = str(entry.get("impact", "")).strip()
    99→        remediation = str(entry.get("remediation", "")).strip()
   100→        detector = str(entry.get("detector", "")).strip() or "detector"
   101→
   102→        headline = summary or f"Coverage reduced for `{detector}`."
   103→        print(colorize(f"  * Coverage preflight: {headline}", "yellow"))
   104→        if impact:
   105→            print(colorize(f"    Repercussion: {impact}", "dim"))
   106→        if remediation:
   107→            print(colorize(f"    Fix: {remediation}", "dim"))
   108→
   109→
   110→def _print_plan_workflow_nudge(state: dict) -> None:
   111→    _print_plan_workflow_nudge_impl(state)
   112→
   113→
   114→def cmd_scan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   115→    """Run all detectors, update persistent state, show diff."""
   116→    scan_preflight_mod.scan_queue_preflight(args)
   117→    try:
   118→        runtime = prepare_scan_runtime(args)
   119→    except LangRuntimeOptionsError as exc:
   120→        lang_cfg = resolve_lang(args)
   121→        lang_name = lang_cfg.name if lang_cfg else "selected"
   122→        print_lang_runtime_options_error(exc, lang_name=lang_name)
   123→        raise CommandError(str(exc), exit_code=2) from exc
   124→    except ScanStateContractError as exc:
   125→        raise CommandError(str(exc), exit_code=2) from exc
   126→    orchestrator = ScanOrchestrator(
   127→        runtime,
   128→        run_scan_generation_fn=run_scan_generation,
   129→        merge_scan_results_fn=merge_scan_results,
   130→        resolve_noise_snapshot_fn=resolve_noise_snapshot,
   131→        persist_reminder_history_fn=persist_reminder_history,
   132→    )
   133→    _print_scan_header(runtime.lang_label)
   134→    if runtime.reset_subjective_count > 0:
   135→        print(
   136→            colorize(
   137→                "  * Subjective reset "
   138→                f"{runtime.reset_subjective_count} subjective dimensions to 0",
   139→                "yellow",
   140→            )
   141→        )
   142→    if runtime.expired_manual_override_count > 0:
   143→        print(
   144→            colorize(
   145→                "  * Expired provisional manual-override assessments: "
   146→                f"{runtime.expired_manual_override_count} dimension(s) reset to 0. "
   147→                "Run a trusted review to replace them (see skill doc for options).",
   148→                "yellow",
   149→            )
   150→        )
   151→    _show_coverage_preflight(runtime)
   152→
   153→    issues, potentials, codebase_metrics = orchestrator.generate()
   154→    merge = orchestrator.merge(issues, potentials, codebase_metrics)
   155→    _print_scan_complete_banner()
   156→
   157→    noise = orchestrator.noise_snapshot()
   158→
   159→    target_value = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   160→
   161→    show_diff_summary(merge.diff)
   162→    show_score_delta(
   163→        runtime.state,
   164→        merge.prev_overall,
   165→        merge.prev_objective,
   166→        merge.prev_strict,
   167→        merge.prev_verified,
   168→        target_strict=target_value,
   169→    )
   170→    # Nudge: if plan_start_scores was just seeded, tell the agent about the lifecycle.
   171→    _print_plan_workflow_nudge(runtime.state)
   172→    _show_scan_visibility(noise, runtime.effective_include_slow)
   173→    show_scorecard_subjective_measures(runtime.state)
   174→    show_score_model_breakdown(runtime.state)
   175→
   176→    new_dim_scores = runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   177→    if new_dim_scores and merge.prev_dim_scores:
   178→        show_dimension_deltas(merge.prev_dim_scores, new_dim_scores)
   179→
   180→    warnings, narrative = show_post_scan_analysis(
   181→        merge.diff,
   182→        runtime.state,
   183→        runtime.lang,
   184→        target_strict_score=target_value,
   185→    )
   186→    orchestrator.persist_reminders(narrative)
   187→
   188→    write_query(
   189→        build_scan_query_payload(
   190→            runtime.state,
   191→            runtime.config,
   192→            runtime.profile,
   193→            merge.diff,
   194→            warnings,
   195→            narrative,
   196→            merge,
   197→            noise,
   198→        ),
   199→        query_file=query_file_path(),
   200→    )
   201→
   202→    badge_path, _badge_result = emit_scorecard_badge(args, runtime.config, runtime.state)
   203→    print_llm_summary(runtime.state, badge_path, narrative, merge.diff)
   204→    auto_update_skill()
   205→
   206→
   207→__all__ = [
   208→    "cmd_scan",
   209→]
```

```
    65→class ScanStateContractError(ValueError):
    66→    """Raised when persisted scan state violates required runtime contracts."""
    67→
    68→
    69→def _clear_needs_rescan_flag(config: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    70→    """Best-effort clear for config.needs_rescan after a successful scan merge."""
    71→    if not config.get("needs_rescan"):
    72→        return
    73→    try:
    74→        config["needs_rescan"] = False
    75→        _save_config(config)
    76→    except OSError as exc:
    77→        _ = exc
    78→        config["needs_rescan"] = True
    79→
    80→
    81→def _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: ScanRuntime) -> None:
    82→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
    83→    _reconcile_plan_post_scan_impl(runtime)
    84→
    85→
    86→def _state_subjective_assessments(
    87→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
    88→) -> dict[str, object]:
    89→    """Return normalized subjective assessment store from state."""
    90→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    91→    if isinstance(assessments, dict):
    92→        return assessments
    93→    raise ScanStateContractError(
    94→        "state.subjective_assessments must be an object; rerun with a valid state file"
    95→    )
    96→
    97→
    98→def _state_lang_capabilities(
    99→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   100→) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   101→    """Return normalized language capability map from state."""
   102→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
   103→    if capabilities is None:
   104→        normalized: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
   105→        state["lang_capabilities"] = normalized
   106→        return normalized
   107→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict):
   108→        return capabilities
   109→    raise ScanStateContractError(
   110→        "state.lang_capabilities must be an object when present"
   111→    )
   112→
   113→
   114→def _state_issues(state: state_mod.StateModel) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   115→    """Return normalized issue map from state."""
   116→    issues = state.get("issues")
   117→    if isinstance(issues, dict):
   118→        return issues
   119→    raise ScanStateContractError(
   120→        "state.issues must be an object; state file appears corrupted"
   121→    )

   129→@dataclass
   130→class ScanRuntime:
   131→    """Resolved runtime context for a single scan invocation."""
   132→
   133→    args: argparse.Namespace
   134→    state_path: Path | None
   135→    state: state_mod.StateModel
   136→    path: Path
   137→    config: dict[str, object]
   138→    lang: LangRun | None
   139→    lang_label: str
   140→    profile: str
   141→    effective_include_slow: bool
   142→    zone_overrides: dict[str, object] | None
   143→    reset_subjective_count: int = 0
   144→    expired_manual_override_count: int = 0
   145→    coverage_warnings: list[DetectorCoverageRecord] = field(default_factory=list)
   146→

   149→class ScanMergeResult:
   150→    """State merge outputs and previous score snapshots."""
   151→
   152→    diff: dict[str, object]
   153→    prev_overall: float | None
   154→    prev_objective: float | None
   155→    prev_strict: float | None

   157→    prev_dim_scores: dict[str, object]

   161→class ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   162→    """Noise budget settings and hidden issue counts for this scan."""
   163→
   164→    noise_budget: int
   165→    global_noise_budget: int
   166→    budget_warning: str | None
   167→    hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int]
   168→    hidden_total: int
   169→
   170→
   171→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   172→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   173→    config: dict[str, object],
   174→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   175→    lang: LangRun | None,
   176→) -> LangRun | None:
   177→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   178→    if not lang:
   179→        return None
   180→
   181→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   182→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   183→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   184→        lang,
   185→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   186→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   187→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   188→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
   189→            runtime_options=lang_options,
   190→            large_threshold_override=config.get("large_files_threshold", 0),
   191→            props_threshold_override=config.get("props_threshold", 0),
   192→        ),
   193→    )
   194→
   195→    lang_capabilities = _state_lang_capabilities(state)
   196→    lang_capabilities[runtime_lang.name] = {
   197→        "fixers": sorted(runtime_lang.fixers.keys()),
   198→        "typecheck_cmd": runtime_lang.typecheck_cmd,
   199→    }
   200→    return runtime_lang
   201→
   202→
   203→def _apply_assessment_reset(payload: dict, *, source: str, now: str) -> None:
   204→    """Apply a standard reset mutation to a single subjective assessment payload.
   205→
   206→    Sets score to 0.0, stamps assessed_at/reset_by/placeholder, and strips
   207→    any cached scoring artifacts (integrity_penalty, components,
   208→    component_scores) plus provisional-override markers.
   209→    """
   210→    payload["score"] = 0.0
   211→    payload["source"] = source
   212→    payload["assessed_at"] = now
   213→    payload["reset_by"] = source
   214→    payload["placeholder"] = True
   215→    payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   216→    payload.pop("components", None)
   217→    payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   218→    payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   219→    payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   220→
   221→
   222→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   223→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   224→    *,
   225→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   226→) -> int:
   227→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   228→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   229→
   230→    reset_keys = {
   231→        key.strip()
   232→        for key in assessments
   233→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   234→    }
   235→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   236→
   237→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   238→    source = "scan_reset_subjective"
   239→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   240→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   241→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   242→            _apply_assessment_reset(payload, source=source, now=now)
   243→            continue
   244→        assessments[key] = {
   245→            "score": 0.0,
   246→            "source": source,
   247→            "assessed_at": now,
   248→            "reset_by": source,
   249→            "placeholder": True,
   250→        }
   251→    return len(reset_keys)
   252→
   253→
   254→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   255→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   256→) -> int:
   257→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   258→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   259→
   260→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   261→    source = "manual_override_expired"
   262→    expired = 0
   263→    for payload in assessments.values():
   264→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   265→            continue
   266→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   267→            continue
   268→        _apply_assessment_reset(payload, source=source, now=now)
   269→        expired += 1
   270→    return expired
   271→
   272→
   273→def prepare_scan_runtime(args: argparse.Namespace) -> ScanRuntime:
   274→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   275→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   276→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   277→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   278→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
   279→    path = Path(args.path)
   280→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   281→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   282→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   283→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   284→        state
   285→    )
   286→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   287→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   288→            state,
   289→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   290→        )
   291→
   292→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   293→    profile = resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   294→    include_slow_effective = effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   295→
   296→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   297→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   298→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   299→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   300→
   301→    return ScanRuntime(
   302→        args=args,
   303→        state_path=state_file,
   304→        state=state,
   305→        path=path,
   306→        config=config,
   307→        lang=lang,
   308→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   309→        profile=profile,
   310→        effective_include_slow=include_slow_effective,
   311→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   312→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   313→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   314→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   315→    )
   316→
   317→
   318→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_issues(
   319→    issues: list[dict[str, Any]],
   320→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   321→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   322→    """Append stale exclude issues when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   323→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   324→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   325→        return issues
   326→
   327→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   328→    stale = audit_excluded_dirs(
   329→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, get_project_root()
   330→    )
   331→    if not stale:
   332→        return issues
   333→
   334→    augmented = list(issues)
   335→    augmented.extend(stale)
   336→    for stale_issue in stale:
   337→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_issue['summary']}", "dim"))
   338→    return augmented
   339→
   340→
   341→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_issues(
   342→    issues: list[dict[str, Any]],
   343→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   344→    *,
   345→    decay_scans: int,
   346→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   347→    """Append re-triage issues for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   348→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   349→        issues,
   350→        state=runtime.state,
   351→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   352→        project_root=get_project_root(),
   353→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   354→    )
   355→
   356→
   357→def run_scan_generation(
   358→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   359→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   360→    """Run detector pipeline and return issues, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   361→    enable_file_cache()
   362→    enable_parse_cache()
   363→    try:
   364→        issues, potentials = plan_mod.generate_issues(
   365→            runtime.path,
   366→            lang=runtime.lang,
   367→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   368→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   369→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   370→                profile=runtime.profile,
   371→            ),
   372→        )
   373→    finally:
   374→        disable_parse_cache()
   375→        disable_file_cache()
   376→
   377→    codebase_metrics = collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   378→    warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   379→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   380→    )
   381→    issues = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_issues(issues, runtime)
   382→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   383→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   384→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   385→    )
   386→    issues, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_issues(
   387→        issues,
   388→        runtime,
   389→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   390→    )
   391→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   392→    return issues, potentials, codebase_metrics
   393→
   394→
   395→def merge_scan_results(
   396→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   397→    issues: list[dict[str, Any]],
   398→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   399→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   400→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   401→    """Merge issues into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   402→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   403→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   404→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   405→
   406→    if not path_changed:
   407→        prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   408→    else:
   409→        prev = state_mod.ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   410→    prev_dim_scores = (
   411→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   412→    )
   413→
   414→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   415→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   416→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   417→
   418→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   419→
   420→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   421→        runtime.state,
   422→        issues,
   423→        options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
   424→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   425→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   426→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   427→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   428→            potentials=potentials,
   429→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   430→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   431→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   432→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   433→        ),
   434→    )
   435→
   436→    expire_stale_holistic(
   437→        runtime.state, runtime.config.get("holistic_max_age_days", 30)
   438→    )
   439→    state_mod.save_state(
   440→        runtime.state,
   441→        runtime.state_path,
   442→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   443→    )
   444→
   445→    _clear_needs_rescan_flag(runtime.config)
   446→    _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime)
   447→
   448→    return ScanMergeResult(
   449→        diff=diff,
   450→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   451→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   452→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   453→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   454→        prev_dim_scores=prev_dim_scores,
   455→    )
   456→
   457→
   458→def resolve_noise_snapshot(
   459→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   460→    config: dict[str, object],
   461→) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   462→    """Resolve noise budget settings and hidden issue counters."""
   463→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   464→        state_mod.resolve_issue_noise_settings(config)
   465→    )
   466→    issues_by_id = _state_issues(state)
   467→    open_issues = [
   468→        issue
   469→        for issue in state_mod.path_scoped_issues(
   470→            issues_by_id, state.get("scan_path")
   471→        ).values()
   472→        if issue.get("status") == "open"
   473→    ]
   474→    _, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_issue_noise_budget(
   475→        open_issues,
   476→        budget=noise_budget,
   477→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   478→    )
   479→
   480→    return ScanNoiseSnapshot(
   481→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   482→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   483→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   484→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   485→        hidden_total=sum(hidden_by_detector.values()),
   486→    )
   487→
   488→
   489→def persist_reminder_history(
   490→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   491→    narrative: dict[str, object],
   492→) -> None:
   493→    """Persist reminder history emitted by narrative computation."""
   494→    if not (narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative):
   495→        return
   496→
   497→    runtime.state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   498→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   499→    state_mod.save_state(
   500→        runtime.state,
   501→        runtime.state_path,
   502→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   503→    )
   504→
   505→
   506→__all__ = [
   507→    "ScanStateContractError",
   508→    "ScanMergeResult",
   509→    "ScanNoiseSnapshot",
   510→    "ScanRuntime",
   511→    "merge_scan_results",
   512→    "persist_reminder_history",
   513→    "prepare_scan_runtime",
   514→    "resolve_noise_snapshot",
   515→    "run_scan_generation",
   516→]
   517→
```

```
1→"""Lightweight integrity helpers for subjective/review scoring.
     2→
     3→This module intentionally lives outside ``desloppify.intelligence.review`` so command/state
     4→paths can import it without loading the heavier review package.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping
    10→
    11→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
    12→    SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE,
    13→    matches_target_score,
    14→)
    15→
    16→__all__ = [
    17→    "SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE",
    18→    "is_holistic_subjective_issue",
    19→    "is_subjective_review_open",
    20→    "matches_target_score",
    21→    "subjective_review_open_breakdown",
    22→    "unassessed_subjective_dimensions",
    23→]
    24→
    25→
    26→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27→# Internal iteration helper
    28→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    29→
    30→
    31→def _iter_issues(
    32→    issues: Mapping[str, dict] | Iterable[dict],
    33→) -> Iterable[tuple[str, dict]]:
    34→    """Yield (issue_id, issue) pairs from mapping or iterable inputs."""
    35→    if isinstance(issues, Mapping):
    36→        for issue_id, issue in issues.items():
    37→            if isinstance(issue, dict):
    38→                yield str(issue_id), issue
    39→        return
    40→
    41→    for index, issue in enumerate(issues):
    42→        if isinstance(issue, dict):
    43→            yield str(index), issue
    44→
    45→
    46→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47→# Public helpers (formerly in integrity/review.py)
    48→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    49→
    50→
    51→def is_subjective_review_open(issue: dict) -> bool:
    52→    """Return True when a issue is an open subjective-review signal."""
    53→    return (
    54→        issue.get("status") == "open"
    55→        and issue.get("detector") == "subjective_review"
    56→    )
    57→
    58→
    59→def is_holistic_subjective_issue(issue: dict, *, issue_id: str = "") -> bool:
    60→    """Best-effort check for holistic subjective-review coverage issues."""
    61→    candidate_id = str(issue.get("id") or issue_id or "")
    62→    if "::holistic_unreviewed" in candidate_id or "::holistic_stale" in candidate_id:
    63→        return True
    64→
    65→    summary = str(issue.get("summary", "") or "").lower()
    66→    if "holistic" in summary and "review" in summary:
    67→        return True
    68→
    69→    detail = issue.get("detail", {})
    70→    return bool(detail.get("holistic"))
    71→
    72→
    73→def subjective_review_open_breakdown(
    74→    issues: Mapping[str, dict] | Iterable[dict],
    75→) -> tuple[int, dict[str, int], dict[str, int]]:
    76→    """Return open subjective count plus reason and holistic-reason breakdowns."""
    77→    reason_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    78→    holistic_reason_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    79→    total = 0
    80→
    81→    for issue_id, issue in _iter_issues(issues):
    82→        if not is_subjective_review_open(issue):
    83→            continue
    84→
    85→        total += 1
    86→        reason = str(issue.get("detail", {}).get("reason", "other") or "other")
    87→        reason_counts[reason] = reason_counts.get(reason, 0) + 1
    88→
    89→        if is_holistic_subjective_issue(issue, issue_id=issue_id):
    90→            holistic_reason_counts[reason] = holistic_reason_counts.get(reason, 0) + 1
    91→
    92→    return total, reason_counts, holistic_reason_counts
    93→
    94→
    95→def unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores: dict | None) -> list[str]:
    96→    """Return subjective dimension display names that are still 0% placeholders."""
    97→    if not dim_scores:
    98→        return []
    99→
   100→    unassessed: list[str] = []
   101→    for name, info in dim_scores.items():
   102→        detectors = info.get("detectors", {})
   103→        if "subjective_assessment" not in detectors:
   104→            continue
   105→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
   106→        if isinstance(assessment_meta, dict) and assessment_meta.get("placeholder"):
   107→            unassessed.append(name)
   108→            continue
   109→        strict_val = float(info.get("strict", info.get("score", 100.0)))
   110→        issues = int(info.get("failing", 0))
   111→        if strict_val <= 0.0 and issues == 0:
   112→            unassessed.append(name)
   113→
   114→    unassessed.sort()
   115→    return unassessed
   116→
```

```
    77→def _scorecard_subjective(

    79→    dim_scores: dict,
    80→) -> list[dict]:
    81→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    82→    return _scorecard_subjective_impl(state, dim_scores)
    83→
    84→
    85→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    86→    state: dict,
    87→    dim_scores: dict,
    88→    *,
    89→    threshold: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    90→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    91→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    92→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    93→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    94→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    95→            continue
    96→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    97→        if strict_val < threshold:
    98→            low.append(
    99→                (
   100→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
   101→                    strict_val,
   102→                    int(entry.get("failing", 0)),
   103→                )
   104→            )
   105→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
   106→    return low
   107→
   108→
   109→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   110→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
   111→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   112→    state = runtime.state
   113→    config = runtime.config
   114→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   115→        return
   116→
   117→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
   118→    if skill_warning:
   119→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
   120→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
   121→    if config_warning:
   122→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
   123→
   124→    print_triage_guardrail_info(state=state)
   125→    _build_and_render_queue(args, state, config)
   126→
   127→
   128→def _resolve_cluster_focus(
   129→    plan_data: dict | None,
   130→    *,
   131→    cluster_arg: str | None,
   132→    scope: str | None,
   133→) -> str | None:
   134→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
   135→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
   136→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   137→        if active_cluster:
   138→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   139→    return effective_cluster
   140→
   141→
   142→def _build_next_payload(
   143→    *,
   144→    queue: dict,
   145→    items: list[dict],
   146→    state: dict,
   147→    narrative: dict,
   148→    plan_data: dict | None,
   149→) -> dict:
   150→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   151→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   152→    )
   153→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   154→    payload["overall_score"] = scores.overall
   155→    payload["objective_score"] = scores.objective
   156→    payload["strict_score"] = scores.strict
   157→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   158→        state,
   159→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   160→    )
   161→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   162→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   163→    ]
   164→    return payload
   165→
   166→
   167→def _emit_requested_output(
   168→    opts: NextOptions,
   169→    payload: dict,
   170→    items: list[dict],
   171→) -> bool:
   172→    if opts.output_file:
   173→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   174→            opts.output_file,
   175→            payload,
   176→            len(items),
   177→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   178→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   179→        ):
   180→            return True
   181→        raise CommandError("Failed to write output file")
   182→
   183→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(opts.output_format, payload, items):
   184→        return True
   185→    return False
   186→
   187→
   188→def _plan_queue_context(
   189→    *,
   190→    state: dict,
   191→    plan_data: dict | None,
   192→    context=None,
   193→) -> tuple[float | None, QueueBreakdown | None]:
   194→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else plan_data
   195→    plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(effective_plan)
   196→    try:
   197→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan_data, context=context)
   198→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   199→        breakdown = None
   200→    return plan_start_strict, breakdown
   201→
   202→
   203→def _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials: dict | None) -> dict | None:
   204→    try:
   205→        return merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   206→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   207→        return raw_potentials or None
   208→
   209→
   210→def _build_and_render_queue(args: argparse.Namespace, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
   211→    opts = NextOptions.from_args(args)
   212→
   213→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   214→
   215→    # Load the living plan
   216→    plan = load_plan()
   217→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   218→    if (
   219→        plan.get("queue_order")
   220→        or plan.get("overrides")
   221→        or plan.get("clusters")
   222→    ):
   223→        plan_data = plan
   224→
   225→    # Build unified context once — all downstream consumers agree on
   226→    # plan, target_strict, and subjective visibility policy.
   227→    ctx = queue_context(
   228→        state, config=config, plan=plan_data, target_strict=target_strict,
   229→    )
   230→
   231→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   232→    effective_cluster = _resolve_cluster_focus(
   233→        plan_data,
   234→        cluster_arg=opts.cluster,
   235→        scope=opts.scope,
   236→    )
   237→
   238→    queue = build_work_queue(
   239→        state,
   240→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   241→            count=None,
   242→            scope=opts.scope,
   243→            status=opts.status,
   244→            include_subjective=True,
   245→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   246→            explain=opts.explain,
   247→            include_skipped=opts.include_skipped,
   248→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   249→            context=ctx,
   250→        ),
   251→    )
   252→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   253→
   254→    # Collapse auto-clusters into display meta-items
   255→    if plan_data and not effective_cluster and not plan_data.get("active_cluster"):
   256→        items = collapse_clusters(items, plan_data)
   257→
   258→    # Apply count truncation after collapsing
   259→    if opts.count:
   260→        items = items[: opts.count]
   261→        queue["items"] = items
   262→        queue["total"] = len(items)
   263→
   264→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   265→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   266→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   267→        state,
   268→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   269→    )
   270→
   271→    payload = _build_next_payload(
   272→        queue=queue,
   273→        items=items,
   274→        state=state,
   275→        narrative=narrative,
   276→        plan_data=plan_data,
   277→    )
   278→    write_query(payload)
   279→
   280→    if _emit_requested_output(opts, payload, items):
   281→        return
   282→
   283→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   284→    issues_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_issues(
   285→        state.get("issues", {}),
   286→        state.get("scan_path"),
   287→    )
   288→
   289→    # Extract frozen plan-start score and queue breakdown for lifecycle display
   290→    plan_start_strict, breakdown = _plan_queue_context(
   291→        state=state,
   292→        plan_data=plan_data,
   293→        context=ctx,
   294→    )
   295→    queue_total = breakdown.queue_total if breakdown else 0
   296→
   297→    _render_queue_header(queue, opts.explain)
   298→    strict_score = state_mod.score_snapshot(state).strict
   299→    if _show_empty_queue(
   300→        queue,
   301→        strict_score,
   302→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   303→        target_strict=target_strict,
   304→    ):
   305→        return
   306→
   307→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   308→    potentials = _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials)
   309→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   310→        items, dim_scores, issues_scoped, group=opts.group, explain=opts.explain,
   311→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   312→        cluster_filter=effective_cluster,
   313→    )
   314→    next_nudges_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   315→    next_nudges_mod.render_uncommitted_reminder(plan_data)
   316→    next_nudges_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   317→        state,
   318→        dim_scores,
   319→        issues_scoped,
   320→        strict_score=strict_score,
   321→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   322→        queue_total=queue_total,
   323→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   324→        breakdown=breakdown,
   325→    )
   326→    print()
   327→
   328→    if items and plan_data:
   329→        print_user_message(
   330→            "Hey — the task details are printed above. Just start"
   331→            " working on it. When you're done, resolve it with"
   332→            " `desloppify plan resolve` and then run `desloppify"
   333→            " next` again. If this doesn't seem like the right"
   334→            " priority, run `desloppify plan show` to see the full"
   335→            " queue, or `desloppify plan --help` for all plan tools."
   336→            " Don't stop until you're done — unless I've told you"
   337→            " otherwise, just keep going."
   338→        )
   339→
   340→
   341→__all__ = ["NextOptions", "_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   342→
```

```
    32→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    33→    """Return True when the living plan has any user-managed queue metadata."""
    34→    return bool(
    35→        plan.get("queue_order")
    36→        or plan.get("overrides")
    37→        or plan.get("clusters")
    38→        or plan.get("skipped")
    39→    )
    40→
    41→
    42→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, reconcile_fn) -> bool:
    43→    """Apply standard post-scan plan reconciliation when user content exists."""
    44→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
    45→        return False
    46→    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)
    47→    if recon.resurfaced:
    48→        print(
    49→            colorize(
    50→                f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
    51→                "cyan",
    52→            )
    53→        )
    54→    return bool(recon.changes)
    55→
    56→
    57→def _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
    58→    """Sync unscored subjective dimensions into the plan queue."""

    61→        print(
    62→            colorize(
    63→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.injected)} unscored subjective dimension(s) queued for initial review.",
    64→                "cyan",
    65→            )
    66→        )
    67→    return bool(sync.changes)
    68→
    69→
    70→def _sync_stale_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
    71→    """Sync stale subjective dimensions (prune refreshed + inject stale) in plan queue."""
    72→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)

   104→    ))

   107→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   108→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   109→    existing = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   110→    if existing and not isinstance(existing, dict):
   111→        return False
   112→    # Seed when empty OR when it's the reset sentinel ({"reset": True})
   113→    if existing and not existing.get("reset"):
   114→        return False
   115→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   116→    if scores.strict is None:
   117→        return False
   118→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   119→        "strict": scores.strict,
   120→        "overall": scores.overall,
   121→        "objective": scores.objective,
   122→        "verified": scores.verified,
   123→    }
   124→    # Record scan count at cycle start so gates can detect whether a new scan ran
   125→    plan["scan_count_at_plan_start"] = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   126→    return True
   127→
   128→
   129→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   130→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   131→) -> bool:
   132→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   133→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   134→        return False
   135→
   136→    try:
   137→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   138→            ScoreDisplayMode,
   139→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   140→            score_display_mode,
   141→        )
   142→
   143→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   144→        frozen_strict = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   145→        queue_empty = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict) is not ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   146→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   147→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "run post-scan plan reconciliation", exc)
   148→        return False
   149→    if not queue_empty:
   150→        return False
   151→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   152→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   153→    return True

   157→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   158→    plan: dict[str, object],
   159→) -> tuple[float, object, bool]:
   160→    from desloppify.base.config import target_strict_score_from_config
   161→    from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_subjective_visibility
   162→
   163→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   164→    policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
   165→        runtime.state,
   166→        target_strict=target_strict,
   167→        plan=plan,
   168→    )
   169→    cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   170→    return target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed

   281→def _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(

   284→) -> bool:

   288→        return True

   297→def reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: Any) -> None:
   298→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   299→    plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   300→    try:
   301→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   302→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   303→        logger.warning("Plan reconciliation skipped (load failed): %s", exc)
   304→        return
   305→    dirty = False
   306→
   307→    if _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, runtime.state, reconcile_plan_after_scan):
   308→        dirty = True
   309→
   310→    if _sync_unscored_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   311→        dirty = True
   312→
   313→    target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed = _subjective_policy_context(
   314→        runtime,
   315→        plan,
   316→    )
   317→    if _sync_stale_and_log(
   318→        plan,
   319→        runtime.state,
   320→        policy=policy,
   321→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   322→    ):
   323→        dirty = True
   324→
   325→    if _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   326→        plan,
   327→        runtime.state,
   328→        target_strict=target_strict,
   329→        policy=policy,
   330→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   331→    ):
   332→        dirty = True
   333→
   334→    if _sync_communicate_score_and_log(plan, runtime.state, policy=policy):
   335→        dirty = True
   336→    if _sync_create_plan_and_log(plan, runtime.state, policy=policy):
   337→        dirty = True
   338→    if _sync_triage_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   339→        dirty = True
   340→    if _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   341→        dirty = True
   342→
   343→    if dirty:
   344→        try:
   345→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   346→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   347→            logger.warning("Plan reconciliation save failed: %s", exc)
   348→
```

```
1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    13→
    14→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan import stale_policy as stale_policy_mod
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import promoted_insertion_index
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
    19→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS as _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    20→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→
    24→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    25→TRIAGE_ID = "triage::pending"  # deprecated, kept for migration
    26→
    27→TRIAGE_PREFIX = "triage::"
    28→TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS = (
    29→    "triage::observe",
    30→    "triage::reflect",
    31→    "triage::organize",
    32→    "triage::commit",
    33→)
    34→TRIAGE_IDS = set(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
    35→WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
    36→WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
    37→WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
    38→WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"
    39→WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"
    40→SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("triage::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
    41→
    42→
    43→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    44→# Result dataclasses
    45→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    46→
    47→@dataclass
    48→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    49→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    50→
    51→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    52→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    53→
    54→    @property
    55→    def changes(self) -> int:
    56→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    57→
    58→
    59→@dataclass
    60→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    61→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    62→
    63→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    64→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    65→
    66→    @property
    67→    def changes(self) -> int:
    68→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    69→
    70→
    71→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    72→# ID helpers
    73→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    74→
    75→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    76→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    77→    return stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
    78→        state,
    79→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    80→    )
    81→
    82→
    83→def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    84→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    85→
    86→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    87→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    88→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
    89→    """
    90→    return stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
    91→        state,
    92→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    93→    )
    94→
    95→
    96→def current_under_target_ids(
    97→    state: StateModel,
    98→    *,
    99→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   100→) -> set[str]:
   101→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.
   102→
   103→    These are dimensions whose assessment is still current (not needing refresh)
   104→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.
   105→    """
   106→    return stale_policy_mod.current_under_target_ids(
   107→        state,
   108→        target_strict=target_strict,
   109→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   110→    )
```
