```
   112→
   113→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   114→# Helpers for sync_stale_dimensions / sync_unscored_dimensions
   115→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   116→
   117→def _has_objective_backlog(
   118→    state: StateModel,
   119→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   120→) -> bool:
   121→    """Return whether an objective backlog exists (open non-subjective issues)."""
   122→    if policy is not None:
   123→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
   124→    return any(
   125→        f.get("status") == "open"
   126→        and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   127→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   128→        for f in state.get("issues", {}).values()
   129→    )
   130→
   131→
   132→def _prune_subjective_ids(
   133→    order: list[str],
   134→    *,
   135→    keep_ids: set[str],
   136→    pruned: list[str],
   137→) -> None:
   138→    """Remove subjective IDs from *order* that are not in *keep_ids*, appending removed to *pruned*."""
   139→    to_remove = [
   140→        fid for fid in order
   141→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   142→        and fid not in keep_ids
   143→    ]
   144→    for fid in to_remove:
   145→        order.remove(fid)
   146→        pruned.append(fid)
   147→
   148→
   149→def _inject_subjective_ids(
   150→    order: list[str],
   151→    plan: PlanModel,
   152→    *,
   153→    inject_ids: set[str],
   154→    to_front: bool,
   155→    injected: list[str],
   156→) -> None:
   157→    """Inject subjective IDs into *order* if not already present.
   158→
   159→    When *to_front* is True, inserts after promoted items (front of queue).
   160→    Otherwise appends to the back.
   161→    """
   162→    existing = set(order)
   163→    if to_front:
   164→        insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   165→        for sid in reversed(sorted(inject_ids)):
   166→            if sid not in existing:
   167→                order.insert(insert_at, sid)
   168→                injected.append(sid)
   169→    else:
   170→        for sid in sorted(inject_ids):
   171→            if sid not in existing:
   172→                order.append(sid)
   173→                injected.append(sid)
   174→
   175→
   176→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   177→# Helpers for sync_triage_needed
   178→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   179→
   180→def _open_review_issue_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   181→    """Return set of open issue IDs whose detector is 'review' or 'concerns'."""
   182→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   183→    return {
   184→        fid for fid, f in issues.items()
   185→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   186→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   187→    }
   188→
   189→
   190→def _new_review_ids_since_triage(
   191→    state: StateModel,
   192→    meta: dict,
   193→) -> set[str]:
   194→    """Return review issue IDs that are new since the last triage."""
   195→    current_review_ids = _open_review_issue_ids(state)
   196→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   197→    return current_review_ids - triaged_ids
   198→
   199→
   200→def _prune_all_triage_stages(order: list[str]) -> None:
   201→    """Remove all ``triage::*`` stage IDs from *order*."""
   202→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
   203→        while sid in order:
   204→            order.remove(sid)
   205→
   206→
   207→def _inject_pending_triage_stages(
   208→    order: list[str],
   209→    plan: PlanModel,
   210→    confirmed: set[str],
   211→) -> bool:
   212→    """Inject triage stages for pending (unconfirmed) items.
   213→
   214→    Returns True if any stages were injected.
   215→    """
   216→    insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   217→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   218→    existing = set(order)
   219→    injected_count = 0
   220→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   221→        if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
   222→            order.insert(insert_at + injected_count, sid)
   223→            injected_count += 1
   224→    return injected_count > 0
   225→
   226→
   227→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   228→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   229→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   230→
   231→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   232→    plan: PlanModel,
   233→    state: StateModel,
   234→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   235→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   236→
   237→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   238→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   239→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   240→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   241→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   242→    """
   243→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   244→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   245→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   246→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   247→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   248→
   249→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   250→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   251→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored_ids | stale_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   252→
   253→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs after any promoted items -------------
   254→    _inject_subjective_ids(order, plan, inject_ids=unscored_ids, to_front=True, injected=result.injected)
   255→
   256→    return result
   257→
   258→
   259→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   260→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   261→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   262→
   263→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   264→    plan: PlanModel,
   265→    state: StateModel,
   266→    *,
   267→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   268→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   269→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   270→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale and under-target subjective dimensions.
   271→
   272→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   273→       longer stale/under-target and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned
   274→       by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   275→    2. Inject stale and under-target dimension IDs when either:
   276→       a. No objective items remain (mid-cycle: append to back), OR
   277→       b. A cycle just completed (post-cycle: insert at front so subjective
   278→          review takes priority over new objective issues).
   279→    """
   280→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   281→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   282→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   283→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   284→    injectable_ids = stale_ids | under_target_ids
   285→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   286→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   287→
   288→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   289→    # Only prune IDs that are no longer injectable and not unscored.
   290→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=injectable_ids | unscored_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   291→
   292→    # --- Inject or evict stale + under-target dimensions -----------------
   293→    has_real_items = _has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
   294→    should_inject = not has_real_items or cycle_just_completed
   295→
   296→    if not should_inject:
   297→        # Mid-cycle with objective backlog: evict any stale/under-target IDs
   298→        # that are present in the queue.  They may have been grandfathered from
   299→        # the unscored phase and should not be visible until the objective
   300→        # backlog clears or a cycle completes.
   301→        to_evict = [
   302→            fid for fid in order
   303→            if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   304→            and fid in injectable_ids
   305→        ]
   306→        for fid in to_evict:
   307→            order.remove(fid)
   308→            result.pruned.append(fid)
   309→
   310→    if should_inject and injectable_ids:
   311→        to_front = cycle_just_completed and has_real_items
   312→        _inject_subjective_ids(order, plan, inject_ids=injectable_ids, to_front=to_front, injected=result.injected)
   313→
   314→    return result
   315→
   316→
   317→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   318→# Triage snapshot hash + sync
   319→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   320→
   321→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   322→    """Hash open review issue IDs to detect changes.
   323→
   324→    Returns empty string when there are no open review issues.
   325→    """
   326→    return stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   327→
   328→
   329→@dataclass
   330→class TriageSyncResult:
   331→    """What changed during a triage sync."""
   332→
   333→    injected: bool = False
   334→    pruned: bool = False
   335→
   336→    @property
   337→    def changes(self) -> int:
   338→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   339→
   340→
   341→def sync_triage_needed(
   342→    plan: PlanModel,
   343→    state: StateModel,
   344→) -> TriageSyncResult:
   345→    """Inject 4 triage stage IDs at front of queue when review issues change.
   346→
   347→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   348→
   349→    When stages are already present but all new issues have been resolved
   350→    since injection, auto-prunes the stale stages and updates the hash.
   351→
   352→    When issues are *resolved* (current IDs are a subset of previously
   353→    triaged IDs), the snapshot hash is updated silently — no re-triage
   354→    is needed since the user is working through the plan.
   355→    """
   356→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   357→    result = TriageSyncResult()
   358→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   359→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   360→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   361→
   362→    # Check if any triage stage is already in queue
   363→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   364→
   365→    current_hash = review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   366→    last_hash = meta.get("issue_snapshot_hash", "")
   367→
   368→    if already_present:
   369→        # Stages present — check if the reason for injection still applies.
   370→        # Only auto-prune when triage was completed before (hash exists),
   371→        # all new issues have been resolved, and no triage work is in
   372→        # progress.  This avoids pruning the initial triage or a
   373→        # user-started triage session.
   374→        if last_hash and not confirmed:
   375→            new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   376→
   377→            if not new_since_triage:
   378→                # No new issues remain — prune stale stages
   379→                _prune_all_triage_stages(order)
   380→                if current_hash:
   381→                    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   382→                    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   383→                result.pruned = True
   384→        return result
   385→
   386→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   387→        # Distinguish "new issues appeared" from "issues were resolved".
   388→        # Only re-triage when genuinely new issues exist.
   389→        new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   390→
   391→        if new_since_triage:
   392→            # New review issues appeared — re-triage needed
   393→            result.injected = _inject_pending_triage_stages(order, plan, confirmed)
   394→        else:
   395→            # Only resolved issues changed the hash — update silently
   396→            meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   397→            plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   398→
   399→    return result
   400→

   402→@dataclass
   403→class ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   404→    """What changed during a score-checkpoint sync."""
   405→
   406→    injected: bool = False
   407→
   408→    @property
   409→    def changes(self) -> int:
   410→        return int(self.injected)
   411→
   412→
   413→def sync_score_checkpoint_needed(
   414→    plan: PlanModel,
   415→    state: StateModel,
   416→    *,
   417→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   418→) -> ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   419→    """Inject ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` when all initial reviews complete.
   420→
   421→    Injects when:
   422→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   423→    - ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` is not already in the queue
   424→
   425→    Positioned after subjective items but before triage/create-plan
   426→    so the user sees their updated strict score right after reviews finish.
   427→    """
   428→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   429→    result = ScoreCheckpointSyncResult()
   430→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   431→
   432→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   433→        return result
   434→
   435→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   436→    if policy is not None:
   437→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   438→            return result
   439→    else:
   440→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   441→        if unscored:
   442→            return result
   443→
   444→    # Insert after any subjective items, before triage/workflow/issues
   445→    insert_at = 0
   446→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   447→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX):
   448→            insert_at = i + 1
   449→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID)
   450→    result.injected = True
   451→    return result
   452→

   454→@dataclass
   455→class CreatePlanSyncResult:
   456→    """What changed during a create-plan sync."""
   457→
   458→    injected: bool = False
   459→
   460→    @property
   461→    def changes(self) -> int:
   462→        return int(self.injected)
   463→
   464→
   465→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   466→    plan: PlanModel,
   467→    state: StateModel,
   468→    *,
   469→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   470→) -> CreatePlanSyncResult:
   471→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   472→
   473→    Only injects when:
   474→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   475→    - At least one objective issue exists
   476→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   477→    - No triage stages are pending
   478→    """
   479→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   480→    result = CreatePlanSyncResult()
   481→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   482→
   483→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   484→        return result
   485→
   486→    # Don't inject if triage stages are pending
   487→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   488→        return result
   489→
   490→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   491→    if policy is not None:
   492→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   493→            return result
   494→        has_objective = policy.has_objective_backlog
   495→    else:
   496→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   497→        if unscored:
   498→            return result
   499→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
   500→        has_objective = any(
   501→            f.get("status") == "open"
   502→            and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   503→            for f in issues.values()
   504→        )
   505→    if not has_objective:
   506→        return result
   507→
   508→    # Insert after any subjective/workflow items, at the end of the
   509→    # synthetic block (so create-plan comes after score-checkpoint).
   510→    insert_at = 0
   511→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   512→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(TRIAGE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX):
   513→            insert_at = i + 1
   514→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   515→    result.injected = True
   516→    return result
   517→

   519→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   520→    """Return the set of open review/concerns issue IDs added since last triage.
   521→
   522→    Returns an empty set when no prior triage has recorded ``triaged_ids``.
   523→    """
   524→    return stale_policy_mod.compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)

   527→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   528→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   529→
   530→    Returns True when genuinely *new* review issues appeared since the
   531→    last triage.  Triage stage IDs being in the queue alone is not
   532→    sufficient — the new issues that triggered injection may have been
   533→    resolved since then.
   534→
   535→    When issues are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   536→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   537→    through the plan.
   538→    """
   539→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   540→    return stale_policy_mod.is_triage_stale(plan, state, triage_ids=TRIAGE_IDS)

   543→@dataclass
   544→class ImportScoresSyncResult:

   553→
   554→def sync_import_scores_needed(
   555→    plan: PlanModel,
   556→    state: StateModel,
   557→    *,
   558→    assessment_mode: str | None = None,
   559→) -> ImportScoresSyncResult:
   560→    """Inject ``workflow::import-scores`` after issues-only import.
   561→
   562→    Only injects when:
   563→    - Assessment mode was ``issues_only`` (scores were skipped)
   564→    - ``workflow::import-scores`` is not already in the queue
   565→    - There are assessments in the payload that could be imported
   566→
   567→    Positioned after score-checkpoint, before create-plan.
   568→    """
   569→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   570→    result = ImportScoresSyncResult()
   571→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   572→
   573→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   574→        return result
   575→
   576→    # Only inject when scores were skipped (issues-only mode)
   577→    if assessment_mode != "issues_only":
   578→        return result
   579→
   580→    # Insert after any subjective/workflow items
   581→    insert_at = 0
   582→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   583→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX):
   584→            insert_at = i + 1
   585→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID)
   586→    result.injected = True
   587→    return result
   588→
   589→
   590→@dataclass
   591→class CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   592→    """What changed during a communicate-score sync."""
   593→
   594→    injected: bool = False
   595→
   596→    @property
   597→    def changes(self) -> int:
   598→        return int(self.injected)
   599→
   600→
   601→def sync_communicate_score_needed(
   602→    plan: PlanModel,
   603→    state: StateModel,
   604→    *,
   605→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   606→    scores_just_imported: bool = False,
   607→) -> CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   608→    """Inject ``workflow::communicate-score`` when scores should be shown.
   609→
   610→    Injects when either:
   611→    - All initial subjective reviews are complete (no unscored dimensions), OR
   612→    - Scores were just imported (trusted/attested/override)
   613→
   614→    And ``workflow::communicate-score`` is not already in the queue.
   615→    Positioned after subjective items but before triage/create-plan.
   616→    """
   617→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   618→    result = CommunicateScoreSyncResult()
   619→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   620→
   621→    # Also treat legacy score-checkpoint as already-present
   622→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in order or WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   623→        return result
   624→
   625→    # Trigger 1: scores just imported
   626→    should_inject = scores_just_imported
   627→
   628→    # Trigger 2: all initial reviews complete (no unscored dimensions)
   629→    if not should_inject:
   630→        if policy is not None:
   631→            should_inject = not policy.unscored_ids
   632→        else:
   633→            should_inject = not current_unscored_ids(state)
   634→
   635→    if not should_inject:
   636→        return result
   637→
   638→    # Insert after any subjective items, before triage/workflow/issues
   639→    insert_at = 0
   640→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   641→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX):
   642→            insert_at = i + 1
   643→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID)
   644→    result.injected = True
   645→    return result
   646→
   647→
   648→__all__ = [
   649→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   650→    "TRIAGE_ID",
   651→    "TRIAGE_IDS",
   652→    "TRIAGE_PREFIX",
   653→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS",
   654→    "SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES",
   655→    "WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID",
   656→    "WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID",
   657→    "WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID",
   658→    "WORKFLOW_PREFIX",
   659→    "WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID",
   660→    "CommunicateScoreSyncResult",
   661→    "CreatePlanSyncResult",
   662→    "ImportScoresSyncResult",
   663→    "ScoreCheckpointSyncResult",
   664→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
   665→    "TriageSyncResult",
   666→    "UnscoredDimensionSyncResult",
   667→    "current_under_target_ids",
   668→    "current_unscored_ids",
   669→    "compute_new_issue_ids",
   670→    "is_triage_stale",
   671→    "review_issue_snapshot_hash",
   672→    "sync_communicate_score_needed",
   673→    "sync_create_plan_needed",
   674→    "sync_import_scores_needed",
   675→    "sync_score_checkpoint_needed",
   676→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   677→    "sync_triage_needed",
   678→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   679→]
   680→
```

```
1→"""CLI entrypoint for review command."""
     2→

     4→

     6→import sys

     8→
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    11→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    12→
    13→from .batch.orchestrator import do_import_run, do_run_batches
    14→from .external import do_external_start, do_external_submit
    15→from .importing.cmd import do_import, do_validate_import
    16→from .merge import do_merge
    17→from .preflight import review_rerun_preflight
    18→from .prepare import do_prepare
    19→
    20→
    21→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    22→class ReviewOptions:
    23→    """All user-facing review command options extracted once from argparse."""
    24→
    25→    merge: bool = False
    26→    run_batches: bool = False
    27→    import_run_dir: str | None = None
    28→    external_start: bool = False
    29→    external_submit: bool = False
    30→    import_file: str | None = None
    31→    validate_import_file: str | None = None
    32→    session_id: str | None = None
    33→    allow_partial: bool = False
    34→    scan_after_import: bool = False

    36→    dry_run: bool = False
    37→    manual_override: bool = False
    38→    attested_external: bool = False
    39→    attest: str | None = None
    40→
    41→    @classmethod
    42→    def from_args(cls, args: argparse.Namespace) -> ReviewOptions:
    43→        return cls(
    44→            merge=bool(getattr(args, "merge", False)),
    45→            run_batches=bool(getattr(args, "run_batches", False)),
    46→            import_run_dir=getattr(args, "import_run_dir", None),
    47→            external_start=bool(getattr(args, "external_start", False)),
    48→            external_submit=bool(getattr(args, "external_submit", False)),
    49→            import_file=getattr(args, "import_file", None),
    50→            validate_import_file=getattr(args, "validate_import_file", None),
    51→            session_id=getattr(args, "session_id", None),
    52→            allow_partial=bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False)),
    53→            scan_after_import=bool(getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False)),
    54→            path=str(getattr(args, "path", ".") or "."),
    55→            dry_run=bool(getattr(args, "dry_run", False)),
    56→            manual_override=bool(getattr(args, "manual_override", False)),
    57→            attested_external=bool(getattr(args, "attested_external", False)),
    58→            attest=getattr(args, "attest", None),
    59→        )
    60→
    61→
    62→def _enable_live_review_output() -> None:
    63→    """Best-effort: force line-buffered review output for non-TTY runners."""
    64→    for stream_name in ("stdout", "stderr"):
    65→        stream = getattr(sys, stream_name, None)
    66→        reconfigure = getattr(stream, "reconfigure", None)
    67→        if not callable(reconfigure):
    68→            continue
    69→        try:
    70→            reconfigure(line_buffering=True, write_through=True)
    71→        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
    72→            _ = exc
    73→
    74→
    75→def _require_lang(lang) -> None:
    76→    if lang:
    77→        return
    78→    raise CommandError("Error: could not detect language. Use --lang.", exit_code=1)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _mode_flags(opts: ReviewOptions) -> list[bool]:
    82→    import_mode = bool(opts.import_file) and not opts.external_submit
    83→    return [
    84→        opts.merge,
    85→        opts.run_batches,
    86→        bool(opts.import_run_dir),
    87→        opts.external_start,
    88→        opts.external_submit,
    89→        import_mode,
    90→        bool(opts.validate_import_file),
    91→    ]
    92→
    93→
    94→def _validate_mode_selection(
    95→    opts: ReviewOptions,
    96→    *,
    97→    mode_flags: list[bool],
    98→) -> None:
    99→    if sum(1 for enabled in mode_flags if enabled) > 1:
   100→        raise CommandError(
   101→            "Error: choose one review mode per command "
   102→            "(--merge | --run-batches | --import-run | --external-start | --external-submit | --import | --validate-import).",
   103→            exit_code=1,
   104→        )
   105→
   106→    if opts.external_submit and not opts.import_file:
   107→        raise CommandError(
   108→            "Error: --external-submit requires --import FILE.",
   109→            exit_code=2,
   110→        )
   111→    if opts.external_submit and not opts.session_id:
   112→        raise CommandError(
   113→            "Error: --external-submit requires --session-id.",
   114→            exit_code=2,
   115→        )
   116→
   117→
   118→def _run_review_mode(
   119→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   120→    *,
   121→    opts: ReviewOptions,
   122→    runtime,
   123→    state,
   124→    lang,
   125→    state_file,
   126→) -> None:
   127→    if opts.merge:
   128→        do_merge(args)
   129→        return
   130→    if opts.run_batches:
   131→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   132→        do_run_batches(
   133→            args,
   134→            state,
   135→            lang,
   136→            state_file,
   137→            config=runtime.config,
   138→        )
   139→        return
   140→    if opts.import_run_dir:
   141→        do_import_run(
   142→            opts.import_run_dir,
   143→            state,
   144→            lang,
   145→            state_file,
   146→            config=runtime.config,
   147→            allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   148→            scan_after_import=opts.scan_after_import,
   149→            scan_path=opts.path,
   150→        )
   151→        return
   152→    if opts.external_start:
   153→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   154→        do_external_start(
   155→            args,
   156→            state,
   157→            lang,
   158→            config=runtime.config,
   159→        )
   160→        return
   161→    if opts.external_submit:
   162→        do_external_submit(
   163→            import_file=str(opts.import_file),
   164→            session_id=str(opts.session_id),
   165→            state=state,
   166→            lang=lang,
   167→            state_file=state_file,
   168→            config=runtime.config,
   169→            allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   170→            scan_after_import=opts.scan_after_import,
   171→            scan_path=opts.path,
   172→            dry_run=opts.dry_run,
   173→        )
   174→        return
   175→    if opts.validate_import_file:
   176→        do_validate_import(
   177→            opts.validate_import_file,
   178→            lang,
   179→            allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   180→            manual_override=opts.manual_override,
   181→            attested_external=opts.attested_external,
   182→            manual_attest=opts.attest,
   183→        )
   184→        return
   185→
   186→    if opts.import_file:
   187→        do_import(
   188→            opts.import_file,
   189→            state,
   190→            lang,
   191→            state_file,
   192→            config=runtime.config,
   193→            allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   194→            manual_override=opts.manual_override,
   195→            attested_external=opts.attested_external,
   196→            manual_attest=opts.attest,
   197→        )
   198→        return
   199→    review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   200→    do_prepare(args, state, lang, state_file, config=runtime.config)
   201→
   202→
   203→def cmd_review(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   204→    """Prepare or import subjective code review issues."""
   205→    _enable_live_review_output()
   206→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   207→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   208→    state = runtime.state
   209→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   210→    _require_lang(lang)
   211→
   212→    opts = ReviewOptions.from_args(args)
   213→    mode_flags = _mode_flags(opts)
   214→    _validate_mode_selection(
   215→        opts,
   216→        mode_flags=mode_flags,
   217→    )
   218→    _run_review_mode(
   219→        args,
   220→        opts=opts,
   221→        runtime=runtime,
   222→        state=state,
   223→        lang=lang,
   224→        state_file=state_file,
   225→    )
```

```
1→"""Prepare flow for review command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
     8→from desloppify.base.coercions import coerce_positive_int
     9→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→import desloppify.intelligence.narrative.core as narrative_mod
    12→from desloppify.intelligence import review as review_mod
    13→
    14→from .helpers import parse_dimensions
    15→from .packet.policy import coerce_review_batch_file_limit, redacted_review_config
    16→from .runtime.setup import setup_lang_concrete
    17→
    18→
    19→def do_prepare(
    20→    args,
    21→    state,
    22→    lang,
    23→    _state_path,
    24→    *,
    25→    config: dict,
    26→) -> None:
    27→    """Prepare mode: holistic-only review packet in query.json."""
    28→    path = Path(args.path)
    29→    dims = parse_dimensions(args)
    30→    dimensions = list(dims) if dims else None
    31→    retrospective = bool(getattr(args, "retrospective", False))
    32→    retrospective_max_issues = coerce_positive_int(
    33→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_issues", None),
    34→        default=30,
    35→    )
    36→    retrospective_max_batch_items = coerce_positive_int(
    37→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_batch_items", None),
    38→        default=20,
    39→    )
    40→
    41→    lang_run, found_files = setup_lang_concrete(lang, path, config)
    42→
    43→    lang_name = lang_run.name
    44→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
    45→        state,
    46→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="review"),
    47→    )
    48→    data = review_mod.prepare_holistic_review(
    49→        path,
    50→        lang_run,
    51→        state,
    52→        options=review_mod.HolisticReviewPrepareOptions(
    53→            dimensions=dimensions,
    54→            files=found_files or None,
    55→            max_files_per_batch=coerce_review_batch_file_limit(config),
    56→            include_issue_history=retrospective,
    57→            issue_history_max_issues=retrospective_max_issues,
    58→            issue_history_max_batch_items=retrospective_max_batch_items,
    59→        ),
    60→    )
    61→    next_command = (
    62→        "desloppify review --prepare"
    63→    )
    64→    if retrospective:
    65→        next_command += (
    66→            " --retrospective"
    67→            f" --retrospective-max-issues {retrospective_max_issues}"
    68→            f" --retrospective-max-batch-items {retrospective_max_batch_items}"
    69→        )
    70→    data["config"] = redacted_review_config(config)
    71→    data["narrative"] = narrative
    72→    data["next_command"] = next_command
    73→    total = data.get("total_files", 0)
    74→    if total == 0:
    75→        msg = f"no files found at path '{path}'. Nothing to review."
    76→        scan_path = state.get("scan_path") if isinstance(state, dict) else None
    77→        if scan_path:
    78→            msg += (
    79→                f"\nHint: your last scan used --path {scan_path}. "
    80→                f"Try: desloppify review --prepare --path {scan_path}"
    81→            )
    82→        else:
    83→            msg += "\nHint: pass --path <dir> matching the path used during scan."
    84→        raise CommandError(msg, exit_code=1)
    85→    write_query(data)
    86→    _print_prepare_summary(data, next_command=next_command, retrospective=retrospective)
    87→
    88→
    89→def _print_prepare_summary(
    90→    data: dict, *, next_command: str, retrospective: bool,
```

```
1→"""Tests for shared queue selection in desloppify.work_queue."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import QueueBuildOptions
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import build_work_queue as _build_work_queue
     7→
     8→
     9→def build_work_queue(state, **kwargs):
    10→    return _build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(**kwargs))
    11→
    12→
    13→def _issue(
    14→    fid: str,
    15→    *,
    16→    detector: str = "smells",
    17→    file: str = "src/a.py",
    18→    tier: int = 3,
    19→    confidence: str = "medium",
    20→    status: str = "open",
    21→    detail: dict | None = None,
    22→) -> dict:
    23→    return {
    24→        "id": fid,
    25→        "detector": detector,
    26→        "file": file,
    27→        "tier": tier,
    28→        "confidence": confidence,
    29→        "summary": fid,
    30→        "status": status,
    31→        "detail": detail or {},
    32→    }
    33→
    34→
    35→def _state(issues: list[dict], *, dimension_scores: dict | None = None) -> dict:
    36→    return {
    37→        "issues": {f["id"]: f for f in issues},
    38→        "dimension_scores": dimension_scores or {},
    39→    }
    40→
    41→
    42→def test_review_issue_uses_natural_tier():
    43→    review = _issue(
    44→        "review::src/a.py::naming",
    45→        detector="review",
    46→        tier=2,
    47→        detail={"dimension": "naming_quality"},
    48→    )
    49→    mechanical = _issue("smells::src/a.py::x", detector="smells", tier=3)
    50→    state = _state(
    51→        [review, mechanical],
    52→        dimension_scores={
    53→            "Naming quality": {"score": 94.0, "strict": 94.0, "failing": 1}
    54→        },
    55→    )
    56→
    57→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=False)
    58→    by_id = {item["id"]: item for item in queue["items"] if item["kind"] == "issue"}
    59→    assert "review::src/a.py::naming" in by_id
    60→    assert "smells::src/a.py::x" in by_id
    61→
    62→
    63→def test_review_items_ranked_alongside_mechanical():
    64→    urgent = _issue(
    65→        "security::src/a.py::x", detector="security", tier=1, confidence="high"
    66→    )
    67→    review = _issue(
    68→        "review::src/a.py::naming",
    69→        detector="review",
    70→        tier=2,
    71→        confidence="high",
    72→        detail={"dimension": "naming_quality"},
    73→    )
    74→    state = _state(
    75→        [urgent, review],
    76→        dimension_scores={
    77→            "Naming quality": {"score": 92.0, "strict": 92.0, "failing": 2}
    78→        },
    79→    )
```

```
1→"""Integration tests for lifecycle transitions through reconcile → work queue.
     2→
     3→Exercises the full lifecycle by walking through each stage:
     4→  scan → initial reviews → communicate-score → create-plan → triage → objectives
     5→
     6→Between scans, items are completed via ``purge_ids`` (what ``plan resolve``
     7→does) and the queue is re-checked without reconciling.  ``reconcile`` only
     8→runs at scan boundaries — matching the real CLI flow.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→from pathlib import Path
    14→from types import SimpleNamespace
    15→
    16→import pytest
    17→
    18→import desloppify.app.commands.scan.plan_reconcile as reconcile_mod
    19→from desloppify.base.subjective_dimensions import DISPLAY_NAMES
    20→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_lifecycle import purge_ids
    21→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan
    22→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    23→    TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
    24→    WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
    25→    WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
    26→)
    27→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import (
    28→    QueueBuildOptions,
    29→    build_work_queue,
    30→)
    31→
    32→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    33→# Dimensions we use throughout (3 is enough to be readable)
    34→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    35→DIM_KEYS = ("naming_quality", "logic_clarity", "type_safety")
    36→DIM_DISPLAY = {k: DISPLAY_NAMES[k] for k in DIM_KEYS}
    37→
    38→OBJECTIVE_ISSUES = [
    39→    {"id": "obj-1", "detector": "complexity", "file": "src/app.py", "status": "open",
    40→     "tier": 2, "confidence": "high"},
    41→    {"id": "obj-2", "detector": "naming", "file": "src/util.py", "status": "open",
    42→     "tier": 3, "confidence": "medium"},
    43→]
    44→
    45→
    46→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47→# State / plan helpers
    48→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    49→
    50→def _placeholder_dim_entries(key: str) -> tuple[str, dict, dict]:
    51→    """(display_name, dim_scores_entry, subjective_assessment) for a placeholder."""
    52→    return (
    53→        DIM_DISPLAY[key],
    54→        {
    55→            "score": 0, "strict": 0, "failing": 0, "checks": 0,
    56→            "detectors": {"subjective_assessment": {"placeholder": True, "dimension_key": key}},
    57→        },
    58→        {"score": 0, "placeholder": True},
    59→    )
    60→
    61→
    62→def _scored_dim_entries(key: str, score: float) -> tuple[str, dict, dict]:
    63→    """(display_name, dim_scores_entry, subjective_assessment) with a real score."""
    64→    return (
    65→        DIM_DISPLAY[key],
    66→        {
    67→            "score": score, "strict": score, "failing": 0, "checks": 1,
    68→            "detectors": {"subjective_assessment": {"placeholder": False, "dimension_key": key}},
    69→        },
    70→        {"score": score, "placeholder": False},
    71→    )
    72→
    73→
    74→def _build_state(
    75→    issues: list[dict],
    76→    dims: list[tuple[str, dict, dict]],
    77→    *,
    78→    strict_score: float | None = None,
    79→    overall_score: float | None = None,
    80→    objective_score: float | None = None,
    81→) -> dict:
    82→    state: dict = {
    83→        "issues": {i["id"]: i for i in issues},
    84→        "scan_count": 1,
    85→        "dimension_scores": {},
    86→        "subjective_assessments": {},
    87→    }
    88→    for display, dim_entry, assessment in dims:
    89→        state["dimension_scores"][display] = dim_entry
    90→        dim_key = dim_entry["detectors"]["subjective_assessment"]["dimension_key"]
    91→        state["subjective_assessments"][dim_key] = assessment
    92→    if strict_score is not None:
    93→        state["strict_score"] = strict_score
    94→    if overall_score is not None:
    95→        state["overall_score"] = overall_score
    96→    if objective_score is not None:
    97→        state["objective_score"] = objective_score
    98→    return state
    99→
   100→
   101→def _reconcile(state: dict, plan: dict, monkeypatch) -> dict:
   102→    """Simulate a scan boundary: run reconcile_plan_post_scan with mocked I/O."""
   103→    saved: list[dict] = []
   104→    monkeypatch.setattr(reconcile_mod, "load_plan", lambda _path=None: plan)
   105→    monkeypatch.setattr(reconcile_mod, "save_plan", lambda p, _path=None: saved.append(p))
   106→    reconcile_mod.reconcile_plan_post_scan(SimpleNamespace(
   107→        state=state, state_path=Path("/tmp/fake-state.json"), config={},
   108→    ))
   109→    return saved[-1] if saved else plan
   110→
   111→
   112→def _queue_ids(state: dict, plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   113→    result = build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(plan=plan, count=None))
   114→    return [item["id"] for item in result["items"]]
   115→
   116→
   117→def _spoof_reviews_complete(state: dict) -> None:
   118→    """Mutate state in place: replace all placeholder dims with scored ones."""
   119→    for key in DIM_KEYS:
   120→        display, dim_entry, assessment = _scored_dim_entries(key, 75.0)
   121→        state["dimension_scores"][display] = dim_entry
   122→        state["subjective_assessments"][key] = assessment
   123→    state["strict_score"] = 75.0
   124→    state["overall_score"] = 75.0
   125→    state["objective_score"] = 80.0
   126→
   127→
   128→def _add_review_issues(state: dict) -> None:
   129→    """Mutate state in place: add review detector issues that trigger triage."""
   130→    for key in ("naming_quality", "logic_clarity"):
   131→        fid = f"review-{key}"
   132→        state["issues"][fid] = {
   133→            "id": fid, "detector": "review", "file": "src/app.py",
   134→            "status": "open", "detail": {"dimension": key},
   135→        }
   136→
   137→
   138→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   139→# Test 1: Fresh scan — only initial reviews visible
   140→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   141→
   142→class TestPhase1OnlyInitialReviews:
   143→
   144→    def test_only_initial_reviews_visible(self, monkeypatch):
   145→        """Fresh scan with placeholders: only subjective initial review items appear."""
   146→        state = _build_state(OBJECTIVE_ISSUES, [_placeholder_dim_entries(k) for k in DIM_KEYS])
   147→        plan = _reconcile(state, empty_plan(), monkeypatch)
   148→
   149→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   150→
   151→        assert all(fid.startswith("subjective::") for fid in ids)
   152→        assert len(ids) == len(DIM_KEYS)
   153→        assert "obj-1" not in ids
   154→        assert "obj-2" not in ids
   155→
   156→
   157→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   158→# Test 2: Complete reviews (between scans) → objectives unlock immediately
   159→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   160→
   161→class TestCompleteReviewsUnlocksObjectives:
   162→
   163→    def test_objectives_visible_after_completing_reviews(self, monkeypatch):
   164→        """After completing reviews (no reconcile), lifecycle filter lets objectives through."""
   165→        state = _build_state(OBJECTIVE_ISSUES, [_placeholder_dim_entries(k) for k in DIM_KEYS])
   166→        plan = _reconcile(state, empty_plan(), monkeypatch)
   167→
   168→        # Complete reviews: update state + purge subjective IDs
   169→        subj_ids = [fid for fid in _queue_ids(state, plan) if fid.startswith("subjective::")]
   170→        _spoof_reviews_complete(state)
   171→        purge_ids(plan, subj_ids)
   172→
   173→        # No reconcile — just check the queue
   174→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   175→        assert "obj-1" in ids
   176→        assert "obj-2" in ids
   177→        # communicate-score hasn't been injected yet (no scan since reviews)
   178→        assert WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID not in ids
   179→
   180→
   181→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   182→# Test 3: Next scan after reviews → communicate-score + create-plan appear
   183→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   184→
   185→class TestScanAfterReviewsInjectsWorkflow:
   186→
   187→    def test_workflow_items_injected_on_next_scan(self, monkeypatch):
   188→        """Scan after completing reviews injects communicate-score and create-plan."""
   189→        state = _build_state(OBJECTIVE_ISSUES, [_placeholder_dim_entries(k) for k in DIM_KEYS])
   190→        plan = _reconcile(state, empty_plan(), monkeypatch)
   191→
   192→        # Complete reviews between scans
   193→        subj_ids = [fid for fid in _queue_ids(state, plan) if fid.startswith("subjective::")]
   194→        _spoof_reviews_complete(state)
   195→        purge_ids(plan, subj_ids)
   196→
   197→        # --- Next scan (reconcile) ---
   198→        plan = _reconcile(state, plan, monkeypatch)
   199→
   200→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   201→        assert WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in ids
   202→        assert WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in ids
   203→        # communicate-score comes before create-plan
   204→        assert ids.index(WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID) < ids.index(WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   205→        # Objectives also visible
   206→        assert "obj-1" in ids
   207→
   208→
   209→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   210→# Test 4: Triage injected when review issues appear
   211→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   212→
   213→class TestTriageInjectedOnScan:
   214→
   215→    def test_triage_after_review_issues_on_scan(self, monkeypatch):
   216→        """Scan with new review issues triggers triage stage injection."""
   217→        state = _build_state(OBJECTIVE_ISSUES, [_placeholder_dim_entries(k) for k in DIM_KEYS])
   218→        plan = _reconcile(state, empty_plan(), monkeypatch)
   219→
   220→        # Complete reviews between scans
   221→        subj_ids = [fid for fid in _queue_ids(state, plan) if fid.startswith("subjective::")]
   222→        _spoof_reviews_complete(state)
   223→        purge_ids(plan, subj_ids)
   224→
   225→        # Add review issues + scan
   226→        _add_review_issues(state)
   227→        plan = _reconcile(state, plan, monkeypatch)
   228→
   229→        ids = _queue_ids(state, plan)
   230→        triage_ids = [fid for fid in ids if fid.startswith("triage::")]
   231→        assert len(triage_ids) == len(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
   232→
   233→        # Triage comes before objective issues
   234→        obj_ids = [fid for fid in ids if fid.startswith("obj-")]
   235→        if obj_ids:
   236→            last_triage = max(ids.index(fid) for fid in triage_ids)
   237→            first_obj = ids.index(obj_ids[0])
   238→            assert last_triage < first_obj
   239→
   240→

   242→# Test 5: Full lifecycle golden path

   244→
   245→class TestFullLifecycleGoldenPath:
   246→

   249→        state = _build_state(OBJECTIVE_ISSUES, [_placeholder_dim_entries(k) for k in DIM_KEYS])
   250→        plan = empty_plan()
```

```
1→"""Post-scan plan workflow nudge.
     2→
     3→Dependency-light module that only imports plan/queue helpers — avoids
     4→the import cycle that previously forced function-level imports in scan.py.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import logging
    10→
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
    12→    ScoreDisplayMode,
    13→    get_plan_start_strict,
    14→    plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
    15→    score_display_mode,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    18→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    19→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    20→
    21→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    22→
    23→
    24→def print_plan_workflow_nudge(state: dict) -> None:
    25→    """Print a queue-count reminder when plan-start scores exist."""
    26→    try:
    27→        plan = load_plan()
    28→        plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(plan)
    29→        if plan_start_strict is None:
    30→            return
    31→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
    32→        mode = score_display_mode(breakdown, plan_start_strict)
    33→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    34→        _logger.debug("plan workflow nudge skipped", exc_info=True)
    35→        return
    36→
    37→    if mode is not ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN:
    38→        return
    39→    queue_total = breakdown.objective_actionable
    40→    print(
    41→        colorize(
    42→            f"  Workflow: {queue_total} queue item{'s' if queue_total != 1 else ''}."
    43→            " Score is frozen until the queue is clear — use `desloppify next` to begin.",
    44→            "dim",
    45→        )
    46→    )
    47→
```

## Desloppify Tool Architecture: Deep Dive into Scan, Review Gates, and Next Command

Based on my thorough exploration of the desloppify source code (installed as editable at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify`), here's a complete understanding of how the "initial-review" gate system works:

---

### 1. **Where is Desloppify Installed?**

- **Location**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify` (editable install via pip)
- **Version**: 0.9.0
- **CLI Entry**: `/Users/user_c042661f/.pyenv/shims/desloppify` (Python tool)
- **Package.json**: Not in reigh project—desloppify is a standalone tool separate from the TypeScript codebase

---

### 2. **The Lifecycle Phases: Three-Stage Gate System**

Desloppify enforces a strict **three-phase lifecycle** that gates different work types. This is defined in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py` (lines 286-306):

```python
def _apply_lifecycle_filter(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    """Enforce lifecycle visibility rules.
    
    The queue enforces a phase order: scan → initial review → objective work.
    
    1. **Initial reviews pending** → only show initial-review subjective items.
       The user must complete first-time reviews before working objective items.
    2. **Objective work remains** → show objective items, hide endgame-only
       subjective reassessments (stale re-reviews).
    3. **Objective drained** → everything visible (endgame).
    """
    if _has_initial_reviews(items):
        # Phase 1: only initial reviews visible
        return [i for i in items if i.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" and i.get("initial_review")]
    if not _has_objective_items(items):
        return items  # endgame: everything visible
    return [i for i in items if not _is_endgame_only(i)]
```

---

### 3. **What Triggers the "initial-review" Gate? Understanding `initial_review` Flag**

The `initial_review` flag is set in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/synthetic.py` (lines 239-258):

```python
item["initial_review"] = is_unassessed
```

Where `is_unassessed` evaluates to **True** when a subjective dimension is:

1. **Placeholder** — never been scored (`placeholder: True` in detectors)
2. **OR** has a score of `0.0` AND zero failing issues (`strict_val <= 0.0 and issues == 0`)

This logic is implemented in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/integrity.py` (lines 95-115):

```python
def unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores: dict | None) -> list[str]:
    """Return subjective dimension display names that are still 0% placeholders."""
    if not dim_scores:
        return []
    
    unassessed: list[str] = []
    for name, info in dim_scores.items():
        detectors = info.get("detectors", {})
        if "subjective_assessment" not in detectors:
            continue
        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
        if isinstance(assessment_meta, dict) and assessment_meta.get("placeholder"):
            unassessed.append(name)  # ← PLACEHOLDER = initial review
            continue
        strict_val = float(info.get("strict", info.get("score", 100.0)))
        issues = int(info.get("failing", 0))
        if strict_val <= 0.0 and issues == 0:
            unassessed.append(name)  # ← ALSO initial review
    
    unassessed.sort()
    return unassessed
```

---

### 4. **How Does `scan` Trigger Initial-Review Gate Reinsertion?**

The scan command **does NOT directly trigger the gate**. Instead:

1. **`desloppify scan` runs** → detectors gather issues → state is merged in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/workflow.py`
2. **After merge, `reconcile_plan_post_scan()` is called** (line 446 in workflow.py)
3. **Reconciliation calls `sync_unscored_dimensions()`** in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py` (lines 231-256):
   - Takes **all currently unscored (placeholder) dimension IDs**
   - **Prepends them to the front of `plan.queue_order`** (after any promoted items)
   - This is an **unconditional insertion** at every scan

```python
def sync_unscored_dimensions(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    """1. **Prune** — remove subjective::* IDs that are no longer unscored
       2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to *front* of queue_order
    """
    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    
    # Prune stale scored items
    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored_ids | stale_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
    
    # Inject unscored at front
    _inject_subjective_ids(order, plan, inject_ids=unscored_ids, to_front=True, injected=result.injected)
    
    return result
```

---

### 5. **Difference Between "Start Loop" vs "End Loop" Scans (and Loop Phases)**

There is **no explicit concept of "start loop" vs "end loop" scans** in the code. However, there are **implicit phases**:

1. **Cycle Start** — When `plan_start_scores` is empty (`{}`), scan records the initial scores:
   ```python
   def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict, state: dict) -> bool:
       existing = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
       if existing and not isinstance(existing, dict):
           return False
       if existing and not existing.get("reset"):
           return False  # Don't re-seed if already populated
   ```
   
   This happens **once per cycle** when a plan is first created or reset.

2. **Mid-Cycle Scans** — After initial review items are cleared and user is working objective issues, subjective items are **evicted** from the queue unless the objective backlog is empty:
   ```python
   if not should_inject:
       # Mid-cycle with objective backlog: evict stale/under-target IDs
       to_evict = [fid for fid in order if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) and fid in injectable_ids]
       for fid in to_evict:
           order.remove(fid)
   ```

3. **Cycle End** — When queue is fully drained, `plan_start_scores` is cleared and can be re-seeded on the next scan.

**Key insight**: The system does **NOT distinguish mid-loop scans from end-loop scans**. Instead, it checks:
- Are there **unscored (placeholder) dimensions**? → Show only those (Phase 1)
- Are there **objective issues remaining**? → Hide stale reviews (Phase 2)
- Is the queue empty? → Show everything (Phase 3)

---

### 6. **Does Scanning Mid-Loop Trigger Subjective Review Gates? Yes and Conditionally**

When you run `desloppify scan` mid-cycle:

- **Always**: `sync_unscored_dimensions()` prepends any NEW unscored dimensions to the queue front
- **Conditionally**: `sync_stale_dimensions()` (called next) will **evict** stale/under-target subjective items IF an objective backlog exists

```python
def sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state, *, policy=None, cycle_just_completed=False):
    """Inject or evict stale + under-target dimensions based on phase.
    
    - If objective backlog EXISTS and cycle NOT just completed → EVICT stale items
    - If objective backlog EMPTY OR cycle just completed → INJECT stale items
    """
    has_real_items = _has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
    should_inject = not has_real_items or cycle_just_completed
    
    if not should_inject:
        # Mid-cycle with backlog: remove stale/under-target from queue
        to_evict = [fid for fid in order if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) and fid in injectable_ids]
        for fid in to_evict:
            order.remove(fid)
```

**This prevents subjective review gates from blocking objective work mid-cycle.**

---

### 7. **How Does `next` Decide What to Show?**

The `next` command in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next/cmd.py`:

1. **Builds the full queue** with `build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(...))`
2. **The queue builder applies the lifecycle filter** (Phase 1/2/3 logic)
3. **Returns only items matching the current phase**:
   - Phase 1 (initial reviews pending) → Only `subjective_dimension` items with `initial_review: True`
   - Phase 2 (objective work) → Mechanical issues, triage, workflow actions (NOT stale reviews)
   - Phase 3 (endgame) → Everything

```python
def _build_and_render_queue(args, state, config):
    queue = build_work_queue(
        state,
        options=QueueBuildOptions(
            count=None,
            scope=opts.scope,
            status=opts.status,
            include_subjective=True,
            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
            explain=opts.explain,
            include_skipped=opts.include_skipped,
            cluster=effective_cluster,
            context=ctx,
        ),
    )
    items = queue.get("items", [])
```

**`next` ALWAYS checks gates automatically** — no explicit gate-check flag needed.

---

### 8. **Key Files & Code Locations**

| Purpose | File | Key Functions |
|---------|------|---------------|
| **Lifecycle filtering** | `engine/_work_queue/core.py` | `_apply_lifecycle_filter()`, `_has_initial_reviews()`, `_is_endgame_only()` |
| **Initial review detection** | `engine/_work_queue/synthetic.py` | `build_subjective_items()` (line 257: `item["initial_review"] = is_unassessed`) |
| **Unscored dimension detection** | `intelligence/integrity.py` | `unassessed_subjective_dimensions()` |
| **Scan→Plan reconciliation** | `app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py` | `reconcile_plan_post_scan()`, `_sync_unscored_and_log()` |
| **Unscored sync logic** | `engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py` | `sync_unscored_dimensions()` (lines 231-256), `sync_stale_dimensions()` (lines 263-314) |
| **Next command queue building** | `app/commands/next/cmd.py` | `_build_and_render_queue()` (line 210) |
| **Test cases** | `tests/commands/test_lifecycle_transitions.py` | Full lifecycle walkthrough: scan → initial reviews → objectives |

---

### 9. **The Complete Flow: End-to-End Example**

**Fresh scan with 3 unscored dimensions:**
1. User runs `desloppify scan`
2. Detectors find objective issues, state updated with dimension_scores (all placeholders)
3. `reconcile_plan_post_scan()` calls `sync_unscored_dimensions()`
4. Plan's `queue_order` becomes: `["subjective::naming", "subjective::logic", "subjective::type_safety", ...]`
5. User runs `desloppify next`
6. `build_work_queue()` applies lifecycle filter → only `["subjective::naming", "subjective::logic", "subjective::type_safety"]` shown
7. Objective issues and triage are **hidden** until all 3 subjective items have `initial_review: False`
8. User completes reviews → dimensions marked as non-placeholder
9. User runs `desloppify next` again
10. Lifecycle filter now shows objective issues (Phase 2)

---

### 10. **Loop Phase Concepts: The Real Implementation**

While there's no "start/end loop" flag, the system **tracks phases via `plan_start_scores`**:

- **`plan_start_scores` populated** → Frozen in a cycle (user is working through the queue)
- **`plan_start_scores` empty** → Ready for new cycle
- **Reconciliation seeds `plan_start_scores`** when transitioning from endgame → new cycle
- **Reconciliation clears `plan_start_scores`** when queue fully drained

This is visible in `app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py` lines 107-153.

---

### 11. **Summary: The Gate Reinsertion Mechanism**

The "auto/initial-review" gate is **not manually reinserted**. Instead:

1. **Every scan** unconditionally runs `sync_unscored_dimensions()`
2. **This prepends all unscored (placeholder) dimensions** to `plan.queue_order`
3. **The lifecycle filter** then automatically hides all other work types when `initial_review` items exist
4. **No configuration needed** — the mechanism is automatic based on assessment state

The cluster label `"auto/initial-review"` (in `render_support.py` line 22) is just a **display label** for the auto-cluster grouping of these items.

```
    41→    path = Path(args.path)
    42→    overrides = command_runtime(args).config.get("zone_overrides", {})
    43→
    44→    files = lang.file_finder(path)
    45→    zone_map = FileZoneMap(
    46→        files, lang.zone_rules, rel_fn=rel, overrides=overrides or None
    47→    )
    48→
    49→    # Group files by zone
    50→    by_zone: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    51→    for f in sorted(files, key=lambda f: rel(f)):
    52→        zone = zone_map.get(f)
    53→        by_zone.setdefault(zone.value, []).append(f)
    54→
    55→    total = len(files)
    56→    print(colorize(f"\nZone classifications ({total} files)\n", "bold"))
    57→
    58→    for zone_val in ["production", "test", "config", "generated", "script", "vendor"]:
    59→        zone_files = by_zone.get(zone_val, [])
    60→        if not zone_files:
    61→            continue
    62→        print(colorize(f"  {zone_val} ({len(zone_files)} files)", "bold"))
    63→        for f in zone_files:
    64→            rp = rel(f)
    65→            is_override = rp in overrides
    66→            suffix = colorize(" (override)", "cyan") if is_override else ""
    67→            print(f"    {rp}{suffix}")
    68→        print()
    69→
    70→    if overrides:
    71→        print(colorize(f"  {len(overrides)} override(s) active", "dim"))
    72→    print(colorize("  Override: desloppify zone set <file> <zone>", "dim"))
    73→    print(colorize("  Clear:    desloppify zone clear <file>", "dim"))
    74→    print_agent_plan(
    75→        ["Fix misclassified files, then re-scan."],
    76→        next_command="desloppify scan",
    77→    )
    78→
    79→
    80→def _zone_set(args: argparse.Namespace):
    81→    """Set a zone override for a file."""
    82→    filepath = args.zone_path
    83→    zone_value = args.zone_value
    84→
    85→    # Validate zone value
    86→    valid_zones = {z.value for z in Zone}
    87→    if zone_value not in valid_zones:
    88→        raise CommandError(
    89→            f"Invalid zone: {zone_value}. Valid: {', '.join(sorted(valid_zones))}"
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    normalized = rel(filepath)
    93→    config = command_runtime(args).config
    94→    config.setdefault("zone_overrides", {})[normalized] = zone_value
    95→    try:
    96→        config_mod.save_config(config)
    97→    except OSError as e:
    98→        raise CommandError(f"could not save config: {e}") from e
    99→    print(f"  Set {normalized} → {zone_value}")
   100→    print(colorize("  Run `desloppify scan` to apply.", "dim"))
   101→    print(colorize("  Next command: `desloppify scan`", "dim"))
   102→
   103→
   104→def _zone_clear(args: argparse.Namespace):
   105→    """Clear a zone override for a file."""
   106→    filepath = args.zone_path
   107→
   108→    normalized = rel(filepath)
   109→    config = command_runtime(args).config
   110→    overrides = config.get("zone_overrides", {})
   111→    if normalized in overrides:
   112→        del overrides[normalized]
   113→        try:
   114→            config_mod.save_config(config)
   115→        except OSError as e:
   116→            raise CommandError(f"could not save config: {e}") from e
   117→        print(f"  Cleared override for {normalized}")
   118→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify scan` to apply.", "dim"))
   119→        print(colorize("  Next command: `desloppify scan`", "dim"))
   120→    else:
   121→        print(colorize(f"  No override found for {normalized}", "yellow"))
   122→
```

```
1→"""Zone classification system — deterministic file intent classification.
     2→
     3→Classifies files into zones (production, test, config, generated, script, vendor)
     4→based on path patterns. Zone metadata flows through issues, scoring, and the LLM.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import logging
    10→import os
    11→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    12→from enum import Enum
    13→
    14→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    15→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones_data import (
    16→    CONFIG_SKIP_DETECTORS,
    17→    SCRIPT_SKIP_DETECTORS,
    18→    SKIP_ALL_DETECTORS,
    19→    TEST_SKIP_DETECTORS,
    20→)
    21→
    22→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    23→
    24→
    25→class Zone(str, Enum):
    26→    """File intent zone — determines scoring and detection policy."""
    27→
    28→    PRODUCTION = "production"
    29→    TEST = "test"
    30→    CONFIG = "config"
    31→    GENERATED = "generated"
    32→    SCRIPT = "script"
    33→    VENDOR = "vendor"
    34→
    35→
    36→# Zones excluded from the health score
    37→EXCLUDED_ZONES = {Zone.TEST, Zone.CONFIG, Zone.GENERATED, Zone.VENDOR}
    38→
    39→# String values for quick lookup in scoring (issues store zone as string)
    40→EXCLUDED_ZONE_VALUES = {z.value for z in EXCLUDED_ZONES}
    41→
    42→
    43→@dataclass
    44→class ZoneRule:
    45→    """A classification rule: zone + list of path patterns.
    46→
    47→    Patterns are matched against relative file paths. First matching rule wins.
    48→    Pattern types (auto-detected from shape):
    49→      - "/dir/"   → substring match on full path (directory marker)
    50→      - ".ext"    → basename ends-with (suffix/extension, e.g. ".d.ts", ".test.")
    51→      - "prefix_" → basename starts-with (trailing underscore)
    52→      - "name.py" → basename exact match (has extension, no /)
    53→      - fallback  → substring on full path
    54→    """
    55→
    56→    zone: Zone
    57→    patterns: list[str]
    58→
    59→
    60→def _match_pattern(rel_path: str, pattern: str) -> bool:
    61→    """Match a zone pattern against a relative file path.
    62→
    63→    See ZoneRule docstring for pattern type conventions.
    64→    """
    65→    basename = os.path.basename(rel_path)
    66→
    67→    # Directory pattern: "/dir/" → substring on padded path
    68→    if pattern.startswith("/") and pattern.endswith("/"):
    69→        return pattern in ("/" + rel_path + "/")
    70→
    71→    # Suffix/extension pattern: starts with "." → contains on basename
    72→    if pattern.startswith("."):
    73→        return pattern in basename
    74→
    75→    # Prefix pattern: ends with "_" → basename starts-with
    76→    if pattern.endswith("_"):
    77→        return basename.startswith(pattern)
    78→
    79→    # Suffix pattern: starts with "_" → basename ends-with (_test.py, _pb2.py)
    80→    if pattern.startswith("_"):
    81→        return basename.endswith(pattern)
    82→
    83→    # Exact basename: has a proper file extension (1-5 chars after last dot),
    84→    # no "/" → exact basename match (config.py, setup.py, conftest.py)
    85→    if "/" not in pattern and "." in pattern:
    86→        ext = pattern.rsplit(".", 1)[-1]
    87→        if ext and len(ext) <= 5 and ext.isalnum():
    88→            return basename == pattern
    89→
    90→    # Fallback: substring on full path (vite.config, tsconfig, eslint, etc.)
    91→    return pattern in rel_path
    92→
    93→
    94→# ── Common zone rules (shared across languages) ──────────
    95→
    96→COMMON_ZONE_RULES = [
    97→    ZoneRule(Zone.VENDOR, ["/vendor/", "/third_party/", "/vendored/"]),
    98→    ZoneRule(Zone.GENERATED, ["/generated/", "/__generated__/"]),
    99→    ZoneRule(Zone.TEST, ["/tests/", "/test/", "/fixtures/"]),
   100→    ZoneRule(Zone.SCRIPT, ["/scripts/", "/bin/"]),
   101→]
   102→
   103→
   104→def classify_file(
   105→    rel_path: str, rules: list[ZoneRule], overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None
   106→) -> Zone:
   107→    """Classify a file by its relative path. Overrides take priority."""
   108→    if overrides:
   109→        override = overrides.get(rel_path)
   110→        if override:
   111→            try:
   112→                return Zone(override)
   113→            except ValueError as exc:
   114→                log_best_effort_failure(
   115→                    logger, f"parse zone override for {rel_path}", exc
   116→                )
   117→    for rule in rules:
   118→        for pattern in rule.patterns:
   119→            if _match_pattern(rel_path, pattern):
   120→                return rule.zone
   121→    return Zone.PRODUCTION
   122→
   123→
   124→class FileZoneMap:
   125→    """Cached zone classification for a set of files.
   126→
   127→    Built once per scan from file list + zone rules.
   128→    """
   129→
   130→    def __init__(
   131→        self,
   132→        files: list[str],
   133→        rules: list[ZoneRule],
   134→        rel_fn=None,
   135→        overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None,
   136→    ):
   137→        """Build a zone map from files, ordered rules, and optional overrides."""
   138→        self._map: dict[str, Zone] = {}
   139→        self._overrides = overrides
   140→        for f in files:
   141→            rp = rel_fn(f) if rel_fn else f
   142→            self._map[f] = classify_file(rp, rules, overrides)
   143→
   144→    def get(self, path: str) -> Zone:
   145→        """Get zone for a file path. Returns PRODUCTION if not classified."""
   146→        return self._map.get(path, Zone.PRODUCTION)
   147→
   148→    def exclude(self, files: list[str], *zones: Zone) -> list[str]:
   149→        """Return files NOT in the given zones."""
   150→        zone_set = set(zones)
   151→        return [f for f in files if self._map.get(f, Zone.PRODUCTION) not in zone_set]
   152→
   153→    def include_only(self, files: list[str], *zones: Zone) -> list[str]:
   154→        """Return files that ARE in the given zones."""
   155→        zone_set = set(zones)
   156→        return [f for f in files if self._map.get(f, Zone.PRODUCTION) in zone_set]
   157→
   158→    def counts(self) -> dict[str, int]:
   159→        """Return file count per zone."""
   160→        counts: dict[str, int] = {}
   161→        for zone in self._map.values():
   162→            counts[zone.value] = counts.get(zone.value, 0) + 1
   163→        return counts
   164→
   165→    def production_count(self) -> int:
   166→        """Count files classified as production."""
   167→        return len(self._map) - self.non_production_count()
   168→
   169→    def non_production_count(self) -> int:
   170→        """Count files in excluded zones (test/config/generated/vendor)."""
   171→        return sum(1 for z in self._map.values() if z in EXCLUDED_ZONES)
   172→
   173→    def all_files(self) -> list[str]:
   174→        """Return all classified file paths."""
   175→        return list(self._map.keys())
   176→
   177→    def items(self) -> list[tuple[str, Zone]]:
   178→        """Return all (path, zone) pairs."""
   179→        return list(self._map.items())
   180→
   181→
   182→# ── Zone detection policies ────────────────────────────────
   183→
   184→
   185→@dataclass
   186→class ZonePolicy:
   187→    """Per-zone detection policy.
   188→
   189→    skip_detectors: detectors to skip entirely for this zone.
   190→    downgrade_detectors: detectors whose confidence is downgraded to "low".
   191→    exclude_from_score: whether issues in this zone are excluded from scoring.
   192→    """
   193→
   194→    skip_detectors: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
   195→    downgrade_detectors: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
   196→    exclude_from_score: bool = False
   197→
   198→
   199→ZONE_POLICIES: dict[Zone, ZonePolicy] = {
   200→    Zone.PRODUCTION: ZonePolicy(),
   201→    Zone.TEST: ZonePolicy(
   202→        skip_detectors=TEST_SKIP_DETECTORS,
   203→        downgrade_detectors={"smells", "structural"},
   204→        exclude_from_score=True,
   205→    ),
   206→    Zone.CONFIG: ZonePolicy(
   207→        skip_detectors=CONFIG_SKIP_DETECTORS,
   208→        exclude_from_score=True,
   209→    ),
   210→    Zone.GENERATED: ZonePolicy(
   211→        skip_detectors=SKIP_ALL_DETECTORS,
   212→        exclude_from_score=True,
   213→    ),
   214→    Zone.VENDOR: ZonePolicy(
   215→        skip_detectors=SKIP_ALL_DETECTORS,
   216→        exclude_from_score=True,
   217→    ),
   218→    Zone.SCRIPT: ZonePolicy(
   219→        skip_detectors=SCRIPT_SKIP_DETECTORS,
   220→        downgrade_detectors={"structural"},
   221→    ),
   222→}
   223→
   224→
   225→# ── Helpers for phase runners ─────────────────────────────
   226→
   227→
   228→def adjust_potential(zone_map, total: int) -> int:
   229→    """Subtract non-production files from a potential count.
   230→
   231→    Uses the zone map's own file list — no need to pass files separately.
   232→    No-op if zone_map is None.
   233→    """
   234→    if zone_map is None:
   235→        return total
   236→    return max(total - zone_map.non_production_count(), 0)
   237→
   238→
   239→def should_skip_issue(zone_map, filepath: str, detector: str) -> bool:
   240→    """Check if a issue should be skipped based on zone policy.
   241→
   242→    Returns True if the file's zone policy says to skip this detector.
   243→    """
   244→    if zone_map is None:
   245→        return False
   246→    zone = zone_map.get(filepath)
   247→    policy = ZONE_POLICIES.get(zone)
   248→    return policy is not None and detector in policy.skip_detectors
   249→
   250→
   251→def filter_entries(
   252→    zone_map, entries: list[dict], detector: str, file_key: str = "file"
   253→) -> list[dict]:
   254→    """Filter detector entries by zone policy. No-op if zone_map is None.
   255→
   256→    If file_key points to a list (e.g. cycle entries with "files"), checks
   257→    the first element.
   258→    """
   259→    if zone_map is None:
   260→        return entries
   261→
   262→    def _get_path(entry):
   263→        val = entry[file_key]
   264→        return val[0] if isinstance(val, list) else val
   265→
   266→    return [
   267→        e for e in entries if not should_skip_issue(zone_map, _get_path(e), detector)
   268→    ]
   269→
```

```
    65→class ScanStateContractError(ValueError):
    66→    """Raised when persisted scan state violates required runtime contracts."""
    67→
    68→
    69→def _clear_needs_rescan_flag(config: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    70→    """Best-effort clear for config.needs_rescan after a successful scan merge."""
    71→    if not config.get("needs_rescan"):
    72→        return
    73→    try:
    74→        config["needs_rescan"] = False
    75→        _save_config(config)
    76→    except OSError as exc:
    77→        _ = exc
    78→        config["needs_rescan"] = True
    79→
    80→
    81→def _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: ScanRuntime) -> None:
    82→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
    83→    _reconcile_plan_post_scan_impl(runtime)
    84→
    85→
    86→def _state_subjective_assessments(
    87→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
    88→) -> dict[str, object]:
    89→    """Return normalized subjective assessment store from state."""
    90→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    91→    if isinstance(assessments, dict):
    92→        return assessments
    93→    raise ScanStateContractError(
    94→        "state.subjective_assessments must be an object; rerun with a valid state file"
    95→    )
    96→
    97→
    98→def _state_lang_capabilities(
    99→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   100→) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   101→    """Return normalized language capability map from state."""
   102→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
   103→    if capabilities is None:
   104→        normalized: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
   105→        state["lang_capabilities"] = normalized
   106→        return normalized
   107→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict):
   108→        return capabilities
   109→    raise ScanStateContractError(
   110→        "state.lang_capabilities must be an object when present"
   111→    )
   112→
   113→
   114→def _state_issues(state: state_mod.StateModel) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   115→    """Return normalized issue map from state."""
   116→    issues = state.get("issues")

   135→    state: state_mod.StateModel

   143→    reset_subjective_count: int = 0

   164→    noise_budget: int
```
