```
   171→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   172→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   173→    config: dict[str, object],
   174→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   175→    lang: LangRun | None,
   176→) -> LangRun | None:
   177→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   178→    if not lang:
   179→        return None
   180→
   181→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   182→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   183→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   184→        lang,
   185→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   186→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   187→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   188→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
   189→            runtime_options=lang_options,
   190→            large_threshold_override=config.get("large_files_threshold", 0),
   191→            props_threshold_override=config.get("props_threshold", 0),
   192→        ),
   193→    )
   194→
   195→    lang_capabilities = _state_lang_capabilities(state)
   196→    lang_capabilities[runtime_lang.name] = {
   197→        "fixers": sorted(runtime_lang.fixers.keys()),
   198→        "typecheck_cmd": runtime_lang.typecheck_cmd,
   199→    }
   200→    return runtime_lang
   201→
   202→
   203→def _apply_assessment_reset(payload: dict, *, source: str, now: str) -> None:
   204→    """Apply a standard reset mutation to a single subjective assessment payload.
   205→
   206→    Sets score to 0.0, stamps assessed_at/reset_by/placeholder, and strips
   207→    any cached scoring artifacts (integrity_penalty, components,
   208→    component_scores) plus provisional-override markers.
   209→    """
   210→    payload["score"] = 0.0
   211→    payload["source"] = source
   212→    payload["assessed_at"] = now
   213→    payload["reset_by"] = source
   214→    payload["placeholder"] = True
   215→    payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   216→    payload.pop("components", None)
   217→    payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   218→    payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   219→    payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   220→
   221→
   222→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   223→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   224→    *,
   225→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   226→) -> int:
   227→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   228→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   229→
   230→    reset_keys = {
   231→        key.strip()
   232→        for key in assessments
   233→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   234→    }
   235→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   236→
   237→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   238→    source = "scan_reset_subjective"
   239→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   240→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   241→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   242→            _apply_assessment_reset(payload, source=source, now=now)
   243→            continue
   244→        assessments[key] = {
   245→            "score": 0.0,
   246→            "source": source,
   247→            "assessed_at": now,
   248→            "reset_by": source,
   249→            "placeholder": True,
   250→        }
   251→    return len(reset_keys)
   252→
   253→
   254→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   255→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   256→) -> int:
   257→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   258→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   259→
   260→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   261→    source = "manual_override_expired"
   262→    expired = 0
   263→    for payload in assessments.values():
   264→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   265→            continue
   266→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   267→            continue
   268→        _apply_assessment_reset(payload, source=source, now=now)
   269→        expired += 1
   270→    return expired
   271→
   272→
   273→def prepare_scan_runtime(args: argparse.Namespace) -> ScanRuntime:
   274→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   275→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   276→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   277→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   278→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
   279→    path = Path(args.path)
   280→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   281→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   282→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   283→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   284→        state
   285→    )
   286→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   287→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   288→            state,
   289→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   290→        )
   291→
   292→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   293→    profile = resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   294→    include_slow_effective = effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   295→
   296→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   297→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   298→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   299→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   300→
   301→    return ScanRuntime(
   302→        args=args,
   303→        state_path=state_file,
   304→        state=state,
   305→        path=path,
   306→        config=config,
   307→        lang=lang,
   308→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   309→        profile=profile,
   310→        effective_include_slow=include_slow_effective,
   311→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   312→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   313→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   314→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   315→    )
   316→
   317→
   318→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_issues(
   319→    issues: list[dict[str, Any]],
   320→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   321→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   322→    """Append stale exclude issues when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   323→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   324→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   325→        return issues
   326→
   327→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   328→    stale = audit_excluded_dirs(
   329→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, get_project_root()
   330→    )
   331→    if not stale:
   332→        return issues
   333→
   334→    augmented = list(issues)
   335→    augmented.extend(stale)
   336→    for stale_issue in stale:
   337→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_issue['summary']}", "dim"))
   338→    return augmented
   339→
   340→
   341→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_issues(
   342→    issues: list[dict[str, Any]],
   343→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   344→    *,
   345→    decay_scans: int,
   346→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   347→    """Append re-triage issues for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   348→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   349→        issues,
   350→        state=runtime.state,
   351→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   352→        project_root=get_project_root(),
   353→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   354→    )
   355→
   356→
   357→def run_scan_generation(
   358→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   359→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   360→    """Run detector pipeline and return issues, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   361→    enable_file_cache()
   362→    enable_parse_cache()
   363→    try:
   364→        issues, potentials = plan_mod.generate_issues(
   365→            runtime.path,
   366→            lang=runtime.lang,
   367→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   368→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   369→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   370→                profile=runtime.profile,
   371→            ),
   372→        )
   373→    finally:
   374→        disable_parse_cache()
   375→        disable_file_cache()
   376→
   377→    codebase_metrics = collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   378→    warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   379→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   380→    )
   381→    issues = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_issues(issues, runtime)
   382→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   383→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   384→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   385→    )
   386→    issues, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_issues(
   387→        issues,
   388→        runtime,
   389→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   390→    )
   391→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   392→    return issues, potentials, codebase_metrics
   393→
   394→
   395→def merge_scan_results(
   396→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   397→    issues: list[dict[str, Any]],
   398→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   399→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   400→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   401→    """Merge issues into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   402→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   403→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   404→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   405→
   406→    if not path_changed:
   407→        prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   408→    else:
   409→        prev = state_mod.ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   410→    prev_dim_scores = (
   411→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   412→    )
   413→
   414→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   415→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   416→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   417→
   418→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   419→
   420→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   421→        runtime.state,
   422→        issues,
   423→        options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
   424→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   425→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   426→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   427→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   428→            potentials=potentials,
   429→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   430→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   431→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   432→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   433→        ),
   434→    )
   435→
   436→    expire_stale_holistic(
   437→        runtime.state, runtime.config.get("holistic_max_age_days", 30)
   438→    )
   439→    state_mod.save_state(
   440→        runtime.state,
   441→        runtime.state_path,
   442→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   443→    )
   444→
   445→    _clear_needs_rescan_flag(runtime.config)
   446→    _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime)
   447→
   448→    return ScanMergeResult(
   449→        diff=diff,
   450→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   451→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   452→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   453→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   454→        prev_dim_scores=prev_dim_scores,
   455→    )
   456→
   457→
   458→def resolve_noise_snapshot(
   459→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   460→    config: dict[str, object],
   461→) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   462→    """Resolve noise budget settings and hidden issue counters."""
   463→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   464→        state_mod.resolve_issue_noise_settings(config)
   465→    )
   466→    issues_by_id = _state_issues(state)
   467→    open_issues = [
   468→        issue
   469→        for issue in state_mod.path_scoped_issues(
   470→            issues_by_id, state.get("scan_path")
   471→        ).values()
   472→        if issue.get("status") == "open"
   473→    ]
   474→    _, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_issue_noise_budget(
   475→        open_issues,
   476→        budget=noise_budget,
   477→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   478→    )
   479→
   480→    return ScanNoiseSnapshot(
   481→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   482→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   483→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   484→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   485→        hidden_total=sum(hidden_by_detector.values()),
   486→    )
   487→
   488→
   489→def persist_reminder_history(
   490→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   491→    narrative: dict[str, object],
   492→) -> None:
   493→    """Persist reminder history emitted by narrative computation."""
   494→    if not (narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative):
   495→        return
   496→
   497→    runtime.state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   498→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   499→    state_mod.save_state(
   500→        runtime.state,
   501→        runtime.state_path,
   502→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   503→    )
   504→
   505→
   506→__all__ = [
   507→    "ScanStateContractError",
   508→    "ScanMergeResult",
   509→    "ScanNoiseSnapshot",
   510→    "ScanRuntime",
   511→    "merge_scan_results",
   512→    "persist_reminder_history",
   513→    "prepare_scan_runtime",
   514→    "resolve_noise_snapshot",
   515→    "run_scan_generation",
   516→]
   517→
```

```
1→"""Issue generation pipeline (phase execution and normalization)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import rel
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    12→from desloppify.engine.planning.helpers import is_subjective_phase
    13→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import ZONE_POLICIES, FileZoneMap
    14→from desloppify.languages import auto_detect_lang, available_langs, get_lang
    15→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import DetectorPhase, LangConfig
    16→from desloppify.languages._framework.runtime import LangRun, make_lang_run
    17→from desloppify.state import Issue
    18→
    19→
    20→@dataclass
    21→class PlanScanOptions:
    22→    """Config object for scan execution behavior."""
    23→
    24→    include_slow: bool = True
    25→    zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None
    26→    profile: str = "full"
    27→
    28→
    29→def _stderr(msg: str) -> None:
    30→    print(colorize(msg, "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    31→
    32→
    33→def _resolve_lang(
    34→    lang: LangConfig | LangRun | None, project_root: Path
    35→) -> LangConfig | LangRun:
    36→    if lang is not None:
    37→        return lang
    38→
    39→    detected = auto_detect_lang(project_root)
    40→    if detected is None:
    41→        langs = available_langs()
    42→        if not langs:
    43→            raise ValueError("No language plugins available")
    44→        detected = langs[0]
    45→    return get_lang(detected)
    46→
    47→
    48→def _build_zone_map(path: Path, lang: LangRun, zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None) -> None:
    49→    if not (lang.zone_rules and lang.file_finder):
    50→        return
    51→
    52→    files = lang.file_finder(path)
    53→    lang.zone_map = FileZoneMap(
    54→        files, lang.zone_rules, rel_fn=rel, overrides=zone_overrides
    55→    )
    56→    counts = lang.zone_map.counts()
    57→    zone_str = ", ".join(
    58→        f"{zone}: {count}" for zone, count in sorted(counts.items()) if count > 0
    59→    )
    60→    _stderr(f"  Zones: {zone_str}")
    61→
    62→    from desloppify.languages._framework.generic import capability_report
    63→
    64→    report = capability_report(lang)
    65→    if report is not None:
    66→        present, missing = report
    67→        if present:
    68→            _stderr(f"  Capabilities: {', '.join(present)}")
    69→        if missing:
    70→            _stderr(f"  Not available: {', '.join(missing)}")
    71→
    72→
    73→def _select_phases(lang: LangRun, *, include_slow: bool, profile: str) -> list[DetectorPhase]:
    74→    active_profile = profile if profile in {"objective", "full", "ci"} else "full"
    75→    phases = lang.phases
    76→    if not include_slow or active_profile == "ci":
    77→        phases = [phase for phase in phases if not phase.slow]
    78→    if active_profile in {"objective", "ci"}:
    79→        phases = [phase for phase in phases if not is_subjective_phase(phase)]
    80→    return phases
    81→
    82→
    83→def _run_phases(path: Path, lang: LangRun, phases: list[DetectorPhase]) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
    84→    issues: list[Issue] = []
    85→    all_potentials: dict[str, int] = {}
    86→
    87→    total = len(phases)
    88→    for idx, phase in enumerate(phases, start=1):
    89→        _stderr(f"  [{idx}/{total}] {phase.label}...")
    90→        phase_issues, phase_potentials = phase.run(path, lang)
    91→        all_potentials.update(phase_potentials)
    92→        issues.extend(phase_issues)
    93→
    94→    return issues, all_potentials
    95→
    96→
    97→def _stamp_issue_context(issues: list[Issue], lang: LangRun) -> None:
    98→    if not issues:
    99→        return
   100→
   101→    zone_policies = None
   102→    if lang.zone_map is not None:
   103→        zone_policies = ZONE_POLICIES
   104→
   105→    for issue in issues:
   106→        issue["lang"] = lang.name
   107→        if lang.zone_map is None:
   108→            continue
   109→
   110→        zone = lang.zone_map.get(issue.get("file", ""))
   111→        issue["zone"] = zone.value
   112→        policy = zone_policies.get(zone) if zone_policies else None
   113→        if policy and issue.get("detector") in policy.downgrade_detectors:
   114→            issue["confidence"] = "low"
   115→
   116→
   117→def _generate_issues_from_lang(
   118→    path: Path,
   119→    lang: LangRun,
   120→    *,
   121→    include_slow: bool = True,
   122→    zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None,
   123→    profile: str = "full",
   124→) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
   125→    """Run detector phases from a LangRun."""
   126→    _build_zone_map(path, lang, zone_overrides)
   127→    phases = _select_phases(lang, include_slow=include_slow, profile=profile)
   128→    issues, all_potentials = _run_phases(path, lang, phases)
   129→    _stamp_issue_context(issues, lang)
   130→    _stderr(f"\n  Total: {len(issues)} issues")
   131→    return issues, all_potentials
   132→
   133→
   134→def generate_issues(
   135→    path: Path,
   136→    lang: LangConfig | LangRun | None = None,
   137→    *,
   138→    options: PlanScanOptions | None = None,
   139→) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
   140→    """Run all detectors and convert results to normalized issues."""
   141→    resolved_options = options or PlanScanOptions()
   142→
   143→    resolved_lang = _resolve_lang(lang, get_project_root())
   144→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(resolved_lang)
   145→    return _generate_issues_from_lang(
   146→        path,
   147→        runtime_lang,
   148→        include_slow=resolved_options.include_slow,
   149→        zone_overrides=resolved_options.zone_overrides,
   150→        profile=resolved_options.profile,
   151→    )
   152→
```

```
    32→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    33→    """Return True when the living plan has any user-managed queue metadata."""
    34→    return bool(
    35→        plan.get("queue_order")
    36→        or plan.get("overrides")
    37→        or plan.get("clusters")
    38→        or plan.get("skipped")
    39→    )
    40→
    41→
    42→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, reconcile_fn) -> bool:
    43→    """Apply standard post-scan plan reconciliation when user content exists."""
    44→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
    45→        return False
    46→    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)
    47→    if recon.resurfaced:
    48→        print(
    49→            colorize(
    50→                f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
    51→                "cyan",
    52→            )
    53→        )
    54→    return bool(recon.changes)
    55→

    66→        )

    72→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)

    93→    *,
    94→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,

   106→
   107→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   108→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   109→    existing = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   110→    if existing and not isinstance(existing, dict):
   111→        return False
   112→    # Seed when empty OR when it's the reset sentinel ({"reset": True})
   113→    if existing and not existing.get("reset"):
   114→        return False
   115→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   116→    if scores.strict is None:
   117→        return False
   118→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   119→        "strict": scores.strict,
   120→        "overall": scores.overall,
   121→        "objective": scores.objective,
   122→        "verified": scores.verified,
   123→    }
   124→    # Record scan count at cycle start so gates can detect whether a new scan ran
   125→    plan["scan_count_at_plan_start"] = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   126→    return True
   127→
   128→
   129→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   130→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   131→) -> bool:
   132→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   133→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   134→        return False
   135→
   136→    try:
   137→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   138→            ScoreDisplayMode,
   139→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   140→            score_display_mode,
   141→        )
   142→
   143→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   144→        frozen_strict = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   145→        queue_empty = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict) is not ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   146→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   147→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "run post-scan plan reconciliation", exc)
   148→        return False
   149→    if not queue_empty:
   150→        return False
   151→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   152→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   153→    return True
   154→
   155→
   156→def _subjective_policy_context(
   157→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   158→    plan: dict[str, object],
   159→) -> tuple[float, object, bool]:
   160→    from desloppify.base.config import target_strict_score_from_config
   161→    from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_subjective_visibility
   162→
   163→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   164→    policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
   165→        runtime.state,
   166→        target_strict=target_strict,
   167→        plan=plan,
   168→    )
   169→    cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   170→    return target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed
   171→
   172→
   173→def _sync_unscored_and_log(
   174→    plan: dict[str, object],
   175→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   176→) -> bool:
   177→    changed = _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state, sync_unscored_dimensions)
   178→    if changed:
   179→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_unscored", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   180→    return changed
   181→
   182→
   183→def _sync_stale_and_log(
   184→    plan: dict[str, object],
   185→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   186→    *,
   187→    policy,
   188→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   189→) -> bool:
   190→    changed = _sync_stale_dimensions(
   191→        plan,
   192→        state,
   193→        lambda p, s: sync_stale_dimensions(
   194→            p,
   195→            s,
   196→            policy=policy,
   197→            cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   198→        ),
   199→    )
   200→    if changed:
   201→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_stale", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   202→    return changed
   203→
   204→
   205→def _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   206→    plan: dict[str, object],
   207→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   208→    *,
   209→    target_strict: float,
   210→    policy,
   211→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   212→) -> bool:
   213→    changed = _sync_auto_clusters(
   214→        plan,
   215→        state,
   216→        target_strict=target_strict,
   217→        policy=policy,
   218→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   219→    )
   220→    if changed:
   221→        append_log_entry(plan, "auto_cluster", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   222→    return changed
   223→
   224→
   225→def _sync_triage_and_log(
   226→    plan: dict[str, object],
   227→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   228→) -> bool:
   229→    triage_sync = sync_triage_needed(plan, state)
   230→    if not triage_sync.changes:
   231→        return False
   232→    if triage_sync.injected:
   233→        print(
   234→            colorize(
   235→                "  Plan: planning mode needed — review issues changed since last triage.",
   236→                "cyan",
   237→            )
   238→        )
   239→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_triage", actor="system", detail={"injected": True})
   240→    return True
   241→
   242→
   243→def _sync_communicate_score_and_log(
   244→    plan: dict[str, object],
   245→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   246→    *,
   247→    policy,
   248→) -> bool:
   249→    communicate_sync = sync_communicate_score_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   250→    if not communicate_sync.changes:
   251→        return False
   252→    append_log_entry(
   253→        plan,
   254→        "sync_communicate_score",
   255→        actor="system",
   256→        detail={"injected": True},
   257→    )
   258→    return True
   259→
   260→
   261→def _sync_create_plan_and_log(
   262→    plan: dict[str, object],
   263→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   264→    *,
   265→    policy,
   266→) -> bool:
   267→    create_plan_sync = sync_create_plan_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   268→    if not create_plan_sync.changes:
   269→        return False
   270→    if create_plan_sync.injected:
   271→        print(
   272→            colorize(
   273→                "  Plan: reviews complete — `workflow::create-plan` queued.",
   274→                "cyan",
   275→            )
   276→        )
   277→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_create_plan", actor="system", detail={"injected": True})
   278→    return True
   279→
   280→
   281→def _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(
   282→    plan: dict[str, object],
   283→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   284→) -> bool:
   285→    seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, state)
   286→    if seeded:
   287→        append_log_entry(plan, "seed_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   288→        return True
   289→    # Only clear scores that existed before this reconcile pass —
   290→    # never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan.
   291→    cleared = _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(state, plan)
   292→    if cleared:
   293→        append_log_entry(plan, "clear_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   294→    return cleared
   295→
   296→
   297→def reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: Any) -> None:
   298→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   299→    plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   300→    try:
   301→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   302→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   303→        logger.warning("Plan reconciliation skipped (load failed): %s", exc)
   304→        return
   305→    dirty = False
   306→
   307→    if _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, runtime.state, reconcile_plan_after_scan):
   308→        dirty = True
   309→
   310→    if _sync_unscored_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   311→        dirty = True
   312→
   313→    target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed = _subjective_policy_context(
   314→        runtime,
   315→        plan,
   316→    )
   317→    if _sync_stale_and_log(
   318→        plan,
   319→        runtime.state,
   320→        policy=policy,
   321→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   322→    ):
   323→        dirty = True
   324→
   325→    if _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   326→        plan,
   327→        runtime.state,
   328→        target_strict=target_strict,
   329→        policy=policy,
   330→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   331→    ):
   332→        dirty = True
   333→
   334→    if _sync_communicate_score_and_log(plan, runtime.state, policy=policy):
   335→        dirty = True
   336→    if _sync_create_plan_and_log(plan, runtime.state, policy=policy):
   337→        dirty = True
   338→    if _sync_triage_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   339→        dirty = True
   340→    if _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
   341→        dirty = True
   342→
   343→    if dirty:
   344→        try:
   345→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   346→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   347→            logger.warning("Plan reconciliation save failed: %s", exc)
   348→
```

```
   171→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   172→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   173→    config: dict[str, object],
   174→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   175→    lang: LangRun | None,
   176→) -> LangRun | None:
   177→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   178→    if not lang:

   180→
   181→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   182→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   183→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   184→        lang,
   185→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   186→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   187→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   188→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,

   190→            large_threshold_override=config.get("large_files_threshold", 0),
   191→            props_threshold_override=config.get("props_threshold", 0),
   192→        ),
   193→    )
   194→
   195→    lang_capabilities = _state_lang_capabilities(state)
   196→    lang_capabilities[runtime_lang.name] = {
   197→        "fixers": sorted(runtime_lang.fixers.keys()),
   198→        "typecheck_cmd": runtime_lang.typecheck_cmd,
   199→    }
   200→    return runtime_lang
   201→
   202→
   203→def _apply_assessment_reset(payload: dict, *, source: str, now: str) -> None:
   204→    """Apply a standard reset mutation to a single subjective assessment payload.
   205→
   206→    Sets score to 0.0, stamps assessed_at/reset_by/placeholder, and strips
   207→    any cached scoring artifacts (integrity_penalty, components,
   208→    component_scores) plus provisional-override markers.
   209→    """
   210→    payload["score"] = 0.0
   211→    payload["source"] = source
   212→    payload["assessed_at"] = now
   213→    payload["reset_by"] = source
   214→    payload["placeholder"] = True
   215→    payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   216→    payload.pop("components", None)
   217→    payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   218→    payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   219→    payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   220→
   221→
   222→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   223→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   224→    *,
   225→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   226→) -> int:
   227→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   228→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   229→
   230→    reset_keys = {
   231→        key.strip()
   232→        for key in assessments
   233→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   234→    }
   235→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   236→
   237→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   238→    source = "scan_reset_subjective"
   239→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   240→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   241→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   242→            _apply_assessment_reset(payload, source=source, now=now)
   243→            continue
   244→        assessments[key] = {
   245→            "score": 0.0,
   246→            "source": source,
   247→            "assessed_at": now,
   248→            "reset_by": source,
   249→            "placeholder": True,
   250→        }
   251→    return len(reset_keys)
   252→
   253→
   254→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   255→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   256→) -> int:
   257→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   258→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   259→
   260→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   261→    source = "manual_override_expired"
   262→    expired = 0
   263→    for payload in assessments.values():
   264→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   265→            continue
   266→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   267→            continue
   268→        _apply_assessment_reset(payload, source=source, now=now)
   269→        expired += 1
   270→    return expired
   271→

   273→def prepare_scan_runtime(args: argparse.Namespace) -> ScanRuntime:
   274→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   275→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   276→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   277→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   278→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
   279→    path = Path(args.path)
   280→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   281→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   282→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   283→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   284→        state
   285→    )
   286→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   287→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   288→            state,
   289→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   290→        )
   291→
   292→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   293→    profile = resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   294→    include_slow_effective = effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   295→
   296→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   297→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   298→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   299→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   300→
   301→    return ScanRuntime(
   302→        args=args,
   303→        state_path=state_file,
   304→        state=state,
   305→        path=path,
   306→        config=config,
   307→        lang=lang,
   308→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   309→        profile=profile,
   310→        effective_include_slow=include_slow_effective,
   311→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   312→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   313→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   314→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   315→    )
   316→
   317→
   318→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_issues(
   319→    issues: list[dict[str, Any]],
   320→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   321→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   322→    """Append stale exclude issues when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   323→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   324→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   325→        return issues
   326→
   327→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   328→    stale = audit_excluded_dirs(
   329→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, get_project_root()
   330→    )
   331→    if not stale:
   332→        return issues
   333→
   334→    augmented = list(issues)
   335→    augmented.extend(stale)
   336→    for stale_issue in stale:
   337→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_issue['summary']}", "dim"))
   338→    return augmented
   339→
   340→
   341→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_issues(
   342→    issues: list[dict[str, Any]],
   343→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   344→    *,
   345→    decay_scans: int,
   346→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   347→    """Append re-triage issues for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   348→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   349→        issues,
   350→        state=runtime.state,
   351→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   352→        project_root=get_project_root(),
   353→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   354→    )
   355→
   356→
   357→def run_scan_generation(
   358→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   359→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   360→    """Run detector pipeline and return issues, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   361→    enable_file_cache()
   362→    enable_parse_cache()
   363→    try:
   364→        issues, potentials = plan_mod.generate_issues(
   365→            runtime.path,
   366→            lang=runtime.lang,
   367→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   368→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   369→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   370→                profile=runtime.profile,
   371→            ),
   372→        )
   373→    finally:
   374→        disable_parse_cache()
   375→        disable_file_cache()
   376→
   377→    codebase_metrics = collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   378→    warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   379→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   380→    )
   381→    issues = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_issues(issues, runtime)
   382→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   383→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   384→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   385→    )
   386→    issues, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_issues(
   387→        issues,
   388→        runtime,
   389→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   390→    )
   391→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   392→    return issues, potentials, codebase_metrics
   393→
   394→
   395→def merge_scan_results(
   396→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   397→    issues: list[dict[str, Any]],
   398→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   399→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   400→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   401→    """Merge issues into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   402→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   403→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   404→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   405→
   406→    if not path_changed:
   407→        prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   408→    else:
   409→        prev = state_mod.ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   410→    prev_dim_scores = (
   411→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   412→    )
   413→
   414→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   415→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   416→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   417→
   418→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   419→
   420→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   421→        runtime.state,
   422→        issues,
   423→        options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
   424→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   425→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   426→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   427→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   428→            potentials=potentials,
   429→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   430→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   431→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   432→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   433→        ),
   434→    )
   435→
   436→    expire_stale_holistic(
   437→        runtime.state, runtime.config.get("holistic_max_age_days", 30)
   438→    )
   439→    state_mod.save_state(
   440→        runtime.state,
   441→        runtime.state_path,
   442→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   443→    )
   444→
   445→    _clear_needs_rescan_flag(runtime.config)
   446→    _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime)
   447→
   448→    return ScanMergeResult(
   449→        diff=diff,
   450→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   451→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   452→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   453→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   454→        prev_dim_scores=prev_dim_scores,
   455→    )
   456→
   457→
   458→def resolve_noise_snapshot(
   459→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   460→    config: dict[str, object],
   461→) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   462→    """Resolve noise budget settings and hidden issue counters."""
   463→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   464→        state_mod.resolve_issue_noise_settings(config)
   465→    )
   466→    issues_by_id = _state_issues(state)
   467→    open_issues = [
   468→        issue
   469→        for issue in state_mod.path_scoped_issues(
   470→            issues_by_id, state.get("scan_path")
   471→        ).values()
   472→        if issue.get("status") == "open"
   473→    ]
   474→    _, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_issue_noise_budget(
   475→        open_issues,
   476→        budget=noise_budget,
   477→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   478→    )
   479→
   480→    return ScanNoiseSnapshot(
   481→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   482→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   483→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   484→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   485→        hidden_total=sum(hidden_by_detector.values()),
   486→    )
   487→
   488→
   489→def persist_reminder_history(
   490→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   491→    narrative: dict[str, object],
   492→) -> None:
   493→    """Persist reminder history emitted by narrative computation."""
   494→    if not (narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative):
   495→        return
   496→
   497→    runtime.state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   498→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   499→    state_mod.save_state(
   500→        runtime.state,
   501→        runtime.state_path,
   502→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   503→    )
   504→
   505→
   506→__all__ = [
   507→    "ScanStateContractError",
   508→    "ScanMergeResult",
   509→    "ScanNoiseSnapshot",
   510→    "ScanRuntime",
   511→    "merge_scan_results",
   512→    "persist_reminder_history",
   513→    "prepare_scan_runtime",
   514→    "resolve_noise_snapshot",
   515→    "run_scan_generation",
   516→]
   517→
```

```
1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import enum
     6→import importlib
     7→import logging
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
    10→
    11→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    12→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    13→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    14→from desloppify.engine import plan as plan_mod
    15→from desloppify.engine._work_queue import core as work_queue_core_mod
    16→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import is_subjective_queue_item
    17→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
    18→
    19→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    20→
    21→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    22→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import QueueContext
    23→
    24→
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→# QueueBreakdown — single source of truth for queue numbers
    27→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    28→
    29→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    30→class QueueBreakdown:
    31→    """All numbers needed to render the standardized queue display."""
    32→
    33→    queue_total: int = 0
    34→    plan_ordered: int = 0
    35→    skipped: int = 0
    36→    subjective: int = 0
    37→    workflow: int = 0
    38→    focus_cluster: str | None = None
    39→    focus_cluster_count: int = 0
    40→    focus_cluster_total: int = 0
    41→
    42→    @property
    43→    def objective_actionable(self) -> int:
    44→        """Objective items only — excludes subjective and workflow.
    45→
    46→        This is the count used by :func:`score_display_mode` to decide
    47→        frozen vs live display.  Do not use it directly for gating —
    48→        use ``score_display_mode()`` instead.
    49→        """
    50→        return max(0, self.queue_total - self.subjective - self.workflow)
    51→
    52→
    53→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54→# ScoreDisplayMode — single decision point for frozen vs live score display
    55→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    56→
    57→class ScoreDisplayMode(enum.Enum):
    58→    """How to display the score given the current plan cycle and queue state.
    59→
    60→    - ``FROZEN``: objective work remains → show frozen plan-start score.
    61→    - ``PHASE_TRANSITION``: objective drained, subjective/workflow remain
    62→      → show live scores + transition banner.
    63→    - ``LIVE``: no active plan cycle or queue fully clear → show live scores.
    64→    """
    65→    FROZEN = "frozen"
    66→    PHASE_TRANSITION = "phase_transition"
    67→    LIVE = "live"
    68→
    69→
    70→def score_display_mode(
    71→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None,
    72→    plan_start_strict: float | None,
    73→) -> ScoreDisplayMode:
    74→    """Determine the score display mode from breakdown + plan-start score.
    75→
    76→    This is the **single source of truth** for the frozen-vs-live decision.
    77→    Every call site (status, scan reveal, next nudges, plan reconcile, plan
    78→    nudge) must use this instead of ad-hoc count checks.
    79→    """
    80→    if plan_start_strict is None:
    81→        return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
    82→    if breakdown is None:
    83→        return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
    84→    if breakdown.objective_actionable > 0:
    85→        return ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
    86→    if breakdown.queue_total > 0:
    87→        return ScoreDisplayMode.PHASE_TRANSITION
    88→    return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
    89→
    90→
    91→def plan_aware_queue_breakdown(
    92→    state: dict,
    93→    plan: dict | None = None,
    94→    *,
    95→    context: QueueContext | None = None,
    96→) -> QueueBreakdown:
    97→    """Build a full :class:`QueueBreakdown` from a single ``build_work_queue`` call.
    98→
    99→    When *context* is provided, its ``plan`` and ``policy`` are forwarded to
   100→    ``build_work_queue`` so the counts agree with the caller's resolution.
   101→    """
   102→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else plan
   103→
   104→    result = work_queue_core_mod.build_work_queue(
   105→        state,
   106→        options=work_queue_core_mod.QueueBuildOptions(
   107→            status="open",
   108→            count=None,
   109→            plan=effective_plan if context is None else None,
   110→            include_skipped=False,
   111→            context=context,
   112→        ),
   113→    )
   114→
   115→    # Collapse clusters for display-level counting
   116→    items = result.get("items", [])
   117→    if effective_plan and not effective_plan.get("active_cluster"):
   118→        items = collapse_clusters(items, effective_plan)
   119→
   120→    queue_total = len(items)
   121→
   122→    # Count subjective and workflow items in the queue.
   123→    # Collapsed clusters whose members are all subjective count as subjective.
   124→    subjective = sum(
   125→        1 for item in items
   126→        if is_subjective_queue_item(item)
   127→    )
   128→    workflow = sum(
   129→        1 for item in items
   130→        if item.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
   131→    )
   132→
   133→    # Plan-derived counts
   134→    plan_ordered = 0
   135→    skipped = 0
   136→    if effective_plan:
   137→        skipped = len(effective_plan.get("skipped", {}))
   138→        # plan_ordered = items that are in queue_order minus skipped
   139→        queue_order = effective_plan.get("queue_order", [])
   140→        skipped_ids = set(effective_plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   141→        plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
   142→
   143→    # Focus cluster info
   144→    focus_cluster = None
   145→    focus_cluster_count = 0
   146→    focus_cluster_total = 0
   147→    if effective_plan:
   148→        active = effective_plan.get("active_cluster")
   149→        if active:
   150→            focus_cluster = active
   151→            cluster_data = effective_plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
   152→            focus_cluster_total = len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   153→            # Count how many cluster members are still in the queue
   154→            cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   155→            open_issues = {
   156→                fid
   157→                for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
   158→                if f.get("status") == "open"
   159→            }
   160→            focus_cluster_count = len(cluster_member_ids & open_issues)
   161→
   162→    return QueueBreakdown(
   163→        queue_total=queue_total,
   164→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
   165→        skipped=skipped,
   166→        subjective=subjective,
   167→        workflow=workflow,
   168→        focus_cluster=focus_cluster,
   169→        focus_cluster_count=focus_cluster_count,
   170→        focus_cluster_total=focus_cluster_total,
   171→    )
   172→
   173→
   174→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   175→# Formatting helpers — single source of truth for queue display
   176→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   177→
   178→def format_plan_delta(live: float, frozen: float) -> str:
   179→    """Format plan-start vs live delta, or '' if below threshold."""
   180→    if abs(live - frozen) < 0.05:
   181→        return ""
   182→    delta = round(live - frozen, 1)
   183→    return f"{'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f}"
   184→
   185→
   186→def format_queue_headline(breakdown: QueueBreakdown) -> str:
   187→    """The one-line Queue summary. Same format everywhere.
   188→
   189→    Examples::
   190→
   191→        Queue: 1934 items (292 planned · 23 skipped)
   192→        Queue: 1934 items
   193→    """
   194→    n = breakdown.queue_total
   195→    label = f"Queue: {n} item{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   196→
   197→    # Parenthesized segments
   198→    segments: list[str] = []
   199→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   200→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.workflow} planning step{'s' if breakdown.workflow != 1 else ''}")
   201→    if breakdown.plan_ordered > 0:
   202→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.plan_ordered} planned")
   203→    if breakdown.skipped > 0:
   204→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.skipped} skipped")
   205→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   206→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.subjective} subjective")
   207→    if segments:
   208→        sep = " \u00b7 "
   209→        detail = sep.join(segments)
   210→        return f"{label} ({detail})"
   211→    return label
   212→
   213→
   214→def format_queue_block(
   215→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   216→    *,
   217→    frozen_score: float | None = None,
   218→    live_score: float | None = None,
   219→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   220→    """Full queue block: focus banner + queue line + contextual hints.
   221→
   222→    Returns a list of ``(text, style)`` pairs ready for ``colorize()``.
   223→    """
   224→    lines: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
   225→
   226→    # Focus banner (prominent, separate)
   227→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   228→        focus_line = (
   229→            f"  Focus: `{breakdown.focus_cluster}` "
   230→            f"\u2014 {breakdown.focus_cluster_count}/{breakdown.focus_cluster_total}"
   231→            f" items remaining"
   232→        )
   233→        lines.append((focus_line, "cyan"))
   234→
   235→    # Score line: show both frozen plan-start and live score when available
   236→    if frozen_score is not None:
   237→        delta_str = format_plan_delta(live_score, frozen_score) if live_score is not None else ""
   238→        if delta_str:
   239→            lines.append((
   240→                f"  Score: strict {live_score:.1f}/100 (plan start: {frozen_score:.1f}, {delta_str})",
   241→                "cyan",
   242→            ))
   243→        else:
   244→            lines.append((
   245→                f"  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {frozen_score:.1f}/100",
   246→                "cyan",
   247→            ))
   248→
   249→    # Queue headline — always the same
   250→    lines.append((f"  {format_queue_headline(breakdown)}", "bold"))
   251→
   252→    # Contextual hints (dim)
   253→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   254→        lines.append((
   255→            f"  Unfocus: `desloppify plan focus --clear`"
   256→            f" \u00b7 Cluster details: `desloppify next --cluster {breakdown.focus_cluster} --count 10`",
   257→            "dim",
   258→        ))
   259→    elif breakdown.plan_ordered > 0 or breakdown.skipped > 0:
   260→        lines.append((
   261→            "  Details: `desloppify plan queue`"
   262→            " \u00b7 Skip: `desloppify plan skip <id>`",
   263→            "dim",
   264→        ))
   265→    else:
   266→        lines.append((
   267→            "  Start planning: `desloppify plan`",
   268→            "dim",
   269→        ))
   270→
   271→    return lines
   272→
   273→
   274→def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
   275→    """Extract the frozen plan-start strict score, or None if unset."""
   276→    if not plan:
   277→        return None
   278→    return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   279→
   280→
   281→def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   282→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   283→    *,
   284→    frozen_strict: float,
   285→    live_score: float | None = None,
   286→) -> None:
   287→    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress.
   288→
   289→    Only call from a ``ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN`` path — assumes objective
   290→    work remains in the queue.
   291→    """
   292→    block = format_queue_block(breakdown, frozen_score=frozen_strict, live_score=live_score)
   293→    print()
   294→    for text, style in block:
   295→        print(colorize(text, style))
   296→    print(colorize(
   297→        "  Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   298→        "dim",
   299→    ))
   300→
   301→
   302→def print_objective_drained_banner(
   303→    frozen_strict: float,
   304→    remaining: int,
   305→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   306→) -> None:
   307→    """Show a phase-transition banner when objective work is drained."""
   308→    kind_labels: list[str] = []
   309→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   310→        kind_labels.append("subjective")
   311→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   312→        kind_labels.append("workflow")
   313→    kind_desc = " + ".join(kind_labels) if kind_labels else "non-objective"
   314→    print(colorize(
   315→        f"\n  Objective queue complete (plan-start was {frozen_strict:.1f})."
   316→        f" {remaining} {kind_desc} item{'s' if remaining != 1 else ''} remain.",
   317→        "cyan",
   318→    ))
   319→    print(colorize(
   320→        "  Run `desloppify next` for remaining work,"
   321→        " then `desloppify scan` to finalize.",
   322→        "dim",
   323→    ))
   324→
   325→
   326→def print_execution_or_reveal(
   327→    state: dict,
   328→    prev,
   329→    plan: dict | None,
   330→) -> None:
   331→    """Context-aware score display using :func:`score_display_mode`."""
   332→    frozen_strict = get_plan_start_strict(plan)
   333→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None
   334→
   335→    if frozen_strict is not None:
   336→        try:
   337→            breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   338→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   339→            _logger.debug("queue breakdown computation skipped", exc_info=True)
   340→
   341→    mode = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict)
   342→
   343→    if mode is ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN:
   344→        print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   345→            breakdown,
   346→            frozen_strict=frozen_strict,
   347→            live_score=state_mod.score_snapshot(state).strict,
   348→        )
   349→        return
   350→
   351→    # LIVE or PHASE_TRANSITION: show current scores
   352→    score_update_mod = importlib.import_module(
   353→        "desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update"
   354→    )
   355→    score_update_mod.print_score_update(state, prev)
   356→
   357→    if mode is ScoreDisplayMode.PHASE_TRANSITION:
   358→        print_objective_drained_banner(frozen_strict, breakdown.queue_total, breakdown)
   359→
   360→
   361→def show_score_with_plan_context(state: dict, prev) -> None:
   362→    """Load plan (best-effort) and show frozen or live score context.
   363→
   364→    Encapsulates the common load_plan + PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS + reveal
   365→    choreography so command modules don't each repeat it.
   366→    """
   367→    try:
   368→        plan = plan_mod.load_plan()
   369→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   370→        plan = None
   371→    print_execution_or_reveal(state, prev, plan)
   372→
   373→
   374→__all__ = [
   375→    "QueueBreakdown",
```

```
1→"""Scoring policies, detector mappings, and shared constants."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import Literal
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.enums import Tier
     9→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.base.scoring_constants import (
    11→    CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS,
    12→    HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import EXCLUDED_ZONE_VALUES
    15→
    16→ScoreMode = Literal["lenient", "strict", "verified_strict"]
    17→SCORING_MODES: tuple[ScoreMode, ...] = ("lenient", "strict", "verified_strict")
    18→
    19→
    20→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    21→class Dimension:
    22→    name: str
    23→    tier: int
    24→    detectors: list[str]
    25→
    26→
    27→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    28→class DetectorScoringPolicy:
    29→    detector: str
    30→    dimension: str | None
    31→    tier: int | None
    32→    file_based: bool = False
    33→    use_loc_weight: bool = False
    34→    excluded_zones: frozenset[str] = frozenset(EXCLUDED_ZONE_VALUES)
    35→
    36→
    37→# Security issues are excluded in non-production zones.
    38→SECURITY_EXCLUDED_ZONES = frozenset({"test", "config", "generated", "vendor"})
    39→_DEFAULT_EXCLUDED_ZONES = frozenset(EXCLUDED_ZONE_VALUES)
    40→
    41→# Non-objective detectors are tracked in state/queue but excluded from
    42→# mechanical dimension scoring.
    43→_NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS = frozenset(
    44→    {
    45→        "concerns",
    46→        "review",
    47→        "uncalled_functions",
    48→        "unused_enums",
    49→        "signature",
    50→        "stale_wontfix",
    51→    }
    52→)
    53→
    54→# Keep policy details that are independent of tier/dimension wiring.
    55→_FILE_BASED_POLICY_DETECTORS = frozenset(
    56→    {"smells", "dict_keys", "test_coverage", "subjective_review", "security", "concerns", "review"}
    57→)
    58→_LOC_WEIGHT_POLICY_DETECTORS = frozenset({"test_coverage"})
    59→_EXCLUDED_ZONE_OVERRIDES: dict[str, frozenset[str]] = {
    60→    "security": SECURITY_EXCLUDED_ZONES,
    61→}
    62→
    63→
    64→def _build_builtin_detector_scoring_policies() -> dict[str, DetectorScoringPolicy]:
    65→    """Build baseline scoring policies from DetectorMeta plus policy overrides."""
    66→    policies: dict[str, DetectorScoringPolicy] = {}
    67→    for detector, meta in DETECTORS.items():
    68→        if detector in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS:
    69→            dimension: str | None = None
    70→            tier: int | None = None
    71→        else:
    72→            dimension = meta.dimension
    73→            tier = meta.tier
    74→
    75→        policies[detector] = DetectorScoringPolicy(
    76→            detector=detector,
    77→            dimension=dimension,
    78→            tier=tier,
    79→            file_based=detector in _FILE_BASED_POLICY_DETECTORS,
    80→            use_loc_weight=detector in _LOC_WEIGHT_POLICY_DETECTORS,
    81→            excluded_zones=_EXCLUDED_ZONE_OVERRIDES.get(
    82→                detector,
    83→                _DEFAULT_EXCLUDED_ZONES,
    84→            ),
    85→        )
    86→    return policies
    87→
    88→
    89→# Central scoring policy for each detector: tier/dimension come from registry,
    90→# while file-based and zone behavior are preserved via local overrides.
    91→DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES: dict[str, DetectorScoringPolicy] = (
    92→    _build_builtin_detector_scoring_policies()
    93→)
    94→_BASE_DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES: dict[str, DetectorScoringPolicy] = dict(
    95→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES
    96→)
    97→
    98→# Detectors where potential = file count but issues are per-(file, sub-type).
    99→# Per-file weighted failures are capped at 1.0 to match the file-based denominator.
   100→FILE_BASED_DETECTORS = {
   101→    detector
   102→    for detector, policy in DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.items()
   103→    if policy.file_based
   104→}
   105→
   106→
   107→def _build_dimensions() -> list[Dimension]:
   108→    """Derive dimensions from DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.
   109→
   110→    Each unique (dimension, tier) pair becomes a Dimension, with its detectors
   111→    collected automatically. Order follows first-seen in the policies dict.
   112→    """
   113→    # Collect (dimension_name -> tier) preserving first-seen order,
   114→    # and group detectors by dimension.
   115→    dim_tiers: dict[str, int] = {}
   116→    grouped: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   117→    for detector, policy in DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.items():
   118→        if policy.dimension is None or policy.tier is None:
   119→            continue
   120→        if policy.dimension not in dim_tiers:
   121→            dim_tiers[policy.dimension] = policy.tier
   122→            grouped[policy.dimension] = []
   123→        grouped[policy.dimension].append(detector)
   124→    return [
   125→        Dimension(name=name, tier=tier, detectors=grouped[name])
   126→        for name, tier in dim_tiers.items()
   127→    ]
   128→
   129→
   130→DIMENSIONS = _build_dimensions()
   131→DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME = {d.name: d for d in DIMENSIONS}
   132→
   133→TIER_WEIGHTS = {
   134→    Tier.AUTO_FIX: 1,
   135→    Tier.QUICK_FIX: 2,
   136→    Tier.JUDGMENT: 3,
   137→    Tier.MAJOR_REFACTOR: 4,
   138→}
   139→# Minimum checks for full dimension weight — below this, weight is dampened
   140→# proportionally. Prevents small-sample dimensions from swinging the overall score.
   141→MIN_SAMPLE = 200
   142→HOLISTIC_POTENTIAL = 10
   143→
   144→# Budget: subjective dimensions get this fraction of the overall score.
   145→# Mechanical dimensions get the remainder.
   146→SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION = 0.60
   147→MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION = 1.0 - SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   148→
   149→# Per-dimension weighting within the mechanical pool.
   150→# Keep this balanced: no special boost for security/test in the pool itself.
```

```
    24→class TestMatchPattern:
    25→    """Tests for the _match_pattern helper."""
    26→
    27→    def test_directory_pattern_matches(self):
    28→        """'/tests/' matches a file inside a tests directory."""
    29→        assert _match_pattern("src/tests/foo.py", "/tests/") is True
    30→
    31→    def test_directory_pattern_at_root(self):
    32→        """'/tests/' matches a file at the root-level tests dir."""
    33→        assert _match_pattern("tests/foo.py", "/tests/") is True
    34→
    35→    def test_directory_pattern_no_false_positive(self):
    36→        """'/tests/' does NOT match 'attestation.py' (substring only)."""
    37→        assert _match_pattern("attestation.py", "/tests/") is False
    38→
    39→    def test_directory_pattern_nested(self):
    40→        """'/vendor/' matches a deeply nested vendor directory."""
    41→        assert _match_pattern("lib/vendor/pkg/file.js", "/vendor/") is True
    42→
    43→    def test_directory_pattern_must_be_component(self):
    44→        """'/test/' does not match 'testimony/foo.py' — needs directory boundary."""
    45→        assert _match_pattern("testimony/foo.py", "/test/") is False
    46→
    47→    def test_suffix_extension_pattern(self):
    48→        """'.test.' matches 'foo.test.ts' via contains on basename."""
    49→        assert _match_pattern("src/foo.test.ts", ".test.") is True
    50→
    51→    def test_suffix_extension_pattern_tsx(self):
    52→        """'.test.' matches 'Component.test.tsx'."""
    53→        assert _match_pattern("components/Component.test.tsx", ".test.") is True
    54→
    55→    def test_suffix_extension_pattern_no_match(self):
    56→        """'.test.' does NOT match 'test_utils.py' (no '.test.' substring)."""
    57→        assert _match_pattern("test_utils.py", ".test.") is False
    58→
    59→    def test_dot_extension_pattern(self):
    60→        """'.d.ts' matches 'types.d.ts' via contains on basename."""
    61→        assert _match_pattern("src/types.d.ts", ".d.ts") is True
    62→
    63→    def test_dot_extension_no_match(self):
    64→        """'.d.ts' does NOT match 'data.ts'."""
    65→        assert _match_pattern("src/data.ts", ".d.ts") is False
    66→
    67→    def test_prefix_pattern_matches(self):
    68→        """'test_' matches 'test_foo.py' — basename starts with prefix."""
    69→        assert _match_pattern("test_foo.py", "test_") is True
    70→
    71→    def test_prefix_pattern_with_directory(self):
    72→        """'test_' matches 'src/test_bar.py'."""
    73→        assert _match_pattern("src/test_bar.py", "test_") is True
    74→
    75→    def test_prefix_pattern_no_match(self):
    76→        """'test_' does NOT match 'contest_results.py' — no prefix match."""
    77→        assert _match_pattern("contest_results.py", "test_") is False
    78→
    79→    def test_suffix_underscore_pattern(self):
    80→        """'_test.py' matches 'foo_test.py' — basename ends with suffix."""
    81→        assert _match_pattern("foo_test.py", "_test.py") is True
    82→
    83→    def test_suffix_underscore_pattern_with_dir(self):
    84→        """'_test.py' matches 'src/bar_test.py'."""
    85→        assert _match_pattern("src/bar_test.py", "_test.py") is True
    86→
    87→    def test_suffix_underscore_no_match(self):
    88→        """'_test.py' does NOT match 'test_foo.py'."""
    89→        assert _match_pattern("test_foo.py", "_test.py") is False
    90→
    91→    def test_suffix_pb2(self):
    92→        """'_pb2.py' matches 'message_pb2.py'."""
    93→        assert _match_pattern("protos/message_pb2.py", "_pb2.py") is True
    94→
    95→    def test_exact_basename_match(self):
    96→        """'config.py' matches 'src/config.py' — exact basename."""
    97→        assert _match_pattern("src/config.py", "config.py") is True
    98→
    99→    def test_exact_basename_no_match(self):
   100→        """'config.py' does NOT match 'src/myconfig.py'."""
   101→        assert _match_pattern("src/myconfig.py", "config.py") is False
   102→
   103→    def test_exact_basename_conftest(self):
   104→        """'conftest.py' matches 'tests/conftest.py'."""
   105→        assert _match_pattern("tests/conftest.py", "conftest.py") is True
   106→
   107→    def test_exact_basename_setup(self):
   108→        """'setup.py' matches 'setup.py' at root."""
   109→        assert _match_pattern("setup.py", "setup.py") is True
   110→
   111→    def test_fallback_substring_match(self):
   112→        """Fallback substring matches on full path (e.g. 'tsconfig')."""
   113→        assert _match_pattern("project/tsconfig.json", "tsconfig") is True
   114→
   115→    def test_fallback_substring_no_match(self):
   116→        """Fallback substring does NOT match unrelated paths."""
   117→        assert _match_pattern("src/app.ts", "tsconfig") is False
   118→
   119→    def test_fallback_vite_config(self):
   120→        """'vite.config' matches 'vite.config.ts' via fallback substring."""
   121→        assert _match_pattern("vite.config.ts", "vite.config") is True

   124→# ── classify_file() ──────────────────────────────────────────

   126→
   127→class TestClassifyFile:
   128→    """Tests for the classify_file function."""
   129→
   130→    def test_vendor_directory(self):
   131→        """File in vendor/ is classified as VENDOR."""
   132→        assert classify_file("lib/vendor/pkg.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES) == Zone.VENDOR
   133→
   134→    def test_third_party_directory(self):
   135→        """File in third_party/ is classified as VENDOR."""
   136→        assert classify_file("third_party/lib.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES) == Zone.VENDOR
   137→
   138→    def test_vendored_directory(self):
   139→        """File in vendored/ is classified as VENDOR."""
   140→        assert classify_file("vendored/dep.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES) == Zone.VENDOR
   141→
   142→    def test_generated_directory(self):
   143→        """File in generated/ is classified as GENERATED."""
   144→        assert (
   145→            classify_file("src/generated/types.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES) == Zone.GENERATED
   146→        )
   147→
   148→    def test_dunder_generated_directory(self):
   149→        """File in __generated__/ is classified as GENERATED."""
   150→        assert (
   151→            classify_file("__generated__/schema.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES)
   152→            == Zone.GENERATED
   153→        )
   154→
   155→    def test_tests_directory(self):
   156→        """File in tests/ is classified as TEST."""
   157→        assert classify_file("tests/test_foo.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES) == Zone.TEST
   158→
   159→    def test_test_directory(self):
   160→        """File in test/ is classified as TEST."""
   161→        assert classify_file("test/test_bar.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES) == Zone.TEST
   162→
   163→    def test_fixtures_directory(self):
   164→        """File in fixtures/ is classified as TEST."""
   165→        assert classify_file("fixtures/data.json", COMMON_ZONE_RULES) == Zone.TEST
   166→
   167→    def test_scripts_directory(self):
   168→        """File in scripts/ is classified as SCRIPT."""
   169→        assert classify_file("scripts/deploy.sh", COMMON_ZONE_RULES) == Zone.SCRIPT
   170→
   171→    def test_bin_directory(self):
   172→        """File in bin/ is classified as SCRIPT."""
   173→        assert classify_file("bin/run.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES) == Zone.SCRIPT
   174→
   175→    def test_production_default(self):
   176→        """File not matching any rule defaults to PRODUCTION."""
   177→        assert classify_file("src/app.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES) == Zone.PRODUCTION
   178→
   179→    def test_production_unknown_path(self):
   180→        """Completely unrecognized path defaults to PRODUCTION."""
   181→        assert (
   182→            classify_file("lib/utils/helpers.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES) == Zone.PRODUCTION
   183→        )
   184→
   185→    def test_first_matching_rule_wins(self):
   186→        """VENDOR comes before TEST in COMMON_ZONE_RULES, so vendor wins."""
   187→        # A file in vendor/tests/ should be VENDOR (first match)
   188→        assert classify_file("vendor/tests/test_x.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES) == Zone.VENDOR
   189→
   190→    def test_override_takes_priority(self):
   191→        """Override value takes priority over rule-based classification."""
   192→        overrides = {"src/app.py": "test"}
   193→        assert classify_file("src/app.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES, overrides) == Zone.TEST
   194→
   195→    def test_override_on_vendor_file(self):
   196→        """Override can reclassify a vendor file as production."""
   197→        overrides = {"vendor/special.py": "production"}
   198→        assert (
   199→            classify_file("vendor/special.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES, overrides)
   200→            == Zone.PRODUCTION
   201→        )
   202→
   203→    def test_override_invalid_zone_falls_through(self):
   204→        """Invalid zone value in override falls through to rule matching."""
   205→        overrides = {"scripts/run.py": "invalid_zone"}
   206→        assert (
   207→            classify_file("scripts/run.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES, overrides) == Zone.SCRIPT
   208→        )
   209→
   210→    def test_override_missing_key_falls_through(self):
   211→        """Override dict present but file not in it falls through to rules."""
   212→        overrides = {"other.py": "test"}
   213→        assert (
   214→            classify_file("src/app.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES, overrides) == Zone.PRODUCTION
   215→        )
   216→
   217→    def test_override_none(self):
   218→        """overrides=None is handled gracefully."""
   219→        assert classify_file("src/app.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES, None) == Zone.PRODUCTION
   220→
   221→    def test_empty_rules(self):
   222→        """Empty rules list defaults everything to PRODUCTION."""
   223→        assert classify_file("tests/test_foo.py", []) == Zone.PRODUCTION
   224→
   225→    def test_custom_rules(self):
   226→        """Custom rules correctly classify files."""
   227→        rules = [ZoneRule(Zone.CONFIG, ["config.py", ".cfg"])]
   228→        assert classify_file("src/config.py", rules) == Zone.CONFIG
   229→        assert classify_file("setup.cfg", rules) == Zone.CONFIG
   230→        assert classify_file("src/main.py", rules) == Zone.PRODUCTION
   231→
```
