   235→
   236→class TestFileZoneMap:
   237→    """Tests for the FileZoneMap class."""
   238→
   239→    @pytest.fixture
   240→    def sample_files(self):
   241→        """A set of files spanning multiple zones."""
   242→        return [
   243→            "src/app.py",
   244→            "src/utils.py",
   245→            "tests/test_app.py",
   246→            "tests/test_utils.py",
   247→            "scripts/deploy.sh",
   248→            "vendor/lib.py",
   249→            "generated/types.py",
   250→        ]
   251→
   252→    @pytest.fixture
   253→    def zone_map(self, sample_files):
   254→        """FileZoneMap built from sample files and common rules."""
   255→        return FileZoneMap(sample_files, COMMON_ZONE_RULES)
   256→
   257→    def test_get_production_file(self, zone_map):
   258→        """Production file returns Zone.PRODUCTION."""
   259→        assert zone_map.get("src/app.py") == Zone.PRODUCTION
   260→
   261→    def test_get_test_file(self, zone_map):
   262→        """Test file returns Zone.TEST."""
   263→        assert zone_map.get("tests/test_app.py") == Zone.TEST
   264→
   265→    def test_get_script_file(self, zone_map):
   266→        """Script file returns Zone.SCRIPT."""
   267→        assert zone_map.get("scripts/deploy.sh") == Zone.SCRIPT
   268→
   269→    def test_get_vendor_file(self, zone_map):
   270→        """Vendor file returns Zone.VENDOR."""
   271→        assert zone_map.get("vendor/lib.py") == Zone.VENDOR
   272→
   273→    def test_get_generated_file(self, zone_map):
   274→        """Generated file returns Zone.GENERATED."""
   275→        assert zone_map.get("generated/types.py") == Zone.GENERATED
   276→
   277→    def test_get_unknown_file_defaults_production(self, zone_map):
   278→        """File not in the map returns PRODUCTION."""
   279→        assert zone_map.get("unknown/file.py") == Zone.PRODUCTION
   280→
   281→    def test_exclude_zones(self, zone_map, sample_files):
   282→        """exclude() removes files in specified zones."""
   283→        result = zone_map.exclude(sample_files, Zone.TEST, Zone.VENDOR)
   284→        assert "tests/test_app.py" not in result
   285→        assert "tests/test_utils.py" not in result
   286→        assert "vendor/lib.py" not in result
   287→        assert "src/app.py" in result
   288→        assert "scripts/deploy.sh" in result
   289→
   290→    def test_exclude_no_zones(self, zone_map, sample_files):
   291→        """exclude() with no zones returns all files."""
   292→        result = zone_map.exclude(sample_files)
   293→        assert len(result) == len(sample_files)
   294→
   295→    def test_include_only_zones(self, zone_map, sample_files):
   296→        """include_only() returns files in specified zones."""
   297→        result = zone_map.include_only(sample_files, Zone.TEST)
   298→        assert len(result) == 2
   299→        assert "tests/test_app.py" in result
   300→        assert "tests/test_utils.py" in result
   301→
   302→    def test_include_only_multiple_zones(self, zone_map, sample_files):
   303→        """include_only() works with multiple zones."""
   304→        result = zone_map.include_only(sample_files, Zone.PRODUCTION, Zone.SCRIPT)
   305→        assert "src/app.py" in result
   306→        assert "src/utils.py" in result
   307→        assert "scripts/deploy.sh" in result
   308→        assert len(result) == 3
   309→
   310→    def test_include_only_empty_result(self, zone_map, sample_files):
   311→        """include_only() with zone having no files returns empty."""
   312→        result = zone_map.include_only(sample_files, Zone.CONFIG)
   313→        assert result == []
   314→
   315→    def test_counts(self, zone_map):
   316→        """counts() returns correct per-zone tallies."""
   317→        c = zone_map.counts()
   318→        assert c["production"] == 2
   319→        assert c["test"] == 2
   320→        assert c["script"] == 1
   321→        assert c["vendor"] == 1
   322→        assert c["generated"] == 1
   323→
   324→    def test_production_count(self, zone_map):
   325→        """production_count() returns total minus non-production (SCRIPT is not excluded)."""
   326→        # 7 files - 4 non-production (2 test + 1 vendor + 1 generated) = 3
   327→        # (SCRIPT is NOT in EXCLUDED_ZONES so it counts toward production_count)
   328→        assert zone_map.production_count() == 3
   329→
   330→    def test_non_production_count(self, zone_map):
   331→        """non_production_count() returns count of excluded-zone files."""
   332→        # test(2) + vendor(1) + generated(1) = 4 (script is NOT in EXCLUDED_ZONES)
   333→        assert zone_map.non_production_count() == 4
   334→
   335→    def test_all_files(self, zone_map, sample_files):
   336→        """all_files() returns the full file list."""
   337→        result = zone_map.all_files()
   338→        assert set(result) == set(sample_files)
   339→
   340→    def test_items(self, zone_map, sample_files):
   341→        """items() returns (path, zone) tuples for all files."""
   342→        result = zone_map.items()
   343→        assert len(result) == len(sample_files)
   344→        result_dict = dict(result)
   345→        assert result_dict["src/app.py"] == Zone.PRODUCTION
   346→        assert result_dict["tests/test_app.py"] == Zone.TEST
   347→
   348→    def test_rel_fn(self):
   349→        """rel_fn is used to convert paths before classification."""
   350→        files = ["/project/tests/test_foo.py", "/project/src/main.py"]
   351→
   352→        def strip_prefix(p):
   353→            return p.replace("/project/", "")
   354→
   355→        zm = FileZoneMap(files, COMMON_ZONE_RULES, rel_fn=strip_prefix)
   356→        assert zm.get("/project/tests/test_foo.py") == Zone.TEST
   357→        assert zm.get("/project/src/main.py") == Zone.PRODUCTION
   358→
   359→    def test_overrides(self):
   360→        """Overrides are passed through to classify_file."""
   361→        files = ["src/special.py"]
   362→        overrides = {"src/special.py": "test"}
   363→        zm = FileZoneMap(files, COMMON_ZONE_RULES, overrides=overrides)
   364→        assert zm.get("src/special.py") == Zone.TEST
   365→
   366→    def test_empty_file_list(self):
   367→        """FileZoneMap with no files works correctly."""
   368→        zm = FileZoneMap([], COMMON_ZONE_RULES)
   369→        assert zm.all_files() == []
   370→        assert zm.counts() == {}
   371→        assert zm.production_count() == 0
   372→        assert zm.non_production_count() == 0
   373→
   374→    def test_exclude_with_unknown_files(self, zone_map):
   375→        """exclude() handles files not in the map (defaulting to PRODUCTION)."""
   376→        files = ["src/app.py", "unknown.py"]
   377→        result = zone_map.exclude(files, Zone.PRODUCTION)
   378→        # Both are PRODUCTION (one explicitly, one by default), so both excluded
   379→        assert result == []
   380→
   381→    def test_include_only_with_unknown_files(self, zone_map):
   382→        """include_only() handles files not in the map (defaulting to PRODUCTION)."""
   383→        files = ["src/app.py", "unknown.py"]
   384→        result = zone_map.include_only(files, Zone.PRODUCTION)
   385→        assert len(result) == 2
   386→

   391→class TestZoneEnumAndConstants:
   392→    """Tests for Zone enum and related constants."""
   393→
   394→    def test_zone_values(self):
   395→        """All expected zone values exist."""
   396→        assert Zone.PRODUCTION.value == "production"
   397→        assert Zone.TEST.value == "test"
   398→        assert Zone.CONFIG.value == "config"
   399→        assert Zone.GENERATED.value == "generated"
   400→        assert Zone.SCRIPT.value == "script"
   401→        assert Zone.VENDOR.value == "vendor"
   402→
   403→    def test_zone_is_str(self):
   404→        """Zone is a str enum."""
   405→        assert isinstance(Zone.PRODUCTION, str)
   406→
   407→    def test_excluded_zones(self):
   408→        """EXCLUDED_ZONES contains the correct zones."""
   409→        assert EXCLUDED_ZONES == {Zone.TEST, Zone.CONFIG, Zone.GENERATED, Zone.VENDOR}
   410→        # SCRIPT and PRODUCTION are NOT excluded
   411→        assert Zone.SCRIPT not in EXCLUDED_ZONES
   412→        assert Zone.PRODUCTION not in EXCLUDED_ZONES
   413→
   414→    def test_excluded_zone_values(self):
   415→        """EXCLUDED_ZONE_VALUES contains string values of excluded zones."""
   416→        assert EXCLUDED_ZONE_VALUES == {"test", "config", "generated", "vendor"}
   417→

   421→
   422→class TestZonePolicies:
   423→    """Tests for zone policy definitions."""
   424→
   425→    def test_production_policy_is_permissive(self):
   426→        """PRODUCTION policy has no skips or downgrades."""
   427→        policy = ZONE_POLICIES[Zone.PRODUCTION]
   428→        assert policy.skip_detectors == set()
   429→        assert policy.downgrade_detectors == set()
   430→        assert policy.exclude_from_score is False
   431→
   432→    def test_test_zone_skips(self):
   433→        """TEST zone skips expected detectors."""
   434→        policy = ZONE_POLICIES[Zone.TEST]
   435→        expected_skips = {
   436→            "boilerplate_duplication",
   437→            "dupes",
   438→            "single_use",
   439→            "orphaned",
   440→            "coupling",
   441→            "facade",
   442→            "dict_keys",
   443→            "test_coverage",
   444→            "security",
   445→            "private_imports",
   446→        }
   447→        assert policy.skip_detectors == expected_skips
   448→
   449→    def test_test_zone_downgrades(self):
   450→        """TEST zone downgrades smells and structural."""
   451→        policy = ZONE_POLICIES[Zone.TEST]
   452→        assert policy.downgrade_detectors == {"smells", "structural"}
   453→
   454→    def test_test_zone_excluded_from_score(self):
   455→        """TEST zone is excluded from scoring."""
   456→        assert ZONE_POLICIES[Zone.TEST].exclude_from_score is True
   457→
   458→    def test_config_zone_skips(self):
   459→        """CONFIG zone skips a broad set of detectors."""
   460→        policy = ZONE_POLICIES[Zone.CONFIG]
   461→        expected_skips = {
   462→            "boilerplate_duplication",
   463→            "smells",
   464→            "structural",
   465→            "dupes",
   466→            "naming",
   467→            "single_use",
   468→            "orphaned",
   469→            "coupling",
   470→            "facade",
   471→            "dict_keys",
   472→            "test_coverage",
   473→            "security",
   474→        }
   475→        assert policy.skip_detectors == expected_skips
   476→
   477→    def test_config_zone_excluded_from_score(self):
   478→        """CONFIG zone is excluded from scoring."""
   479→        assert ZONE_POLICIES[Zone.CONFIG].exclude_from_score is True
   480→
   481→    def test_generated_zone_skips_most(self):
   482→        """GENERATED zone skips nearly all detectors."""
   483→        policy = ZONE_POLICIES[Zone.GENERATED]
   484→        assert "test_coverage" in policy.skip_detectors
   485→        assert "unused" in policy.skip_detectors
   486→        assert "smells" in policy.skip_detectors
   487→        assert "react" in policy.skip_detectors
   488→        assert "naming" in policy.skip_detectors
   489→        assert "cycles" in policy.skip_detectors
   490→
   491→    def test_generated_zone_excluded_from_score(self):
   492→        """GENERATED zone is excluded from scoring."""
   493→        assert ZONE_POLICIES[Zone.GENERATED].exclude_from_score is True
   494→
   495→    def test_vendor_zone_matches_generated(self):
   496→        """VENDOR zone has the same skip set as GENERATED."""
   497→        vendor = ZONE_POLICIES[Zone.VENDOR]
   498→        generated = ZONE_POLICIES[Zone.GENERATED]
   499→        assert vendor.skip_detectors == generated.skip_detectors
   500→        assert vendor.exclude_from_score is True
   501→
   502→    def test_script_zone_skips(self):
   503→        """SCRIPT zone skips coupling, single_use, orphaned, facade."""
   504→        policy = ZONE_POLICIES[Zone.SCRIPT]
   505→        assert policy.skip_detectors == {"coupling", "single_use", "orphaned", "facade"}
   506→
   507→    def test_script_zone_downgrades_structural(self):
   508→        """SCRIPT zone downgrades structural."""
   509→        assert ZONE_POLICIES[Zone.SCRIPT].downgrade_detectors == {"structural"}
   510→
   511→    def test_script_zone_not_excluded_from_score(self):
   512→        """SCRIPT zone is NOT excluded from scoring."""
   513→        assert ZONE_POLICIES[Zone.SCRIPT].exclude_from_score is False
   514→
   515→    def test_all_non_production_zones_skip_test_coverage(self):
   516→        """test_coverage is in skip_detectors for TEST, CONFIG, GENERATED, VENDOR."""
   517→        for zone in [Zone.TEST, Zone.CONFIG, Zone.GENERATED, Zone.VENDOR]:
   518→            policy = ZONE_POLICIES[zone]
   519→            assert "test_coverage" in policy.skip_detectors, (
   520→                f"{zone.value} zone should skip test_coverage"
   521→            )
   522→
   523→    def test_production_does_not_skip_test_coverage(self):
   524→        """PRODUCTION zone does NOT skip test_coverage."""

   526→
   527→    def test_script_does_not_skip_test_coverage(self):
   528→        """SCRIPT zone does NOT skip test_coverage."""
   529→        assert "test_coverage" not in ZONE_POLICIES[Zone.SCRIPT].skip_detectors
   530→
   531→    def test_every_zone_has_policy(self):
   532→        """Every Zone enum value has a corresponding policy."""
   533→        for zone in Zone:
   534→            assert zone in ZONE_POLICIES, f"Missing policy for {zone.value}"

   539→
   540→class TestAdjustPotential:
   541→    """Tests for the adjust_potential helper."""
   542→
   543→    def test_with_zone_map(self):
   544→        """Subtracts non-production count from total."""
   545→        files = [
   546→            "src/app.py",
   547→            "tests/test_app.py",
   548→            "vendor/lib.py",
   549→        ]
   550→        zm = FileZoneMap(files, COMMON_ZONE_RULES)
   551→        # non_production = test(1) + vendor(1) = 2
   552→        result = adjust_potential(zm, 10)
   553→        assert result == 8
   554→
   555→    def test_with_zone_map_all_production(self):
   556→        """No adjustment when all files are production."""
   557→        files = ["src/app.py", "src/utils.py"]
   558→        zm = FileZoneMap(files, COMMON_ZONE_RULES)
   559→        assert adjust_potential(zm, 5) == 5
   560→
   561→    def test_with_zone_map_clamps_to_zero(self):
   562→        """Result is clamped to 0 if non-production exceeds total."""
   563→        files = ["tests/a.py", "tests/b.py", "vendor/c.py"]
   564→        zm = FileZoneMap(files, COMMON_ZONE_RULES)
   565→        # All 3 are non-production, total is 1
   566→        assert adjust_potential(zm, 1) == 0
   567→
   568→    def test_none_zone_map(self):
   569→        """Returns total unchanged when zone_map is None."""
   570→        assert adjust_potential(None, 42) == 42
   571→
   572→    def test_zero_total(self):
   573→        """Zero total stays zero."""
   574→        files = ["src/app.py"]
   575→        zm = FileZoneMap(files, COMMON_ZONE_RULES)
   576→        assert adjust_potential(zm, 0) == 0

   582→class TestShouldSkipIssue:
   583→    """Tests for the should_skip_issue helper."""
   584→
   585→    @pytest.fixture
   586→    def zone_map(self):
   587→        files = [
   588→            "src/app.py",
   589→            "tests/test_app.py",
   590→            "scripts/deploy.sh",
   591→            "vendor/lib.py",
   592→        ]
   593→        return FileZoneMap(files, COMMON_ZONE_RULES)
   594→
   595→    def test_skip_in_test_zone(self, zone_map):
   596→        """Detectors in TEST skip_detectors are skipped for test files."""
   597→        assert should_skip_issue(zone_map, "tests/test_app.py", "dupes") is True
   598→        assert should_skip_issue(zone_map, "tests/test_app.py", "coupling") is True
   599→        assert (
   600→            should_skip_issue(zone_map, "tests/test_app.py", "test_coverage") is True
   601→        )
   602→        assert should_skip_issue(zone_map, "tests/test_app.py", "security") is True
   603→
   604→    def test_allow_in_test_zone(self, zone_map):
   605→        """Detectors NOT in TEST skip_detectors are allowed for test files."""
   606→        assert should_skip_issue(zone_map, "tests/test_app.py", "unused") is False
   607→        assert should_skip_issue(zone_map, "tests/test_app.py", "logs") is False
   608→
   609→    def test_production_never_skips(self, zone_map):
   610→        """PRODUCTION zone never skips any detector."""
   611→        assert should_skip_issue(zone_map, "src/app.py", "dupes") is False
   612→        assert should_skip_issue(zone_map, "src/app.py", "smells") is False
   613→        assert should_skip_issue(zone_map, "src/app.py", "coupling") is False
   614→
   615→    def test_vendor_skips_most(self, zone_map):
   616→        """VENDOR zone skips most detectors."""
   617→        assert should_skip_issue(zone_map, "vendor/lib.py", "unused") is True
   618→        assert should_skip_issue(zone_map, "vendor/lib.py", "smells") is True
   619→        assert should_skip_issue(zone_map, "vendor/lib.py", "naming") is True
   620→        assert should_skip_issue(zone_map, "vendor/lib.py", "test_coverage") is True
   621→
   622→    def test_script_skips_subset(self, zone_map):
   623→        """SCRIPT zone skips only its specific detectors."""
   624→        assert should_skip_issue(zone_map, "scripts/deploy.sh", "coupling") is True
   625→        assert should_skip_issue(zone_map, "scripts/deploy.sh", "facade") is True
   626→        # But not smells
   627→        assert should_skip_issue(zone_map, "scripts/deploy.sh", "smells") is False
   628→
   629→    def test_none_zone_map_never_skips(self):
   630→        """Returns False when zone_map is None (backward compat)."""
   631→        assert should_skip_issue(None, "tests/test_app.py", "dupes") is False
   632→
   633→    def test_unknown_file_defaults_production(self, zone_map):
   634→        """File not in the map is treated as PRODUCTION (no skips)."""
   635→        assert should_skip_issue(zone_map, "unknown.py", "dupes") is False

1→"""Score/value accessor helpers for state schema payloads."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→
     9→def json_default(obj: Any) -> Any:
    10→    """JSON serializer fallback for known non-JSON-native state values."""
    11→    if isinstance(obj, set):
    12→        return sorted(obj)
    13→    if isinstance(obj, Path):
    14→        return str(obj).replace("\\", "/")
    15→    if hasattr(obj, "isoformat"):
    16→        return obj.isoformat()
    17→    raise TypeError(
    18→        f"Object of type {type(obj).__name__} is not JSON serializable: {obj!r}"
    19→    )
    20→
    21→
    22→def get_overall_score(state: dict[str, Any]) -> float | None:
    23→    value = state.get("overall_score")
    24→    return float(value) if isinstance(value, int | float) else None
    25→
    26→
    27→def get_objective_score(state: dict[str, Any]) -> float | None:
    28→    value = state.get("objective_score")
    29→    return float(value) if isinstance(value, int | float) else None
    30→
    31→
    32→def get_strict_score(state: dict[str, Any]) -> float | None:
    33→    value = state.get("strict_score")
    34→    return float(value) if isinstance(value, int | float) else None
    35→
    36→
    37→def get_verified_strict_score(state: dict[str, Any]) -> float | None:
    38→    value = state.get("verified_strict_score")
    39→    return float(value) if isinstance(value, int | float) else None
    40→
    41→
    42→__all__ = [
    43→    "get_objective_score",
    44→    "get_overall_score",
    45→    "get_strict_score",
    46→    "get_verified_strict_score",
    47→    "json_default",
    48→]

1→"""Bridge between state persistence and scoring computation.
     2→
     3→This module owns the score-recomputation step that runs before state is written.
     4→The dependency direction is: _scoring/state_integration -> _state (reads state),
     5→_scoring/state_integration -> _scoring (calls scoring functions).
     6→State persistence calls this module, never the reverse.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from copy import deepcopy
    12→
    13→from desloppify.base.enums import issue_status_tokens
    14→from desloppify.engine._scoring.detection import merge_potentials
    15→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import matches_target_score
    16→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.core import (
    17→    compute_health_score,
    18→    compute_score_bundle,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.engine._scoring.state_coverage import (
    21→    apply_scan_coverage_to_dimension_scores as _apply_scan_coverage_to_dimension_scores,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import path_scoped_issues
    24→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, ensure_state_defaults
    25→
    26→_EMPTY_COUNTERS = tuple(sorted(issue_status_tokens()))
    27→_SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_RESET_THRESHOLD = 2
    28→
    29→
    30→def _resolve_lang_from_state(state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    31→    """Best-effort language detection from state (scan_history > lang_capabilities)."""
    32→    history = state.get("scan_history")
    33→    if isinstance(history, list):
    34→        for entry in reversed(history):
    35→            if isinstance(entry, dict):
    36→                lang = entry.get("lang")
    37→                if isinstance(lang, str) and lang.strip():
    38→                    return lang.strip().lower()
    39→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
    40→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict) and len(capabilities) == 1:
    41→        only_lang = next(iter(capabilities.keys()))
    42→        if isinstance(only_lang, str) and only_lang.strip():
    43→            return only_lang.strip().lower()
    44→    return None
    45→
    46→
    47→def _count_issues(issues: dict) -> tuple[dict[str, int], dict[int, dict[str, int]]]:
    48→    """Tally per-status counters and per-tier breakdowns."""
    49→    counters = dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0)
    50→    tier_stats: dict[int, dict[str, int]] = {}
    51→
    52→    for issue in issues.values():
    53→        if issue.get("suppressed"):
    54→            continue
    55→        status = issue["status"]
    56→        tier = issue.get("tier", 3)
    57→        counters[status] = counters.get(status, 0) + 1
    58→        tier_counter = tier_stats.setdefault(tier, dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0))
    59→        tier_counter[status] = tier_counter.get(status, 0) + 1
    60→
    61→    return counters, tier_stats
    62→
    63→
    64→def _coerce_subjective_score(value: dict | float | int | str | None) -> float:
    65→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float."""
    66→    raw = value.get("score", 0) if isinstance(value, dict) else value
    67→    try:
    68→        score = float(raw)
    69→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    70→        score = 0.0
    71→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, score))
    72→
    73→
    74→def _subjective_target_matches(
    75→    subjective_assessments: dict, *, target: float
    76→) -> list[str]:
    77→    """Return dimension keys whose subjective score matches the target band."""
    78→    matches = [
    79→        dimension
    80→        for dimension, payload in subjective_assessments.items()
    81→        if matches_target_score(_coerce_subjective_score(payload), target)
    82→    ]
    83→    return sorted(matches)
    84→
    85→
    86→def _subjective_integrity_baseline(target: float | None) -> dict[str, object]:
    87→    """Create baseline subjective-integrity metadata for scan/reporting output."""
    88→    return {
    89→        "status": "disabled" if target is None else "pass",
    90→        "target_score": None if target is None else round(float(target), 2),
    91→        "matched_count": 0,
    92→        "matched_dimensions": [],
    93→        "reset_dimensions": [],
    94→    }
    95→
    96→
    97→def _apply_subjective_integrity_policy(
    98→    subjective_assessments: dict,
    99→    *,
   100→    target: float,
   101→) -> tuple[dict, dict[str, object]]:
   102→    """Apply anti-gaming penalties for subjective scores clustered on the target."""
   103→    normalized_target = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(target)))
   104→    matched_dimensions = _subjective_target_matches(
   105→        subjective_assessments,
   106→        target=normalized_target,
   107→    )
   108→    meta = _subjective_integrity_baseline(normalized_target)
   109→    meta["matched_count"] = len(matched_dimensions)
   110→    meta["matched_dimensions"] = matched_dimensions
   111→
   112→    if len(matched_dimensions) < _SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_RESET_THRESHOLD:
   113→        meta["status"] = "warn" if matched_dimensions else "pass"
   114→        return subjective_assessments, meta
   115→
   116→    adjusted = deepcopy(subjective_assessments)
   117→    for dimension in matched_dimensions:
   118→        payload = adjusted.get(dimension)
   119→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   120→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   121→            payload["integrity_penalty"] = "target_match_reset"
   122→        else:
   123→            adjusted[dimension] = {
   124→                "score": 0.0,
   125→                "integrity_penalty": "target_match_reset",
   126→            }
   127→
   128→    meta["status"] = "penalized"
   129→    meta["reset_dimensions"] = matched_dimensions
   130→    return adjusted, meta
   131→
   132→
   133→def _aggregate_scores(dim_scores: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   134→    """Derive the four aggregate scores from dimension-level data."""
   135→    mechanical = {
   136→        n: d
   137→        for n, d in dim_scores.items()
   138→        if "subjective_assessment" not in d.get("detectors", {})
   139→    }
   140→    return {
   141→        "overall_score": compute_health_score(dim_scores),
   142→        "strict_score": compute_health_score(dim_scores, score_key="strict"),
   143→        "objective_score": compute_health_score(mechanical),
   144→        "verified_strict_score": compute_health_score(
   145→            mechanical,
   146→            [REDACTED],
   147→        ),
   148→    }
   149→
   150→
   151→def _normalize_integrity_target(
   152→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None,
   153→) -> float | None:
   154→    """Normalize and clamp a subjective integrity target to [0, 100]."""
   155→    if isinstance(subjective_integrity_target, int | float):
   156→        return max(0.0, min(100.0, float(subjective_integrity_target)))
   157→    return None
   158→
   159→
   160→def _set_perfect_scores(state: StateModel) -> None:
   161→    """Set all score fields to 100 when there are no active checks."""
   162→    state["dimension_scores"] = {}
   163→    state["overall_score"] = 100.0
   164→    state["objective_score"] = 100.0
   165→    state["strict_score"] = 100.0
   166→    state["verified_strict_score"] = 100.0
   167→
   168→
   169→def _resolve_allowed_subjective_dimensions(
   170→    state: StateModel,
   171→) -> set[str] | None:
   172→    """Resolve allowed subjective dimensions from the language config."""
   173→    lang_name = _resolve_lang_from_state(state)
   174→    if not lang_name:
   175→        return None
   176→    try:
   177→        from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.data import (
   178→            load_dimensions_for_lang,
   179→        )
   180→
   181→        dims, _, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   182→        if dims:
   183→            return set(dims)
   184→    except (ImportError, AttributeError) as exc:
   185→        _ = exc
   186→    return None
   187→
   188→
   189→def _materialize_dimension_scores(
   190→    state: StateModel,
   191→    bundle: object,
   192→) -> None:
   193→    """Write dimension scores from a score bundle into state, carrying forward old dims."""
   194→    lenient_scores = bundle.dimension_scores
   195→    strict_scores = bundle.strict_dimension_scores
   196→    verified_strict_scores = bundle.verified_strict_dimension_scores
   197→
   198→    prev_dim_scores = dict(state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   199→
   200→    state["dimension_scores"] = {
   201→        name: dict(
   202→            score=lenient_scores[name]["score"],
   203→            strict=strict_scores[name]["score"],
   204→            verified_strict_score=verified_strict_scores[name]["score"],
   205→            checks=lenient_scores[name]["checks"],
   206→            failing=lenient_scores[name]["failing"],
   207→            tier=lenient_scores[name]["tier"],
   208→            detectors=lenient_scores[name].get("detectors", {}),
   209→        )
   210→        for name in lenient_scores
   211→    }
   212→
   213→    for dim_name, prev_data in prev_dim_scores.items():
   214→        if dim_name in state["dimension_scores"]:
   215→            continue
   216→        if not isinstance(prev_data, dict):
   217→            continue
   218→        if "subjective_assessment" in prev_data.get("detectors", {}):
   219→            continue
   220→        carried = {**prev_data, "carried_forward": True}
   221→        carried.setdefault("score", 0.0)
   222→        carried.setdefault("strict", carried.get("score", 0.0))
   223→        carried.setdefault(
   224→            "verified_strict_score",
   225→            carried.get("strict", carried.get("score", 0.0)),
   226→        )
   227→        state["dimension_scores"][dim_name] = carried
   228→
   229→    _apply_scan_coverage_to_dimension_scores(
   230→        state,
   231→        dimension_scores=state["dimension_scores"],
   232→    )
   233→    state.update(_aggregate_scores(state["dimension_scores"]))
   234→
   235→
   236→def _update_objective_health(
   237→    state: StateModel,
   238→    issues: dict,
   239→    *,
   240→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   241→) -> None:
   242→    """Compute canonical score tuple from current detector issues/potentials."""
   243→    pots = state.get("potentials", {})
   244→    if not pots:
   245→        return
   246→
   247→    merged = merge_potentials(pots)
   248→    if not merged:
   249→        return
   250→

1→"""State persistence and migration routines."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import shutil
     8→import sys
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→from typing import cast
    11→
    12→__all__ = [
    13→    "load_state",
    14→    "save_state",
    15→]
    16→
    17→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import safe_write_text
    18→from desloppify.base.text_utils import is_numeric
    19→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    20→    CURRENT_VERSION,
    21→    STATE_FILE,
    22→    StateModel,
    23→    empty_state,
    24→    ensure_state_defaults,
    25→    json_default,
    26→    validate_state_invariants,
    27→)
    28→
    29→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    30→
    31→
    32→from desloppify.engine._state import _recompute_stats
    33→
    34→
    35→def _load_json(path: Path) -> dict[str, object]:
    36→    data = json.loads(path.read_text())
    37→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    38→        raise ValueError("state file root must be a JSON object")
    39→    return data
    40→
    41→
    42→def _normalize_loaded_state(data: object) -> dict[str, object]:
    43→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    44→        raise ValueError("state file root must be a JSON object")
    45→    ensure_state_defaults(data)
    46→    normalized = cast(StateModel, data)
    47→    validate_state_invariants(normalized)
    48→    return normalized
    49→
    50→
    51→def load_state(path: Path | None = None) -> StateModel:
    52→    """Load state from disk, or return empty state on missing/corruption."""
    53→    state_path = path or STATE_FILE
    54→    if not state_path.exists():
    55→        return empty_state()
    56→
    57→    try:
    58→        data = _load_json(state_path)
    59→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError, ValueError) as ex:
    60→        backup = state_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    61→        if backup.exists():
    62→            logger.warning(
    63→                "Primary state load failed for %s; attempting backup %s: %s",
    64→                state_path,
    65→                backup,
    66→                ex,
    67→            )
    68→            try:
    69→                backup_data = _load_json(backup)
    70→                logger.warning(
    71→                    "Recovered state from backup %s after primary load failure at %s",
    72→                    backup,
    73→                    state_path,
    74→                )
    75→                print(
    76→                    f"  ⚠ State file corrupted ({ex}), loaded from backup.",
    77→                    file=sys.stderr,
    78→                )
    79→                return _normalize_loaded_state(backup_data)
    80→            except (
    81→                json.JSONDecodeError,
    82→                UnicodeDecodeError,
    83→                OSError,
    84→                ValueError,
    85→                TypeError,
    86→                AttributeError,
    87→            ) as backup_ex:
    88→                logger.warning(
    89→                    "Backup state load failed from %s after corruption in %s: %s",
    90→                    backup,
    91→                    state_path,
    92→                    backup_ex,
    93→                )
    94→                logger.debug("Backup state load failed from %s: %s", backup, backup_ex)
    95→
    96→        logger.warning(
    97→            "State file load failed for %s and backup recovery was unavailable. "
    98→            "Falling back to empty state: %s",
    99→            state_path,
   100→            ex,
   101→        )
   102→        print(f"  ⚠ State file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
   103→        rename_failed = False
   104→        try:
   105→            state_path.rename(state_path.with_suffix(".json.corrupted"))
   106→        except OSError as rename_ex:
   107→            rename_failed = True
   108→            logger.debug(
   109→                "Failed to rename corrupted state file %s: %s", state_path, rename_ex
   110→            )
   111→        if rename_failed:
   112→            logger.debug(
   113→                "Corrupted state file retained at original path: %s", state_path
   114→            )
   115→        return empty_state()
   116→
   117→    version = data.get("version", 1)
   118→    if version > CURRENT_VERSION:
   119→        print(
   120→            "  ⚠ State file version "
   121→            f"{version} is newer than supported ({CURRENT_VERSION}). "
   122→            "Some features may not work correctly.",
   123→            file=sys.stderr,
   124→        )
   125→
   126→    try:
   127→        return _normalize_loaded_state(data)
   128→    except (ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as normalize_ex:
   129→        logger.warning(
   130→            "State invariants invalid for %s; falling back to empty state: %s",
   131→            state_path,
   132→            normalize_ex,
   133→        )
   134→        print(
   135→            f"  ⚠ State invariants invalid ({normalize_ex}). Starting fresh.",
   136→            file=sys.stderr,
   137→        )
   138→        return empty_state()
   139→
   140→
   141→def _coerce_integrity_target(value: object) -> float | None:
   142→    if not is_numeric(value):
   143→        return None
   144→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value)))
   145→
   146→
   147→def _resolve_integrity_target(
   148→    state: StateModel,
   149→    explicit_target: float | None,
   150→) -> float | None:
   151→    target = _coerce_integrity_target(explicit_target)
   152→    if target is not None:
   153→        return target
   154→
   155→    integrity = state.get("subjective_integrity")
   156→    if not isinstance(integrity, dict):
   157→        return None
   158→    return _coerce_integrity_target(integrity.get("target_score"))
   159→
   160→
   161→def save_state(
   162→    state: StateModel,
   163→    path: Path | None = None,
   164→    *,
   165→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   166→) -> None:
   167→    """Recompute stats/score and save to disk atomically."""
   168→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   169→    _recompute_stats(
   170→        state,
   171→        scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   172→        subjective_integrity_target=_resolve_integrity_target(
   173→            state,
   174→            subjective_integrity_target,
   175→        ),
   176→    )
   177→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   178→
   179→    state_path = path or STATE_FILE
   180→    state_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   181→
   182→    content = json.dumps(state, indent=2, default=json_default) + "\n"
   183→
   184→    if state_path.exists():
   185→        backup = state_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
   186→        try:
   187→            shutil.copy2(str(state_path), str(backup))
   188→        except OSError as backup_ex:
   189→            logger.debug(
   190→                "Failed to create state backup %s: %s",
   191→                state_path.with_suffix(".json.bak"),
   192→                backup_ex,
   193→            )
   194→
   195→    try:
   196→        safe_write_text(state_path, content)
   197→    except OSError as ex:
   198→        print(f"  Warning: Could not save state: {ex}", file=sys.stderr)
   199→        raise
   200→

   277→def recompute_stats(
   278→    state: StateModel,

   290→        "by_tier": {
   291→            str(tier): tier_counts for tier, tier_counts in sorted(tier_stats.items())
   292→        },
   293→    }
   294→    _update_objective_health(
   295→        state,

    37→# Mechanical detectors → subjective dimensions they provide evidence for.
    38→# When issues from these detectors change during a scan, the corresponding
    39→# subjective assessments are marked stale so reviewers know to re-evaluate.
    40→_DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    41→    "structural": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    42→    "smells": ("design_coherence", "error_consistency"),
    43→    "global_mutable_config": ("initialization_coupling",),
    44→    "coupling": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    45→    "layer_violation": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    46→    "private_imports": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    47→    "dupes": ("convention_outlier",),
    48→    "boilerplate_duplication": ("convention_outlier",),
    49→    "naming": ("convention_outlier",),
    50→    "flat_dirs": ("package_organization",),
    51→    "orphaned": ("design_coherence",),
    52→    "uncalled_functions": ("design_coherence",),
    53→    "responsibility_cohesion": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    54→    "cycles": ("cross_module_architecture", "dependency_health"),
    55→}
    56→
    57→
    58→def _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(
    59→    state: StateModel,
    60→    *,
    61→    changed_detectors: set[str],
    62→    now: str,
    63→) -> None:
    64→    """Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical issues change.
    65→
    66→    Only marks dimensions that already have an assessment — doesn't create
    67→    new entries for dimensions that have never been reviewed.
    68→    """
    69→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    70→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    71→        return
    72→
    73→    affected_dims: set[str] = set()
    74→    for detector in changed_detectors:
    75→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
    76→        if meta is None or not meta.marks_dims_stale:
    77→            continue
    78→        dims = _DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS.get(detector)
    79→        if dims:
    80→            affected_dims.update(dims)
    81→            continue
    82→        # Safety fallback for newly added "marks_dims_stale" detectors that
    83→        # have not declared fine-grained dimension mappings yet.
    84→        affected_dims.update(
    85→            dim
    86→            for dim in assessments
    87→            if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()
    88→        )
    89→
    90→    if not affected_dims:
    91→        return
    92→
    93→    for dimension in sorted(affected_dims):
    94→        if dimension not in assessments:
    95→            continue
    96→        payload = assessments[dimension]
    97→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    98→            continue
    99→        # Don't overwrite if already stale
   100→        if payload.get("needs_review_refresh"):
   101→            continue
   102→        payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
   103→        payload["refresh_reason"] = "mechanical_issues_changed"
   104→        payload["stale_since"] = now
   105→
   106→
   107→@dataclass
   108→class MergeScanOptions:
   109→    """Configuration bundle for merging a scan into persisted state."""
   110→
   111→    lang: str | None = None
   112→    scan_path: str | None = None
   113→    force_resolve: bool = False
   114→    exclude: tuple[str, ...] = ()
   115→    potentials: dict[str, int] | None = None

   118→    include_slow: bool = True
   119→    ignore: list[str] | None = None
   120→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None
   121→
   122→
   123→def merge_scan(
   124→    state: StateModel,
   125→    current_issues: list[dict],
   126→    options: MergeScanOptions | None = None,
   127→) -> ScanDiff:
   128→    """Merge a fresh scan into existing state and return a diff summary."""
   129→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   130→    resolved_options = options or MergeScanOptions()
   131→
   132→    now = utc_now()
   133→    _record_scan_metadata(
   134→        state,
   135→        now,
   136→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   137→        include_slow=resolved_options.include_slow,
   138→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   139→    )
   140→    _merge_scan_inputs(
   141→        state,
   142→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   143→        potentials=resolved_options.potentials,
   144→        merge_potentials=resolved_options.merge_potentials,
   145→        codebase_metrics=resolved_options.codebase_metrics,
   146→    )
   147→
   148→    existing = state["issues"]
   149→    ignore_patterns = (
   150→        resolved_options.ignore
   151→        if resolved_options.ignore is not None
   152→        else state.get("config", {}).get("ignore", [])
   153→    )
   154→    current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, current_by_detector, ignored_count, upsert_changed = (
   155→        upsert_issues(
   156→            existing,
   157→            current_issues,
   158→            ignore_patterns,
   159→            now,
   160→            lang=resolved_options.lang,
   161→        )
   162→    )
   163→
   164→    raw_issues = len(current_issues)
   165→    suppressed_pct = _compute_suppression(raw_issues, ignored_count)
   166→
   167→    ran_detectors = (
   168→        set(resolved_options.potentials.keys())
   169→        if resolved_options.potentials is not None
   170→        else None
   171→    )
   172→    suspect_detectors = find_suspect_detectors(
   173→        existing,
   174→        current_by_detector,
   175→        resolved_options.force_resolve,
   176→        ran_detectors,
   177→    )
   178→    auto_resolved, skipped_other_lang, resolved_out_of_scope, resolve_changed = auto_resolve_disappeared(
   179→        existing,
   180→        current_ids,
   181→        suspect_detectors,
   182→        now,
   183→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   184→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   185→        exclude=resolved_options.exclude,
   186→    )
   187→
   188→    # Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical issues changed.
   189→    changed_detectors = upsert_changed | resolve_changed
   190→    if changed_detectors:
   191→        _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(
   192→            state, changed_detectors=changed_detectors, now=now,
   193→        )
   194→
   195→    _recompute_stats(

   197→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   198→        subjective_integrity_target=resolved_options.subjective_integrity_target,
   199→    )

    67→def _show_scan_visibility(noise, effective_include_slow: bool) -> None:

    69→
    70→    Side-effect only: conditionally prints scan-mode warnings to stdout.
    71→    All branches may be skipped (full scan, no noise budget hit, no hidden
    72→    issues), so the function can legitimately produce no output.

    74→    if not effective_include_slow:

    76→    if noise.budget_warning:

    99→        remediation = str(entry.get("remediation", "")).strip()
   100→        detector = str(entry.get("detector", "")).strip() or "detector"

   115→    """Run all detectors, update persistent state, show diff."""

   128→        run_scan_generation_fn=run_scan_generation,
   129→        merge_scan_results_fn=merge_scan_results,
   130→        resolve_noise_snapshot_fn=resolve_noise_snapshot,
   131→        persist_reminder_history_fn=persist_reminder_history,
   132→    )
   133→    _print_scan_header(runtime.lang_label)
   134→    if runtime.reset_subjective_count > 0:
   135→        print(

   137→                "  * Subjective reset "
   138→                f"{runtime.reset_subjective_count} subjective dimensions to 0",
   139→                "yellow",
   140→            )
   141→        )
   142→    if runtime.expired_manual_override_count > 0:
   143→        print(

   146→                f"{runtime.expired_manual_override_count} dimension(s) reset to 0. "

   149→            )
   150→        )

   155→    _print_scan_complete_banner()

   157→    noise = orchestrator.noise_snapshot()
   158→

   165→        merge.prev_objective,
   166→        merge.prev_strict,
   167→        merge.prev_verified,
   168→        target_strict=target_value,
   169→    )
   170→    # Nudge: if plan_start_scores was just seeded, tell the agent about the lifecycle.
   171→    _print_plan_workflow_nudge(runtime.state)
   172→    _show_scan_visibility(noise, runtime.effective_include_slow)
   173→    show_scorecard_subjective_measures(runtime.state)
   174→    show_score_model_breakdown(runtime.state)
   175→
   176→    new_dim_scores = runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   177→    if new_dim_scores and merge.prev_dim_scores:
   178→        show_dimension_deltas(merge.prev_dim_scores, new_dim_scores)
   179→
   180→    warnings, narrative = show_post_scan_analysis(

   182→        runtime.state,
   183→        runtime.lang,

   185→    )
   186→    orchestrator.persist_reminders(narrative)

   188→    write_query(

   201→
   202→    badge_path, _badge_result = emit_scorecard_badge(args, runtime.config, runtime.state)
   203→    print_llm_summary(runtime.state, badge_path, narrative, merge.diff)

## Summary of Zone Override System in Desloppify

Based on my thorough search of the codebase, here are the answers to your questions:

### 1. **Message After Setting a Zone**

When you run `desloppify zone set <file> <zone>`, the `_zone_set()` function in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/zone.py` (lines 80-102) prints:

```
  Set <normalized_path> → <zone_value>
  Run `desloppify scan` to apply.
  Next command: `desloppify scan`
```

The exact message is at **line 100**: `"Run `desloppify scan` to apply."` (colorized in dim).

Similarly, `zone clear` (line 118) prints the same message.

### 2. **Can Zone Overrides Apply Immediately Without a Scan?**

**Currently: NO.** Zone overrides require a full `desloppify scan` to take effect. Here's why:

- **Zone overrides are config-only**: They're stored in the project config file at `zone_overrides` key (a dictionary mapping normalized file paths to zone values).
- **Applied during scan setup only**: The zone overrides are only read and applied when:
  1. `prepare_scan_runtime()` is called (line 298 in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/workflow.py`)
  2. `run_scan_generation()` calls `plan_mod.generate_issues()` (line 364)
  3. Which calls `_build_zone_map()` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/planning/scan.py` (line 48)
  4. `FileZoneMap` is instantiated with the overrides (line 53-54)

- **Impact on detection**: The zone map affects:
  - Which detectors are **skipped** (via `ZONE_POLICIES`)
  - Which issues are **excluded from scoring** (non-production zones)
  - Which issues have **downgraded confidence** (some zones downgrade smells/structural)

### 3. **Where Zone Overrides Are Stored and Applied**

**Storage:**
- **Location**: Project config file (typically `.desloppify/config.json` or equivalent)
- **Key**: `zone_overrides` (dictionary)
- **Format**: `{"relative/path/to/file.ext": "zone_name", ...}`
- **Path normalization**: Uses `rel()` function to normalize paths before storing

**Applied During Scan:**
1. **Reading** (workflow.py, lines 298-299):
   ```python
   zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   ```

2. **Building zone map** (scan.py, lines 48-60):
   ```python
   def _build_zone_map(path: Path, lang: LangRun, zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None) -> None:
       if not (lang.zone_rules and lang.file_finder):
           return
       files = lang.file_finder(path)
       lang.zone_map = FileZoneMap(
           files, lang.zone_rules, rel_fn=rel, overrides=zone_overrides
       )
   ```

3. **Classification** (zones.py, lines 104-122):
   ```python
   def classify_file(
       rel_path: str, rules: list[ZoneRule], overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None
   ) -> Zone:
       """Overrides take priority."""
       if overrides:
           override = overrides.get(rel_path)
           if override:
               try:
                   return Zone(override)
               except ValueError as exc:
                   log_best_effort_failure(...)
       for rule in rules:
           # ... rule matching
   ```

4. **Stamping issues** (scan.py, lines 97-115):
   - Issues are stamped with the zone (line 111: `issue["zone"] = zone.value`)
   - Confidence is downgraded for certain zones (lines 113-114)

**Impact Points:**
- **Detection filtering**: `should_skip_issue()` and `filter_entries()` use zone_map to skip detectors
- **Scoring**: Issues in excluded zones (test, config, generated, vendor) don't count toward score
- **Potential adjustment**: Non-production file counts subtract from potential scores

### 4. **Why Immediate Application Isn't Currently Possible**

The reason zone overrides can't apply immediately without a scan:
- The zone map is **rebuilt from scratch** during scan generation
- Changing a zone override only updates the config file; the **in-memory state** doesn't reflect it
- The state file contains **snapshotted issues** with zone values already set
- You'd need to either:
  - Re-run detectors to rebuild the issue list (= a scan)
  - Reprocess all issues in state by re-stamping them (risky, could lose data)
  - Provide a lightweight "zone refresh" command that re-stamps issues without re-detecting (not currently implemented)

The design prioritizes **determinism and safety** over convenience—zone changes are validated against actual file discovery during a full scan.

    23→def _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(

## Summary: Strict Score Update & Plan Score Freezing Mechanism

I've traced through the desloppify codebase and identified exactly how strict scores are updated during scans and how `plan_start_scores` controls score display freezing. Here are my findings:

### 1. **Where Strict Score Is Computed/Updated During Scan**

**The flow:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/workflow.py` (line 395-455)
  - `merge_scan_results()` calls `state_mod.merge_scan()` with the new issues
  - This triggers a full score recomputation before state is persisted

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/merge.py` (line 123-200)
  - `merge_scan()` upserts new issues, marks stale dimensions, then calls `_recompute_stats()`
  - This passes `subjective_integrity_target` to ensure anti-gaming penalties apply during score calc

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/__init__.py`
  - `_recompute_stats()` delegates to `desloppify.engine._scoring.state_integration.recompute_stats()`

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_scoring/state_integration.py` (line 277-298)
  - `recompute_stats()` calls `_update_objective_health()` which:
    - Computes a score bundle from issues and subjective assessments
    - Applies anti-gaming (target-match resets) to subjective dimensions
    - Materializes dimension scores and calls `_aggregate_scores()`
  - `_aggregate_scores()` (line 133-148) computes the four canonical scores:
    - `strict_score = compute_health_score(dim_scores, score_key="strict")`
    - This is written directly to `state["strict_score"]`

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/persistence.py` (line 161-199)
  - `save_state()` calls `_recompute_stats()` before writing to disk
  - The state dict now contains the freshly computed `strict_score`

**Key insight:** The strict score is **always recomputed and updated** during every `merge_scan()` call. It's not frozen—only the *display* is frozen via `plan_start_scores`.

### 2. **How `plan_start_scores` Freezes Score Display**

**Seeding (at queue cycle start):**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py` (line 107-126)
  - `_seed_plan_start_scores()` runs after a scan when no `plan_start_scores` exists
  - Captures a snapshot: `plan.plan_start_scores = {"strict": scores.strict, "overall": scores.overall, ...}`
  - Also records `scan_count_at_plan_start` for gate logic

**Display decision point (single source of truth):**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/queue_progress.py` (line 70-88)
  ```python
  def score_display_mode(breakdown, plan_start_strict) -> ScoreDisplayMode:
      if plan_start_strict is None:
          return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
      if breakdown is None:
          return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
      if breakdown.objective_actionable > 0:
          return ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN  # Show frozen plan-start score
      if breakdown.queue_total > 0:
          return ScoreDisplayMode.PHASE_TRANSITION  # Show live + transition banner
      return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
  ```

- **FROZEN mode** is triggered when:
  - `plan_start_scores.strict` is set (queue cycle active)
  - AND `objective_actionable > 0` (there's still objective work in queue)
  - `objective_actionable = queue_total - subjective_count - workflow_count`

**Clearing (at queue drain):**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py` (line 129-153)
  - `_clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty()` runs after a scan
  - Calls `score_display_mode()` to check if queue is drained
  - If queue is DRAINED (and not FROZEN), stashes the frozen scores on state as `_plan_start_scores_for_reveal`
  - Clears `plan.plan_start_scores = {}`

**Where frozen scores are used:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/reporting/summary.py` (line 44-79)
  - `_show_score_reveal()` retrieves `state.pop("_plan_start_scores_for_reveal")` 
  - Shows "SCORE UPDATE" banner comparing plan-start vs final scores
  - Only fires when queue was just cleared

### 3. **The `score_display_mode` Function & `ScoreDisplayMode` Enum**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/queue_progress.py` (line 57-88)

**Three modes:**
- **FROZEN**: `plan_start_strict` is set AND objective work remains → show frozen plan-start score + warning "Score will not update until queue clear"
- **PHASE_TRANSITION**: objective drained but subjective/workflow remain → show live scores + transition banner
- **LIVE**: no active cycle or queue fully clear → show live scores

**Decision logic:**
```python
if plan_start_strict is None:
    return LIVE
if breakdown.objective_actionable > 0:
    return FROZEN
if breakdown.queue_total > 0:
    return PHASE_TRANSITION
return LIVE
```

All five score-reporting call sites use this function:
1. `status` command
2. `scan` reveal (show_score_delta)
3. `next` nudges
4. plan reconcile (clearing check)
5. plan nudge

### 4. **Provisional Manual Override Expiration**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/workflow.py` (line 254-270)

```python
def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(state: StateModel) -> int:
    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    now = utc_now()
    source = "manual_override_expired"
    expired = 0
    for payload in assessments.values():
        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
            continue
        _apply_assessment_reset(payload, source=source, now=now)
        expired += 1
    return expired
```

**Called at:** `prepare_scan_runtime()` (line 283-285)—before any scan work starts

**Effect:** Resets assessments with `provisional_override: True` flag, removing the override and marking source as `"manual_override_expired"`. Counts are reported in scan output (line 142-149 of cmd.py).

---

### 5. **The `_apply_assessment_reset` Function**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/workflow.py` (line 203-219)

```python
def _apply_assessment_reset(payload: dict, *, source: str, now: str) -> None:
    """Apply a standard reset mutation to a single subjective assessment payload.
    
    Sets score to 0.0, stamps assessed_at/reset_by/placeholder, and strips
    any cached scoring artifacts (integrity_penalty, components,
    component_scores) plus provisional-override markers.
    """
    payload["score"] = 0.0
    payload["source"] = source
    payload["assessed_at"] = now
    payload["reset_by"] = source
    payload["placeholder"] = True
    payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
    payload.pop("components", None)
    payload.pop("component_scores", None)
    payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
    payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
```

**What it does:**
1. **Sets score to 0.0** — the dimension becomes unscored
2. **Stamps metadata** — records when reset happened and who triggered it
3. **Marks as placeholder** — signals the dimension needs fresh review
4. **Strips artifacts:**
   - `integrity_penalty` — removes anti-gaming penalties
   - `components` / `component_scores` — cached component-level scores
   - `provisional_override` / `provisional_until_scan` — overrides and their expiry gates

**Used by:**
- `_expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments()` — reset at scan start if `provisional_override: True`
- `_reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset()` — reset with `--reset-subjective` flag

---

### KEY FINDING: Score Changes Are NOT Prevented Mid-Cycle

**Critical insight:** The system does **NOT** prevent score changes during mid-cycle scans. Here's what actually happens:

1. **During objective phase** (queue has objective items):
   - Strict score IS **physically recomputed** on every scan (line 195-198 in merge.py)
   - BUT score **display is frozen** to show plan-start value (queue_progress.py)
   - User sees the frozen value, but the underlying state has the new score

2. **At queue drain** (objective work complete):
   - `_clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty()` detects the transition
   - Stashes old frozen score on state (`_plan_start_scores_for_reveal`)
   - Clears `plan_start_scores`
   - Next display switches to PHASE_TRANSITION mode (shows live score + banner)

3. **Consequence**: If someone manually edits state.json mid-cycle or queries the strict_score field directly, they'll see the live value, not the frozen value. The freeze is **display-only**, not a data-layer lock.

---

### Whether Score Changes Should Be Prevented

**Based on the code architecture**, the current design appears intentional:
- Scores are meant to be live internally (all scoring gates use current scores)
- Display freezing prevents **user confusion** about which score to optimize for
- The `_plan_start_scores_for_reveal` stash enables a "before/after" reveal at cycle end

**However**, if you want to prevent **any** score mutations mid-cycle (not just display), you would need to:
- Add a gate in `merge_scan()` that checks if `plan_start_scores` exists
- Skip `_recompute_stats()` or apply the frozen score instead of recomputing
- This would require changes to `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/merge.py` lines 195-198
